TL;DR: 本文提出MiPO方法，通过少数偏好优化微调扩散模型，提升对数据流形中低密度少数区域的生成覆盖，从而改善扩散分类器的零样本分类性能，解决了扩散分类器在少数类区域识别能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散分类器在数据流形的高密度多数区域表现良好，但在低密度少数区域准确率显著下降。已有少数采样工作聚焦于生成更多少数类图像，但少数采样在感知能力提升方面的根本作用尚未被探索。

Method: 提出MiPO（Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization），使用少数偏好奖励对预训练扩散模型进行微调。仅利用任意文本描述数据，生成候选样本，奖励那些更好覆盖少数区域的样本，通过LoRA和Group Relative Policy Optimization进行优化，无需额外图像数据、外部基础模型或外部奖励模型。

Result: MiPO实现了稳定、提示自适应的少数采样，将低密度生成覆盖转化为改进的零样本扩散分类性能，在五个标准数据集上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 扩散分类器的感知存在朝向多数区域的偏差，这种偏差可以通过少数偏好优化得到缓解。

Abstract: Prior studies have demonstrated that diffusion classifiers achieve robust zero-shot classification performance. However, their effectiveness is strongly tied to the pretraining data distribution: they perform well in majority, high-density regions of the data manifold, but are significantly less accurate in minority, low-density regions. Although prior works on minority sampling have focused on generating more minority-like images, what minority sampling fundamentally enables beyond generation remains underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a direct relationship between minority sampling in generation and the perception capability of diffusion classifiers. Specifically, we show that enhancing minority sampling broadens the coverage of underrepresented regions on the data manifold, thereby improving diffusion-based recognition. To exploit this connection, we propose \textit{Self-Improving Diffusion Classifiers with Minority Preference Optimization} (MiPO), which fine-tunes a pretrained diffusion model using minority preference rewards. Using only arbitrary caption data, MiPO generates candidate samples, rewards those that better cover minority regions, and optimizes the model with LoRA and Group Relative Policy Optimization, without additional image data, external foundation models, or external reward models. This enables stable, prompt-adaptive minority sampling and translates low-density generative coverage into improved zero-shot diffusion classification. To sum up, we show that diffusion classifier perception is biased toward majority regions, demonstrate that this bias can be alleviated through minority preference optimization, and evaluate MiPO on five standard datasets.

</details>


### [152] [City-Level 3D Surface Reconstruction with Viewpoint Orientation Partitioning and Scene Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03771)
*Liang Han,Wenyuan Zhang,Junsheng Zhou,Yu-Shen Liu,Zhizhong Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视角方向场景划分的VOP-GS方法，通过将相似视角的视图联合处理以提升深度估计精度，并设计了缺失区域检测与修复策略，实现了大规模场景的高质量表面重建，在多个数据集上超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于3DGS的大规模重建方法在新视图合成上表现优异，但在复杂几何、长优化时间和有限内存条件下，生成高质量的大场景表面仍然困难。

Method: 提出基于视角方向的场景划分方法，将相似朝向的视图联合处理以获得更准确的深度估计；同时提出检测和修复初始点云中因稀疏视角或纹理不足导致的缺失区域的策略。

Result: 在GauU-Scene、MatrixCity和UrbanScene3D数据集上，该方法在大规模场景表面重建中优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 基于视角方向的场景划分能够实现多GPU并行下的均衡计算与精确表面重建，缺失区域修复进一步提升了几何质量。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D surface reconstruction is a longstanding challenge in computer vision. Although recent large-scale reconstruction methods based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieve impressive novel-view synthesis, producing high-quality surfaces over large scenes remains difficult, due to complex geometry, long optimization, and limited memory. In this paper, we propose a novel yet simple partitioning method to efficiently and faithfully reconstruct large-scale scene surfaces. Our key insight lies in a scene partitioning method based on viewpoint orientation. This partitioning approach ensures that views with similar orientations are jointly involved for more accurate depth estimations, leading to precise surface reconstructions and balanced computation on multiple GPUs in parallel. In addition, we propose a strategy to detect and repair missing regions in the initial point cloud caused by sparse viewpoints or insufficient textures, thereby further improving the geometric quality. Extensive experiments on the GauU-Scene, MatrixCity, and UrbanScene3D datasets demonstrate that our method outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in surface reconstruction for large-scale scenes. Project page: https://hanl2010.github.io/VOP-GS.

</details>


### [153] [Rethinking Depth Pruning for Vision Transformers: A Heterogeneity-Aware Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03784)
*Zhenfeng Su,Kang Zhao,Han Bao,Tao Yuan,Zhongzhe Hu,Xianzhi Yu,Wenxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HetDPT，一种考虑层间异质性的ViT深度剪枝方法，解决了现有深度剪枝忽视不同层差异的问题，可实现高效加速且保持精度，与宽度剪枝结合后在极端剪枝场景下创下新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT剪枝方法主要集中在宽度剪枝，深度剪枝虽能带来更高加速但精度恢复困难，现有联合剪枝方法因忽视不同层之间的异质性导致深度剪枝失效。

Method: 提出HetDPT（Heterogeneity-aware Depth Pruning for Transformers），一种感知层间异质性的深度剪枝方法，避免维度不匹配问题。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K、CIFAR-100、COCO、ADE20K上验证：DeiT-B获得1.58倍加速保持精度，DeiT-S获得1.39倍加速精度几乎无损；与宽度剪枝结合的HetDPT+在Isomorphic-Pruning-2.6G配置下将加速比从4.24倍提升至5.19倍，实现近无损精度。

Conclusion: HetDPT通过感知层异质性有效解决了ViT深度剪枝的精度恢复难题，联合宽度剪枝后在极端剪枝场景达到SOTA。

Abstract: While prior studies have successfully compressed vision Transformers (ViTs) through various pruning techniques, most have concentrated on width pruning to achieve significant reductions in model size. Depth pruning, which removes entire layers from a ViT, is notoriously difficult for accuracy recovery despite its potential to deliver higher speedups, limiting the acceleration achieved by existing joint width-and-depth pruning methods. In this work, we reveal that the failure of existing depth pruning methods lies in their neglect of heterogeneity between different layers, and we introduce HetDPT, a heterogeneity-aware depth pruning method that avoids dimension mismatch. Comprehensive experiments on ImageNet-1K, CIFAR-100, COCO, and ADE20K validate our method: HetDPT achieves a 1.58$\times$ speedup for DeiT-B while maintaining accuracy and a 1.39$\times$ speedup for DeiT-S with nearly no accuracy degradation. Furthermore, when combined with width pruning, HetDPT+ sets a new state-of-the-art record in extreme ViT pruning, enhancing the acceleration ratio from 4.24$\times$ to 5.19$\times$ for the Isomorphic-Pruning-2.6G configuration while maintaining near-lossless accuracy; our code is available at https://github.com/Efficient-AI-for-All/HetDPT.

</details>


### [154] [G$^2$TAM: Geometry Grounded Track Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03789)
*Chenming Zhu,Peizhou Cao,Jingli Lin,Wenbo Hu,Yunlong Ran,Jiangmiao Pang,Tai Wang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出G²TAM，一种利用3D重建模型提供的空间一致性来实现跨视图、跨时间稳定实例跟踪的统一框架，通过将视觉和文本提示映射到共享几何空间，实现了端到端空间重建与实例一致的掩码预测，并在新构建的InsTrack数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频分割模型依赖显式外观记忆库进行实例跟踪，但在大视角变化和长时遮挡下表现脆弱，缺乏几何空间一致性的支撑。

Method: 提出G²TAM框架，利用前馈3D重建模型提供的空间一致性，采用空间对齐的几何表示作为隐式记忆；核心是跨模态空间编码器，将视觉和文本提示集成到共享几何空间，实现端到端空间重建与实例一致的掩码预测。

Result: 在自建的InsTrack数据集上，G²TAM在跨视图一致性、可提示实例空间跟踪、视频目标分割和空间重建等任务上均表现优异。

Conclusion: G²TAM建立了交互式、几何引导的空间推理基础，证明了将几何空间一致性融入实例跟踪的有效性。

Abstract: Human spatial understanding arises from jointly perceiving geometry and semantics, enabling consistent object identification and localization across viewpoints and time. Current video segmentation models depend on explicit object appearance memory banks for instance tracking, yet they remain vulnerable to large viewpoint changes and long-term occlusions. Leveraging the spatial consistency afforded by modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction models, we propose the Geometry Grounded Tracking Anything Model (G$^2$TAM), a unified framework for promptable instance tracking in 3D using only unordered RGB images or videos. G$^2$TAM employs spatially aligned geometric representations as implicit memory, ensuring stable instance identity and localization across frames and views. At its core is a cross-modal spatial encoder that integrates visual and textual prompts into a shared geometric space, enabling end-to-end spatial reconstruction and instance-consistent mask prediction. To support training and evaluation, we construct InsTrack, a large-scale dataset with a dedicated validation split for benchmarking. Extensive experiments show that G$^2$TAM delivers strong cross-view consistency, promptable instance spatial tracking, video object segmentation and spatial reconstruction, establishing a foundation for interactive, geometry-grounded spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [155] [InfraNet: Quality-Aware RGB Guidance for Efficient Infrared Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03795)
*Zichao Feng,Haodong Zhu,Jingying Yang,Sheng Xu,Yangyang Ren,Yuguang Yang,Xuhui Liu,Juan Zhang,Tian Wang,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 InfraNet，一种以红外为中心、具有质量感知能力的多模态目标检测框架。通过非对称架构和 QualGate 模块，在训练时利用 RGB 提供受控监督信号，在推理时支持 RGB-IR 或仅 IR 模式，在四个基准数据集上展现了高精度和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合方法将 RGB 和红外输入同等对待，当 RGB 模态在低光照、恶劣天气等条件下变得不可靠或不可用时，检测鲁棒性严重下降。

Method: 提出 InfraNet 框架，包含非对称架构（主红外通路 + 辅助 RGB 通路）和 QualGate 质量感知融合模块。QualGate 学习任务导向的控制信号，抑制不可靠的 RGB 引导并补偿红外特征。基于该框架设计了两种变体：轻量仅IR架构 InfraNet-IR 和 RGB-IR 架构 InfraNet-RGB-IR。

Result: 在 LLVIP、FLIR-Aligned、M³FD 和 DroneVehicle 四个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在低光照和恶劣天气条件下具有强竞争精度，且仅 IR 推理时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: InfraNet 通过以红外为中心、质量感知的跨模态训练策略，解决了 RGB 模态不可靠时的鲁棒检测问题，同时支持灵活部署（RGB-IR 或仅 IR），兼顾了准确性和效率。

Abstract: Robust object detection under adverse visual conditions remains a long-standing challenge for multi-modal perception systems. Existing fusion-based methods typically require both RGB and infrared (IR) inputs, and treat them equally during both training and inference, which compromises their robustness when the RGB modality becomes unreliable or unavailable. In this case, we propose \textbf{InfraNet}, an IR-centric quality-aware framework that regulates RGB guidance during training and supports flexible RGB--IR or IR-only deployment. InfraNet employs an asymmetric architecture where the primary IR pathway extracts multi-scale infrared features for predictions, while the auxiliary RGB pathway provides reliability-controlled supervisory signals. The core of InfraNet is \textbf{QualGate}, a quality-aware fusion module that learns a task-oriented control signal to suppress unreliable RGB guidance and compensate IR features during cross-modal training. Built upon InfraNet, we design two architectural variants: a lightweight IR-only architecture InfraNet-IR and an RGB--IR architecture InfraNet-RGB-IR. Our method is evaluated through extensive experiments on four benchmark datasets (LLVIP, FLIR-Aligned, M$^3$FD, and DroneVehicle), showing strong or competitive accuracy in challenging low-light and adverse weather conditions. Notably, InfraNet maintains high efficiency in IR-only inference, making it both accurate and computationally efficient.

</details>


### [156] [Probabilistic Robustness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03797)
*Yi Zhang,Siddartha Khastgir,Xingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究医学图像分类中深度学习模型的概率鲁棒性（PR），通过在MedMNIST v2数据集上构建自然损坏场景，系统评估常用模型，提出比对抗鲁棒性（AR）更实用的可信度衡量标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注最坏情况下的对抗鲁棒性（AR），但这种方法在真实医疗应用中代表性不足。为了更实际地评估模型可信度，需要引入概率鲁棒性（PR）作为衡量标准。

Method: 在MedMNIST v2数据集上为医学图像分类构建一组自然损坏场景，系统评估常用的深度学习模型在概率鲁棒性框架下的表现。

Result: 提供了基于统计视角的深度学习模型可信度评估结果，揭示了概率鲁棒性相比对抗鲁棒性在医学图像分类中更具实际意义。

Conclusion: 概率鲁棒性（PR）是比对抗鲁棒性（AR）更实用的模型可信度衡量指标，有助于在医学成像应用中实现更可信的深度学习模型部署。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) has shown strong performance in medical image classification, but its trustworthy deployment remains challenging in safety-critical clinical settings, where prediction errors under perturbations may lead to severe consequences. Existing studies mainly focus on adversarial robustness (AR) from a worst-case perspective; however, such settings may be less representative of real medical applications. In this work, we investigate probabilistic robustness (PR) as a more practical measure of model trustworthiness. To this end, we construct a set of natural corruption settings for medical image classification and systematically evaluate commonly used DL models on MedMNIST v2 dataset. Our study provides a statistically grounded perspective on assessing the trustworthiness of DL models, thereby supporting their more trustworthy deployment in medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [157] [CineMobile: On-Device Image-to-Video Diffusion for Cinematic Camera Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03803)
*Xuyao Huang,Zelai Deng,Xu Wang,Xizhong Xiao,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineMobile，通过三步优化策略（蒸馏引导剪枝、扩散蒸馏+强化学习、混合后训练量化）将DiT视频生成模型压缩至1GB以下，在移动端实现40倍加速，480p视频生成延迟达20秒（MTK平台）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动设备上图像转视频的需求日益增长（如子弹时间、慢动作等电影级效果），但Diffusion Transformers参数量大且多步迭代去噪导致计算开销过高，难以在移动设备上高效运行。

Method: 三步优化策略：(1) 蒸馏引导剪枝，获得紧凑模型；(2) 结合扩散蒸馏和强化学习，压缩为4步生成器；(3) 混合后训练量化，将模型压缩至1GB以下。

Result: 相比教师模型(原Wan 2.1架构)，生成速度提升40倍，视觉质量相当。49帧480p视频在H200 GPU上每步去噪延迟0.6s，MTK Dimensity 8400平台上20s，峰值内存1.8GB。

Conclusion: CineMobile在移动端实现了实用的图像转视频生成能力，大幅降低计算开销的同时保持了生成质量，验证了在移动平台上部署高性能视频生成模型的可行性。

Abstract: The growing demand for image-to-video creation on mobile devices has increasingly focused on cinematic motion effects like bullet time, dolly zoom, slow motion, etc. While Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) exhibit strong performance in video generation, their large parameter sizes and multi-step iterative denoising processes lead to substantial computational overhead, making efficient generation on mobile devices challenging. We propose CineMobile to bridge the gap. In particular, CineMobile adopts a three-fold optimization strategy: (1) leveraging a distillation-guided pruning approach to derive a compact yet efficient model that retains the essential video generation capabilities required for cinematic effects; (2) optimizing the compressed model into a 4-step generator via a combination of diffusion distillation and reinforcement learning; (3) employing a hybrid post-training quantization strategy to compress the model footprint to under 1 GB. Experimental results show that compared to the teacher model with the Wan 2.1 architecture, CineMobile achieves a 40x speedup in generation while maintaining comparable visual quality. Specifically, CineMobile generates 49-frame 480p videos with a per-step denoising latency of 0.6s on an NVIDIA H200 GPU and 20s on the MediaTek Dimensity 8400 Ultimate 5G platform, with a peak memory usage of 1.8 GB, demonstrating its practical applicability for mobile-based image-to-video creation.

</details>


### [158] [TestMate: Test-Time Domain Adaptation Aided by Lightweight Vision Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03810)
*Dimitrios Fotiou,Vasileios Mygdalis,Ioannis Pitas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TestMate是一种无需反向传播的实时测试时域适应框架，利用轻量级视觉基础模型生成多尺度掩码提案，通过启发式竞争机制融合分割结果，在不依赖梯度更新和记忆库的情况下实现从第一帧开始的即时适应，避免灾难性遗忘并保留细粒度边界细节，在语义分割的TTDA、SFDA和在线TTDA任务上达到SOTA。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTDA方法存在两大局限：熵最小化方法需要昂贵的反向传播，易导致灾难性遗忘并产生噪声分割边界；基于记忆库的方法虽无需反向传播，但适应速度慢、需要大量样本收敛且难以应对连续域偏移。亟待一种实时、无需反向传播且能快速适应的TTDA方法。

Method: 提出TestMate框架，核心思路是借助轻量级视觉基础模型（VFM）的泛化能力引导主模型适应。具体使用基于YOLOv8-seg的零样本实例分割模型在实时条件下生成多尺度未标记掩码提案，然后通过启发式的、按尺寸排序的竞争方案融合这些提案：小尺寸、高置信度区域占据主导地位，并逐步细化其周围较大且不确定区域的预测。该机制无需可学习参数，从第一帧即开始适应，天然避免灾难性遗忘，并保留精细目标细节和边界。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，TestMate在TTDA、Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA)和在线TTDA三种不同的适应任务中均达到最先进水平。可作为独立的轻量精炼模块使用，也能无缝集成到现有TTDA方法中显著提升其性能。

Conclusion: TestMate通过引入视觉基础模型作为引导，以完全无参数、无需反向传播的方式解决了TTDA中长期存在的适应速度与遗忘问题之间的权衡，验证了利用预训练基础模型的先验知识来指导在线域适应的可行性，为实时域适应提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Test-Time Domain Adaptation (TTDA) aims to adapt Deep Neural Networks to distribution shifts using only streaming, unlabeled test data in real time. Current methods for semantic segmentation tasks suffer from critical limitations. Entropy minimization techniques require costly backpropagation, risking catastrophic forgetting and producing noisy segmentation boundaries. Memory-bank methods, while backpropagation-free, exhibit slow adaptation, requiring numerous samples to converge and struggle to handle continuous domain shifts. We introduce TestMate, a novel, real-time, and backpropagation-free TTDA framework that overcomes these issues. TestMate leverages generalization capability of a lightweight Visual Foundation Model to guide the adaptation. We use a zero-shot instance segmentation YOLOv8-seg based model to generate unlabeled mask proposals for objects and their parts at multiple scales in real time. These proposals are fused with the primary model via a heuristic, size-ordered competitive scheme, where small, high-confidence regions dominate and refine predictions in surrounding larger, less certain areas. This paremeter-free mechanism enables immediate adaptation from the first frame, inherently avoids catastrophic forgetting and effectively preserves fine object details and boundaries, even for small objects. TestMate can be used as a standalone, efficient refinement module or seamlessly integrated into existing TTDA methods to significantly boost their performance. We demonstrate state-of-the-art results across two benchmark datasets, proving TestMate's effectiveness in three distinct adaptation tasks: TTDA, Source-Free Domain Adaptation (SFDA), and online-TTDA. Code is available.

</details>


### [159] [Global Logic and Local Search: Dual-Stream Multimodal In-Context Learning for Verifiable Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03817)
*Runzhi Deng,Yundi Hu,Yiming Zhong,Zhao Wang,Xixi Liu,Hongsong Wang,Caifeng Shan,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GLLS是一个无需训练的框架，通过全局逻辑流（SAM提取视觉事实）和局部搜索流（MCTS选择证据区域）相结合，在工业异常检测中实现了参考引导的多模态上下文验证，无需缺陷样本即可提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM在工业异常检测中面临缺陷小、分辨率有限、文本标准与视觉证据不对齐等问题；基于优化的方法需要大量缺陷样本，在早期部署阶段不可用。

Method: 提出GLLS框架：1) 构建Part-Aware Visual-Logical Atlas组织正常参考和结构化规范；2) 全局逻辑流使用SAM 3提取可部分检查的视觉事实；3) 细粒度动作流使用MCTS在固定预算下选择局部证据区域。

Result: 在MMAD-QA及其他异常检测数据集上，GLLS相较于匹配基线和通用基线均取得一致提升，且诊断决策可追溯到明确的视觉证据。

Conclusion: GLLS是一种无需训练、不依赖缺陷样本的参考引导多模态上下文验证框架，通过全局-局部协同搜索实现可溯源的工业异常检测。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) show strong few-shot generalization, but industrial anomaly detection remains difficult because defects are small, input resolution is limited, and textual standards are not always grounded in visual evidence. Recent optimization-based methods improve alignment through fine-tuning, but they often require many defective samples, which are unavailable in early deployment. We present Global Logic and Local Search (GLLS), a training-free framework for reference-guided multimodal in-context verification. GLLS uses a Part-Aware Visual-Logical Atlas to organize normal references and structured specifications in the inference context. It combines a Global & Logic Stream, where SAM 3 extracts partially checkable visual facts, with a Fine-Grained & Actions Stream, where MCTS selects local evidence crops under a fixed budget. Experiments on MMAD-QA and additional anomaly detection datasets show consistent gains over matched and general-purpose baselines, while keeping the final diagnostic decision traceable to explicit visual evidence throughout the inspection trace.

</details>


### [160] [TRISTAR: Triple-Signal Stair Recognition and Vision-Only Indoor Navigation for Search-and-Rescue Micro-UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03818)
*Octavian Gîngu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单目视觉的低成本无人机自主室内导航框架，在DJI Tello平台上实现。该框架结合Depth Anything V2单目深度估计、经典计算机视觉和轻量级深度学习模型，实现走廊探索、自动门检测、房间识别、受害者检测以及基于TRISTAR三重传感器融合的自主楼梯攀爬。实验表明深度误差降至10%以下，门检测精度0.93/ F1分数0.91，证明了无需专用测距硬件即可在资源受限平台上实现可靠的室内探索和楼梯穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内搜救需要快速态势感知，但GNSS信号不可用、人类难以进入。现有方案依赖LiDAR、立体视觉或专用深度相机，增加了硬件复杂性和部署成本。本文旨在为低成本无人机提供纯单目视觉的自主导航方案，降低硬件门槛。

Method: 基于DJI Tello平台，使用Depth Anything V2进行单目深度估计；结合经典计算机视觉与轻量级深度学习实现场景理解、受害者检测和危险识别。框架包含两个独立行为：①走廊探索（自动门检测→房间进入→OCR房间识别→受害者检查）；②自主楼梯攀爬（TRISTAR方法：融合Sobel滤波结构线索、多尺度Gabor滤波纹理分析和单目深度几何信息的三重传感器融合）。

Result: 深度校准将相对深度误差从27.4%降至10%以下；门检测精度0.93，F1分数0.91；消融实验表明多传感器融合显著提升楼梯识别鲁棒性；故障案例分析揭示了单目感知在光照和反射表面条件下的局限性。

Conclusion: 在无需专用测距硬件的资源受限平台上，可以实现可靠的室内探索和楼梯穿越，为快速搜救部署提供了一种实用且低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Indoor search-and-rescue (SAR) operations often require rapid situational awareness where GNSS signals are unavailable and human access is difficult or hazardous. While most autonomous aerial systems rely on LiDAR, stereo vision, or specialized depth cameras, such solutions increase both hardware complexity and deployment costs. This paper presents a complete autonomous indoor navigation framework for low-cost unmanned aerial vehicles based exclusively on monocular vision. Implemented on a DJI Tello platform, the system combines monocular depth estimation using Depth Anything V2 with classical computer vision and lightweight deep learning models for scene understanding, victim detection, and hazard recognition. The framework consists of two independent behaviors: (i) corridor exploration with automatic door detection, room entry, OCR-based room identification, and victim inspection; and (ii) autonomous stair ascent based on TRISTAR (TRI-Signal STair Ascent Recognition), a novel triple-sensor fusion method that integrates structural cues (Sobel filtering), texture analysis (multi-scale Gabor filtering), and geometric depth from monocular depth estimation. Evaluation used real indoor flights in a university building. Depth calibration reduced relative depth error from 27.4% to below 10%, while the door detection algorithm reached a precision of 0.93 and an F1-score of 0.91. A dedicated ablation study shows that multi-sensor fusion significantly improves stair-recognition robustness compared to individual sensing modalities, and a failure-case analysis delineates the limits of monocular perception under challenging lighting and reflective surfaces. The results demonstrate that reliable indoor exploration and stair traversal are achievable on resource-constrained platforms without specialized ranging hardware, a practical, cost-effective solution for rapid SAR deployment.

</details>


### [161] [FDR-Occ: Factorized Dense Routing for Full-Spectrum 3D Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03822)
*Dubing Chen,Huan Zheng,Tianyi Yan,Yucheng Zhou,Runzhou Tao,Zhongying Qiu,Jianfei Yang,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出分解式密集路由（FDR）框架，通过层次化张量收缩近似密集2D到3D投影，打破传统物理投影的局部性瓶颈，并引入分辨率-上下文解耦架构，在Occ3D-nuScenes和Occ3D-Waymo基准上达到最先进性能，且在无标定环境下展现出更强的结构鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的3D占用预测依赖显式物理投影，将路由矩阵限制在稀疏相机射线上，造成严重的局部性瓶颈（Locality Bottleneck），阻碍网络构建全局上下文理解，且在外参不可靠或缺失时性能急剧下降。

Method: 1) 将视图变换抽象为无约束二分路由，提出分解式密集路由（FDR），通过层次化张量收缩近似密集2D到3D混合，实现全全局感受野且复杂度为次二次；2) 识别密集路由中空间收缩带来的分辨率-上下文权衡（Resolution-Context Trade-off），提出分辨率-上下文解耦架构，将3D空间分解为全局宏观拓扑锚点（通过FDR）和精确局部几何平面（通过显式投影）。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes和Occ3D-Waymo基准上取得最先进性能；在无标定设置（隐藏物理外参）下，全局路由内化了多相机系统的隐式拓扑，相比物理投影基线表现出显著更强的结构鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 打破物理投影的局部性瓶颈、实现全局密集路由是提升3D占用预测性能的关键；分辨率-上下文解耦架构有效解决了密集路由中的固有权衡，为无标定或外参不稳定的场景提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D occupancy prediction fundamentally relies on the 2D-to-3D view transformation. Current paradigms predominantly utilize explicit physical projection, which artificially restricts the routing matrix to strict, sparse camera rays. While computationally efficient, this imposes a severe Locality Bottleneck, preventing the network from constructing holistic contextual understanding and degrading sharply when camera extrinsics are unreliable or absent. To break this bottleneck, we abstract view transformation as unconstrained bipartite routing and propose Factorized Dense Routing (FDR). By approximating dense 2D-to-3D mixing through hierarchical tensor contractions, FDR guarantees a fully-global receptive field with tractable, sub-quadratic complexity. Crucially, the mandatory spatial contraction in dense routing exposes a fundamental Resolution-Context Trade-off. To address this, we introduce a Resolution-Context Decoupled Architecture. We factorize the 3D space into a global macroscopic topological anchor (via FDR) and precise local geometric planes (via explicit projection). This decoupling enables global semantic inference and exact surface localization to complement each other without mutual compromise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Occ3D-nuScenes and Occ3D-Waymo benchmarks. More notably, in an uncalibrated setting where physical extrinsics are withheld, our global routing internalizes the implicit multi-camera rig topology and exhibits substantially stronger structural robustness than physical-projection baselines under the same protocol.

</details>


### [162] [Q-TriM: Question-Guided Tri-Modal Attention for Audio-Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03825)
*SungHun Kim,SeungJun Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对AVQA中深层顺序注意力融合导致信息丢失和误差累积的问题，提出Q-TriM，采用浅层并行的三模态注意力机制（查询、键、值分别来自不同模态），在单阶段并行融合多模态信息，在三个基准上达到SOTA，尤其在MUSIC-AVQA-R上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AVQA系统使用深层堆叠的自注意力和交叉注意力层进行多模态融合，这种顺序堆叠方式会导致跨层间细微的模态间线索丢失，且误差在顺序注意力层中不断累积，影响最终性能。

Method: 提出Q-TriM框架，采用浅层并行的多模态融合方式替代深层顺序融合。核心创新是Tri-Modal Attention机制，其中查询、键、值分别来自文本、视频、音频三个不同模态，在单阶段并行组合这些注意力表示，避免深度堆叠带来的问题。

Result: 在三个AVQA基准数据集上达到最先进性能，尤其在MUSIC-AVQA-R数据集上取得了显著提升，证明了模型鲁棒性和分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 浅层并行的三模态注意力融合可以有效避免深层顺序融合带来的信息丢失和误差累积问题，是多模态问答领域一种有效的框架设计思路。

Abstract: Audio-Visual Question Answering (AVQA) extends classical VQA by requiring joint reasoning over video and synchronized audio. However, many AVQA systems rely on deeply stacked layers of self- and cross attention across text, video, and audio. Such sequential stacking may incur loss of information such as subtle inter-modal cues over the layers, causing errors to accumulate across sequential attention layers during the fusion. We introduce Q-TriM which performs multi-modal fusion in a shallow and parallel manner instead of a deep and sequential manner. For Q-TriM, we propose a novel framework for attention operation incorporating video and audio conditioned on text. As a result, we obtain not only standard cross attention outputs but also Tri-Modal Attention representations in which Query, Key, and Value come from distinct modalities. These attention representations are combined in parallel at a single stage, thus avoiding the multi-modal fusion with deep stacks in order to mitigate error accumulation and depth-induced issues. Q-TriM achieves state-of-the-art performance on three AVQA benchmarks, including substantial gains on MUSIC-AVQA-R, which demonstrates its robustness and out-of-distribution generalization. Code is available at https://github.com/Sunghun95/Q-TriM

</details>


### [163] [How Do Diffusion Classifiers Decide? A Bias-Centric Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03831)
*Saba Fathi,Fardin Ayar,Maryam Abdolali,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada,Mahdi Javanmardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ASOB-Bench，从属性绑定、尺寸-顺序偏差、背景依赖三个维度系统评估扩散分类器的偏见，发现其与视觉语言模型存在不同的偏见特征，并通过重建误差热图和交叉注意力可视化揭示了背后的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散分类器被越来越多地用于零样本分类任务，但其决策机制尚不明确。已有研究对判别式视觉语言模型的偏见有深入探讨，但对扩散分类器的偏见评估仍属空白。

Method: 在现有框架基础上扩展五个新的属性类别，构建新数据集；从属性绑定、尺寸-顺序偏差、背景依赖性三个维度评估扩散分类器；使用重建误差热图和U-Net交叉注意力可视化分析偏见机制。

Result: 与OpenCLIP基线相比，扩散分类器在属性误绑定方面表现更好；在ComCo基准上对尺寸-顺序捷径更敏感；在ImageNet-B上准确率下降更明显，表明更依赖背景而非前景线索。

Conclusion: 扩散分类器展现出与视觉语言模型不同的独特偏见特征，这些发现为构建更鲁棒的扩散模型提供了指导，且其单次诊断结果也能反映文本到图像生成模型中的类似失败模式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently been repurposed for zero-shot classification, giving rise to diffusion classifiers that identify the best-matching text prompt by minimizing the noise-prediction error. Despite their growing adoption, how these models make classification decisions remains poorly understood. We introduce ASOB-Bench, a bias evaluation for diffusion classifiers along three dimensions: Attribute binding, Size-Order bias, and Background dependency. These dimensions serve not as an exhaustive taxonomy but as targeted probes of how the text-conditioned reconstruction-error score reaches a decision. Such a perspective is well studied for discriminative vision-language models, yet remains overlooked for diffusion classifiers. Extending an existing framework with five new attribute categories on newly constructed datasets, we find diffusion classifiers are less prone to attribute misbinding than an OpenCLIP baseline; on the established ComCo benchmark they are substantially more susceptible to size-order shortcuts; and on ImageNet-B they suffer far larger accuracy drops, revealing heavy reliance on background over foreground cues. Reconstruction-error heatmaps and U-Net cross-attention visualizations expose the mechanism behind each bias. Because diffusion classifiers share the same denoiser as text-to-image models, these single-pass diagnostics also point toward analogous failure modes in generation. Overall, diffusion classifiers exhibit a distinct bias profile from vision-language models, offering guidance for building more robust diffusion-based models.

</details>


### [164] [When Simpler Is Better: Evaluating Translation Pipelines for Medieval Latin Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03836)
*Nguyen Kim Hai Bui,Md. Easin Arafat,Tamás Gábor Orosz,Mufti Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了中世纪拉丁文手稿的图像到翻译完整流程，发现专用OCR模型比通用VLM在字符错误率上降低最多4.3倍，且最简单的流水线（专用OCR + VLM）优于多组件变体，RAG和后OCR校正会导致提示饱和与错误传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译虽取得显著进展，但视觉语言模型在处理历史手稿（低资源音译、古词汇、噪声输入）时表现不佳，现有清洁文本基准无法暴露真实失败模式。

Method: 构建了CATMuS拉丁数据集进行基准测试，并引入了首个中世纪拉丁文平行语料库Interpres-Parallel-Corpus (IPC)，包含1,383条对齐的手稿图像行、转录文本和专家翻译。对比了专用OCR模型与通用VLM，以及不同流水线变体（含RAG、后OCR校正等）。

Result: 专用OCR模型在字符错误率上比通用VLM低最多4.3倍，且参数量少数个数量级。最简单的流水线（专用OCR→VLM）优于多组件变体，RAG和后OCR校正反而引入提示饱和与错误传播，降低翻译质量。

Conclusion: 发现了"复杂度悖论"，为低资源历史场景下的翻译系统部署提供了新的基准和实践指导。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in machine translation, Vision Language Models (VLMs) struggle on historical manuscripts, a domain that stresses core Natural Language Processing (NLP) capabilities: low-resource transliteration, archaic vocabulary, and noisy input signals. We present a systematic framework for evaluating the full image-to-translation pipeline on medieval Latin manuscripts, a setting in which scribal shorthand, ligatures, and parchment degradation expose failure modes that are invisible in clean-text benchmarks. Benchmarking on the CATMuS Latin dataset reveals a specialization gap: domain-specific Optical Character Recognition (OCR) models reduce character error rate by up to 4.3$\times$ compared to general-purpose VLMs, despite operating at orders of magnitude fewer parameters. We introduce the Interpres-Parallel-Corpus (IPC), a novel dataset comprising 1,383 aligned manuscript image lines, transcriptions, and expert translations, the first of its kind for medieval Latin. Our experiments uncover a complexity paradox: the simplest pipeline, a specialized OCR model feeding directly into a VLM, outperforms all multi-component variants. Adding retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) or post-OCR correction introduces prompt saturation and error propagation that degrade aggregate translation quality. These findings offer both a new benchmark and practical guidance for deploying translation systems in low-resource historical settings.

</details>


### [165] [ContiStain: Cross-Domain Relation-Preserving Distillation for Continual Multi-Domain Virtual IHC Staining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03851)
*Fuqiang Chen,Yifeng Wang,Hongpeng Wang,Yongbing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ContiStain，一种面向连续虚拟免疫组化（IHC）染色任务的多领域关系蒸馏框架，通过混合专家特征提取器和跨域token级余弦相似度矩阵的一致性蒸馏，解决了顺序微调导致的多生物标志物表示灾难性遗忘问题，在MIST数据集上显著降低了FID和ConchFID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多路复用虚拟染色模型能实现从H&E切片进行可扩展、非破坏性的多标志物分析，但临床实践中新生物标志物数据通常是按时间顺序依次获取的。直接对时序到达的新数据进行微调会严重破坏先前学习的生物标志物表征，因为顺序优化会扰乱隐空间中不同标志物表征之间的结构化关系。

Method: 提出ContiStain框架，包含两个核心模块：(i) 构建域感知的结构化特征空间，采用混合专家（MoE）特征提取器减少不同生物标志物域之间的表征干扰；(ii) 提出关系保持蒸馏策略，在连续适应过程中显式强制保持已学习生物标志物域之间的跨域token级余弦相似度矩阵的一致性。

Result: 在MIST数据集上，采用四域顺序虚拟IHC染色设置进行实验，与顺序微调相比，FID降低11.1，ConchFID降低60.9，展示了更好的稳定性，实现了可扩展且鲁棒的多域虚拟染色。

Conclusion: ContiStain通过保持跨域结构一致性，在适应新域的同时有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，为临床时序数据场景下的多生物标志物虚拟染色提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A unified multiplex virtual staining model enables scalable and non-destructive multiplex analysis from H&E slides while promoting parameter efficiency, shared pathological knowledge, and consistent cross-biomarker representations. However, in clinical practice, data for new biomarkers are typically acquired sequentially over time. Fine-tuning on such temporally arriving data leads to severe performance degradation on previously learned biomarkers, as sequential optimization disrupts the structured relationships among biomarker representations in the latent space. To address this issue, we propose ContiStain, an IHC multi-domain relational distillation framework for continual virtual staining. We first (i) construct a domain-aware structured feature space using a mixture-of-experts (MoE) feature extractor to reduce representation interference across biomarker domains. Based on this stabilized feature space, we then (ii) propose a relation-preserving distillation strategy that explicitly enforces the consistency of cross-domain token-level cosine similarity matrices between learned biomarker domains during continual adaptation. By maintaining cross-domain structural coherence, ContiStain mitigates forgetting while retaining adaptability to new domains. Experiments on the MIST dataset under a four-domain sequential virtual IHC staining setting show improved stability, reducing FID and ConchFID by 11.1 and 60.9 compared to sequential fine-tuning, enabling scalable and robust multi-domain virtual staining. Code is released at https://github.com/ccitachi/ContiStain.

</details>


### [166] [CogRad: A Cognitively-Inspired Multi-Agent Framework for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03853)
*Saif Ur Rehman Khan,Hasaan Maqsood,Sebastian Vollmer,Andreas Dengel,Muhammad Nabeel Asim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogRad是一个受放射科医生阅读过程启发的多智能体框架，包含Scout（发现）、Investigator（聚焦）、Writer（生成）和Verifier（验证）四个智能体，通过区域注意力、疾病门控视觉前缀和视觉蕴含损失实现可验证的放射学报告生成，在CheXpert Plus和IU X-Ray数据集上取得了领先的NLG和临床指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成系统通常采用单次前向传播方式生成完整报告，缺乏对图像中信息的二次检查机制，无法在生成后验证报告中的陈述是否与图像一致，导致报告可能包含幻觉或错误。作者希望模拟放射科医生的认知阅读过程来解决这一问题。

Method: CogRad包含四个智能体：(1) Scout智能体通过slot注意力直接从图像块中发现解剖区域，分配区域和疾病级分流评分；(2) Investigator智能体将表征能力集中在Scout标记的可疑区域；(3) Writer智能体将信号编译为疾病门控视觉前缀输入给大语言模型；(4) Verifier智能体在训练时使用视觉蕴含损失监督，在推理时逐句检查并重新生成不充分基于图像的句子。

Result: 在CheXpert Plus上：BLEU-4=0.316, CIDEr=0.322，为对比方法中最优；在IU X-Ray上：BLEU-4=0.201, CIDEr=0.724，在所有NLG指标上领先所有基线。额外使用RadGraph F1、CheXbert F1和幻觉分析评估临床准确性，并进行了消融实验和Grad-CAM可视化分析。

Conclusion: 模拟放射科医生认知过程的多智能体框架能有效提升报告生成的准确性和可验证性，区域级注意力聚焦和逐句验证机制是减少幻觉和提升临床精度的关键要素。

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation (RRG) can ease radiologist workload, yet most existing systems produce a report in a single forward pass, with no mechanism to check a claim against the image or revisit a finding once stated. We present CogRad, a cognitively inspired multi-agent framework that structures generation around four stages of a radiologist's reading process. A Scout agent discovers anatomical regions directly from image patches via slot attention and assigns region and disease-level triage scores; an Investigator agent concentrates representational capacity on the regions Scout flags as suspicious; a Writer agent compiles these signals into a disease gated visual prefix for a large language model; and a Verifier agent supervises training with a visual entailment loss and, at inference, re-examines its own draft sentence by sentence, regenerating any report it judges insufficiently grounded. On CheXpert Plus, CogRad attains a BLEU-4 of 0.316 and a CIDEr of 0.322, the best scores among the methods we compare against. On IU X-Ray, it attains a BLEU-4 of 0.201 and a CIDEr of 0.724, leading every baseline on every standard NLG metric. We further evaluate CogRad with RadGraph F1, CheXbert F1, and a hallucination analysis to assess clinical accuracy beyond standard text-overlap metrics, complemented by ablation studies and Grad-CAM-based visualizations that characterize each agent's contribution and the model's visual grounding.

</details>


### [167] [PRISM3D: Probabilistic Refinement and Robust Initialization for Physically Consistent Scene Modeling under Extreme Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03855)
*Gopi Raju Matta,Reddypalli Trisha,Vemunuri Divya Madhuri,Kaushik Mitra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PRISM3D框架，实现从极端运动模糊图像中进行盲3D场景重建，通过鲁棒初始化、概率几何致密化和物理图像建模解决传统方法失效的问题，并扩展了多模态版本PRISM3D-E引入事件流辅助重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SfM管线在极端运动模糊图像上失效，现有方法依赖不切实际的清晰图像监督，亟需一种能从严重退化输入中直接进行鲁棒3D重建的方法。

Method: 1) 利用深度密集跟踪方法(VGGSfM)实现鲁棒初始化，恢复全局拓扑；2) 采用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛(MCMC)进行概率几何致密化；3) 通过连续贝塞尔轨迹建模物理图像形成过程；4) 多模态扩展PRISM3D-E引入事件流作为结构先验；5) 贡献PRISM3D-E Benchmark数据集。

Result: 在RGB单模态和多模态扩展上均达到新的最先进性能。

Conclusion: PRISM3D及其多模态扩展为极端运动模糊场景下的3D重建提供了鲁棒且有效的统一解决方案，通过概率与物理耦合的策略克服了传统方法的局限性。

Abstract: We address the inverse problem of blind 3D scene reconstruction from extremely motion-blurred images, a scenario where traditional Structure-from-Motion (SfM) pipelines fail. Existing approaches typically circumvent this bottleneck by relying on impractical sharp-image supervision. In this work, we introduce PRISM3D, a unified framework enabling robust reconstruction directly from severely degraded inputs. To overcome the lack of a reliable starting point, we propose a Robust Initialization strategy utilizing deep dense tracking method (VGGSfM) to recover global topology where feature matching fails. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to effectively leverage this paradigm to bootstrap 3D Gaussian Splatting from extreme motion blur. However, while robust, this initialization yields sparse and noisy geometry that causes deterministic optimization to diverge. To resolve this, we propose a coupled solution driven by probability and physics: we adopt a probabilistic formulation for geometric densification via Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) to robustly populate the sparse priors, while simultaneously modeling physical image formation via continuous Bezier Trajectories. Furthermore, while PRISM3D establishes a highly robust standalone pipeline, the availability of complementary event streams offers an opportunity to push the reconstruction fidelity further. To exploit this, we introduce PRISM3D-E, a multi-modal (RGB + Events) extension that seamlessly integrates high-temporal-resolution events as structural priors to maximize geometric recovery. Because existing datasets lack paired event streams under such severe degradation, we concurrently contribute the PRISM3D-E Benchmark to facilitate rigorous evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that both our standalone RGB framework and its multi-modal extension establish new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [168] [Ghosts Beneath Textures: Texture-Relation Cues for Cross-Paradigm AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03862)
*Haoyu Wang,Yiming Qin,Zhongjie Ba,Ziping Dong,Jishen Zeng,Peng Cheng,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对跨范式（图像无条件生成 vs. 图像条件生成）的AI生成图像检测问题，构建了ConImageGen基准，发现现有检测器无法泛化。通过抑制语义干扰首次可视化出跨范式的语义无关纹理模式，并基于此提出DTS-Det框架，通过建模结构化局部-全局纹理关系实现通用检测，在多个评估设置上达到SOTA，包括ConImageGen上99.6% ACC（领先最佳基线10.5%），跨数据集93.2%/94.1% ACC，以及鲁棒性测试中95.2%/88.1%的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器大多面向图像无条件生成范式（如噪声/文本引导），但图像条件生成应用日益广泛，两种范式之间的跨范式检测问题被长期忽视，现有方法无法可靠泛化。

Method: 首先构建跨范式基准ConImageGen；然后通过抑制语义干扰，首次可视化出跨范式一致的语义无关纹理模式，揭示其具有结构化局部-全局纹理关系；基于此发现提出DTS-Det框架，建模纹理关系而非直接利用显式伪影，实现跨范式通用检测。

Result: DTS-Det在ConImageGen上达99.6% ACC（领先最佳基线10.5%），跨数据集评估PicoBanana/RAID上达93.2%/94.1% ACC，在重建攻击和黑盒对抗攻击下分别维持95.2%/88.1%的检测率。

Conclusion: 跨范式AI生成图像检测是一个关键但被忽视的问题；语义无关纹理模式的结构化局部-全局关系是可泛化的取证线索；DTS-Det通过建模这种纹理关系而非显式伪影，实现了跨范式、跨数据集的先进检测性能与强鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated images have proliferated rapidly, motivating extensive research. Most existing AI-generated image detectors are developed and evaluated under image-free generation paradigms, such as noise-based or text-guided generation. However, image-conditioned generation has become increasingly important in practical applications, as it enables more fine-grained control over generated content. Detecting AI-generated images across these two paradigms creates a critical cross-paradigm detection problem that has long been overlooked. To study this problem, we construct ConImageGen, a benchmark for cross-paradigm AI-generated image detection. Evaluations on ConImageGen show that existing detectors fail to generalize reliably across image-free and image-conditioned generation. To address this failure, this paper identifies a cross-paradigm forensic cue and provides a new perspective for generalized AI-generated image detection. Specifically, by suppressing semantic interference, we visualize, for the first time, semantics-irrelevant texture patterns across generation paradigms. These patterns exhibit structured local-global texture relations, indicating a generalizable form of forensic evidence. Motivated by this finding, we shift the focus from directly exploiting explicit artifacts to modeling texture relations and propose DTS-Det, a detection framework that captures and leverages such relations for generalized AI-generated image detection. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our method. DTS-Det achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse evaluation settings, reaching 99.6% ACC on ConImageGen with a 10.5% gain over the best baseline. It also achieves 93.2%/94.1% ACC in cross-dataset evaluation on PicoBanana/RAID and maintains detection rates of 95.2%/88.1% under reconstruction attacks and black-box adversarial attacks, respectively.

</details>


### [169] [GeoSelect: Spatial-Program Execution for Training-Free Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03869)
*Yuhang Jiang,Guohui Deng,Miaozhong Xu,Chao Ruan,Jinling Zhao,Linsheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoSelect是一种无需训练的遥感图像指代分割方法，通过将自然语言表达式编译为类型化空间程序并显式执行，在RRSIS-D和RISBench上分别达到58.86和55.27 mIoU，远超此前最好的无训练方法（两倍以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练方法通过隐式视觉-语言激活或区域-文本相似度进行指代分割，无法有效处理遥感指代中常见的空间、比较和顺序关系（如"最大的船"或"左边第二个球场"），缺乏对这些结构化表达的精确控制能力。

Method: 提出GeoSelect管线：1) 冻结的纯文本语言模型将表达式编译为小型领域特定语言（DSL）程序；2) 格式检查器验证程序合法性；3) 确定性执行器运行程序。核心抽象是单一打分候选集类型，其中连续几何场实现位置和邻近度的逐像素密集映射，离散的集合和排序算子处理极值、序数、计数并集和关系构造。程序执行失败时可降级为纯场特例，保证每个表达式都能返回答案。

Result: RRSIS-D测试集上达到58.86 mIoU，RISBench测试集上达到55.27 mIoU，在RRSIS-D上超过此前最佳无训练方法两倍以上，无需任何指代监督且仅需单GPU。消融实验证明性能增益来自显式执行而非骨干网络，Oracle分解定位残差来自RRSIS-D的检测召回和RISBench的选择问题，暴露审计确认了对预训练泄漏的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GeoSelect通过将指代分割重构为空间程序执行，显著提升了无训练方法在遥感指代分割上的表现，具有可解释、可检查、鲁棒性强的特点，为无需标注的遥感指代理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Referring remote sensing image segmentation isolates the object named by a natural-language expression in an aerial image. Existing training-free methods resolve the expression through implicit vision-language activations or region-text similarity, which gives weak control over the spatial, comparative, and ordinal relations that dominate aerial referring: they cannot represent constructions such as the largest ship or the second court from the left. We propose GeoSelect, a training-free pipeline that reframes referring as the execution of a typed spatial program. A frozen, text-only language model synthesises the expression into a small domain-specific language, a well-formedness checker accepts the program, and a deterministic executor runs it. The central abstraction is a single scored candidate set type under which every operator composes: continuous geometric fields realise position and proximity as dense pixel-level maps, while discrete set and order operators add the extremum, ordinal, counted-union, and relational constructions that fields alone cannot express. Because execution is explicit, every intermediate program, field, and ranking is inspectable, and a reliability ladder degrades any failing program to a field-only special case, so every expression returns an answer. GeoSelect attains 58.86 mIoU on RRSIS-D test and 55.27 mIoU on RISBench test, more than twice the best prior training-free method on RRSIS-D, with no referring supervision and on a single GPU. A controlled comparison with candidates and segmenter fixed attributes the gain to explicit execution, not the backbone; an oracle decomposition localises the residual gap to detection recall on RRSIS-D and selection on RISBench, and an exposure audit confirms robustness to pretraining leakage. Code will be released upon acceptance at the project page https://avalon-s.github.io/GeoSelect/.

</details>


### [170] [SharpSplat: Edge-Regularized 3D Gaussian Splatting for High Fidelity Urban Building Reconstruction from UAV images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03872)
*Porus Vaid,Shivam Chopra,Vaibhav Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对无人机影像三维重建中3DGS难以捕捉建筑立面锐利几何边界的问题，提出语义边缘正则化框架，利用SAM 3生成建筑蒙版并提取边缘，通过对齐渲染图像梯度与提取边缘，迫使高斯体收敛为清晰结构几何，在不修改3DGS管线的情况下显著提升边缘保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS技术从无人机影像重建高保真三维建筑模型时，难以处理建筑立面，无法捕捉锐利的几何过渡，影响了大规模数字孪生的发展。

Method: 提出语义边缘正则化框架：首先利用SAM 3生成精确建筑蒙版并提取具有建筑学意义的边缘；然后在训练过程中将渲染图像梯度与提取的边缘进行对齐，迫使高斯体收敛为锐利的结构几何形状。

Result: 在校园环境、密集城市中心和自定义住宅数据集上的评估表明，该方法在不修改3DGS管线架构的情况下，显著提升了边缘保真度，并对不同建筑类型、屋顶几何结构和城市密度均展现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的语义边缘正则化框架能够有效增强3DGS在建筑立面重建中的边缘锐利度，是一种轻量级且鲁棒的改进方案，适用于大规模数字孪生场景。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity 3D building models from UAV imagery is essential for large-scale digital twin development. However, existing 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) techniques often struggle with building facades, failing to capture sharp geometric transitions. To address this, we propose a semantic edge regularization framework that supervises 3DGS to produce crisp architectural boundaries. Our method leverages SAM 3 to generate precise building masks, from which we extract architecturally significant edges. During training, we align rendered image gradients with these extracted edges, forcing the Gaussians to converge into sharp structural geometries. Evaluations across campus environments, dense urban centers, and custom residential datasets demonstrate significant improvements in edge fidelity without requiring architectural modifications to the 3DGS pipeline. Our approach proves robust across diverse building types, roof geometries, and urban densities.

</details>


### [171] [MACRO: Training-free Multi-plane Attention for Closeup Render Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03875)
*Nitzan Hodos,Roy Amoyal,Lior Fritz,Ianir Ideses,Sagie Benaim,Netalee Efrat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MACRO是一种无需训练的方法，通过利用场景的3D结构进行深度平面分解、尺度匹配裁剪和深度感知注意力掩码，解决了3D高斯泼溅在近景渲染中因尺度差异导致的跨视图注意力匹配错误问题，在近景新视角合成任务上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在近景渲染时质量严重下降，现有基于扩散的参考图像条件增强方法因近景与参考视图之间的尺度差异而产生显著伪影。作者分析了根本原因：参考条件增强模型的特征不具备尺度不变性，导致跨视图注意力无法正确匹配不同尺度下的相同内容，且VAE编码器不具有尺度等变性无法在潜空间修正。

Method: MACRO将近景视图分解为多个深度平面，在图像空间中对每个平面裁剪并缩放参考图以匹配其尺度，然后采用深度感知的注意力掩码，使每个token只关注与其尺度匹配的参考图，从而无需修改架构或额外训练。

Result: 在两个新提出的近景新视角合成基准上，MACRO在重建指标和感知指标上均优于现有的3DGS和扩散方法，达到了SOTA效果。

Conclusion: MACRO通过解耦深度平面并匹配尺度，有效解决了近景渲染中的尺度不一致问题，为高质量近景新视角合成提供了一种无需训练的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Close-up rendering, zooming into a scene well beyond any training camera, is important for virtual production and interactive 3D content, yet remains an open challenge. 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity, real-time novel view synthesis, but its rendering quality degrades at close range. Recent diffusion-based methods that enhance the rendering by conditioning on reference images from the training set produce significant artifacts in this setting. We analyze this failure and identify its root cause: the scale gap between the close-up and reference views. We show that the features in reference-conditioned enhancement models are not scale-invariant, causing cross-view attention to retrieve incorrect correspondences when the same content appears at different scales, and that this mismatch cannot be corrected in latent space because the VAE encoder is not scale-equivariant. Building on this analysis we introduce MACRO, Multi-plane Attention for Closeup Render Optimization, a training-free method for high-quality close-up novel view synthesis from 3DGS. MACRO resolves the scale gap by leveraging the scene's known 3D structure: it decomposes the close-up into depth planes, crops and resizes references in image space to match the scale of each plane before encoding, and applies a depth-aware attention mask so each token attends only to scale-matched references. The method requires no architectural changes or additional training. We further contribute two new close-up novel view synthesis benchmarks, the first standardized evaluation protocol for this setting, and demonstrate state-of-the-art results on both, outperforming existing 3DGS and diffusion-based methods on both reconstruction and perceptual metrics. Project page: https://nitzanhod.github.io/MACRO

</details>


### [172] [SGF-CDNet: A Consistency-Discrepancy Graph Network over Semantic-Geometric Fused Nodes for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03883)
*Jiayao Jiang,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGF-CDNet，通过构建语义-几何融合节点（SGF节点）和一致性-差异性图网络（CD-GNN），从面部区域间的和谐/冲突关系入手进行深度伪造检测，在公开数据集上取得优秀性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法主要关注明显的伪造伪影，但高质量伪造人脸往往缺乏明显痕迹，其不同面部区域之间存在微妙的不和谐关系，现有方法未能有效捕捉此类区域间的结构性矛盾。

Method: 首先，融合人脸解析的语义区域信息与人脸关键点的几何信息构建SGF节点；然后，通过双路径CD-GNN在图节点上进行并行关系推理：一致性路径评估面部组件是否符合自然生物学模式，差异性路径挖掘伪造引入的结构冲突与特征矛盾。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，SGF-CDNet取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: SGF-CDNet通过捕捉面部区域间的和谐与冲突关系，有效识别深度伪造人脸，为伪造人脸检测提供了一种可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of deepfakes necessitates robust face forgery detection. Although forged faces may lack obvious artifacts, they often contain subtle disharmony among different facial regions. We propose SGF-CDNet, a Consistency-Discrepancy Graph Network (CD-GNN) over Semantic-Geometric Fused (SGF) nodes. First, SGF-CDNet constructs SGF nodes by deeply fusing semantic regions from face parsing with geometric information from facial landmarks, allowing nodes to capture both high-level concepts and precise geometric constraints. Next, a dual-path CD-GNN performs parallel relational reasoning on these nodes across two dimensions: consistency and discrepancy. The consistency path evaluates if facial components follow natural biological patterns, while the discrepancy path mines for structural tensions and feature conflicts introduced by forgeries. By integrating these processes, our model effectively identifies disharmonious relationships between facial components. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that SGF-CDNet achieves superior performance, establishing it as a reliable solution for face forgery detection.

</details>


### [173] [BAT3R: Bootstrapping Articulated 3D Reconstruction from 2D Image Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03891)
*Jakub Zadrozny,Oisin Mac Aodha,Hakan Bilen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需人工标注的关节物体3D重建训练框架，仅需每类一个带骨架的标准网格和未标注2D图像集，通过迭代预测点图、估计关节姿态并生成新训练数据，性能接近需要人工策划数据集的SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图关节物体3D重建方法依赖大量人工合成的3D关节数据及精心设计的姿态分布，生成逼真的关节姿态成本高昂且耗时，亟待降低对人工标注3D数据的依赖。

Method: 1) 用标准姿态渲染图训练弱3D形状预测器；2) 迭代地将标准网格拟合到预测的点图上，同时估计物体关节状态和相机位姿；3) 用恢复的关节和视角渲染更新的合成训练数据，逐步提升预测器性能。

Result: 在弱3D监督条件下，该方法达到了与需要人工策划关节训练数据集的DualPM相当的重建性能。

Conclusion: 通过迭代自训练机制，仅需每类一个带骨架的标准网格，即可有效减少对大规模人工标注关节3D数据的需求，实现高质量的单视图关节物体重建。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of articulated objects from a single image is challenging because large training datasets with paired image and 3D supervision are difficult to obtain. Recent point map-based methods achieve strong performance but rely on synthetic datasets rendered from manually created articulated 3D assets with carefully curated pose distributions. While camera viewpoints can be easily sampled, generating realistic object articulations remains costly and labor-intensive. We propose a training framework that reduces this requirement by leveraging unannotated 2D images collections with only a single rigged canonical mesh per category. Starting from a weak 3D shape predictor trained on canonical-pose renders, we iteratively estimate object articulation and camera pose by fitting the mesh to predicted point maps. The recovered articulations and viewpoints are then used to render updated synthetic training data, progressively improving the predictor. Despite using substantially weaker 3D supervision, our models achieve performance comparable with DualPM, which requires manually curated articulated training datasets.

</details>


### [174] [DICT: Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement for Conditional Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03899)
*Chunnan Shang,Xin Zhang,Zhizhong Wang,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DICT，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过数据注入将噪声扰动的条件信号融入早期去噪阶段，并采用对比轨迹精炼在相邻去噪状态间进行成对比较，从而在不依赖任务特定架构的情况下提升条件图像生成的保真度和感知质量。在风格迁移、超分辨率和去模糊等任务上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件图像生成方法存在两方面局限：任务特定设计虽能提升性能但限制泛化能力；无训练损失引导方法将丰富条件压缩为标量目标进行逐步引导，导致信息瓶颈和沿采样轨迹的误差累积。亟需一个有效的统一框架来应对多样化的条件图像生成任务。

Method: 提出DICT，包含两个核心组件：1) 数据注入(Data Injection)：将噪声扰动的条件信号融入早期去噪阶段，通过引导去噪自适应地选择和蒸馏条件中的任务关键信息，保留空间丰富性并确保条件到生成的精确对齐；2) 对比轨迹精炼(Contrastive Trajectory Refinement)：在相邻去噪状态间进行成对比较，逐步提升样本质量。该方法为无训练推理方法，不依赖任务特定架构。

Result: 在风格迁移、图像超分辨率和图像去模糊等条件图像生成任务上的大量实验表明，DICT在保真度和感知质量上均一致优于代表性的任务特定方法和损失引导基线方法。

Conclusion: DICT通过数据注入和对比轨迹精炼，在统一扩散公式下实现了跨任务迁移，同时保持推理简单。该方法有效解决了现有方法在泛化性和信息瓶颈方面的问题，为条件图像生成提供了一种有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for conditional image generation, yet existing approaches generally follow two directions: task-specific designs that can improve performance but limit generalization, and training-free loss guidance that compresses rich conditions into scalar objectives and applies stepwise guidance, leading to information bottlenecks and error accumulation along the sampling trajectory. Given the urgent need for an effective unified framework across diverse conditional image generation tasks, we propose Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement (DICT), a training-free inference method that enhances conditional image generation without introducing task-dependent architectures. DICT introduces Data Injection, where noise-perturbed conditional signals are integrated into early denoising stages; by performing guided denoising on these injected signals, DICT adaptively selects and distills task-salient information from the raw condition, effectively preserving spatial richness and ensuring precise condition-to-generation alignment. Furthermore, DICT applies Contrastive Trajectory Refinement across adjacent denoising states, enabling pairwise comparisons that progressively improve sample quality. These designs keep inference simple while improving cross-task transfer under a unified diffusion formulation. Extensive experiments on conditional image generation tasks (e.g., style transfer, image super-resolution, and image deblurring) show consistent gains in fidelity and perceptual quality over representative task-specific and loss-guided baselines.

</details>


### [175] [USE: A Unified Self-Ensembling Framework for Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03900)
*Siru Jiang,Jian Liang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了测试时提示调优（TPT）方法，发现其优化可解释为从自生成伪标签中隐式学习。基于此，提出统一自集成框架（USE），在优化和推理阶段分别采用自适应自集成策略，强调测试图像本身而非其增强视图以获得更可靠的伪标签，从而统一两个阶段的目标。实验表明SE和USE均优于现有方法，且SE可作为轻量级免优化TTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPT方法虽有效，但其优化机制未被深入理解。作者揭示TPT的本质是隐式地从自生成伪标签中学习，这为改进提供了理论基础。同时，优化与推理阶段目标不一致的问题需要解决。

Method: 提出统一自集成框架USE：1）优化阶段引入自集成（SE）策略，自适应地强调测试图像本身而非其增强视图，以获得更可靠的伪标签；2）推理阶段应用相同策略，统一两个阶段的目标；3）SE也可作为轻量级免优化的TTA方法独立使用。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SE和USE分别优于对应的基线方法；SE与现有TTA方法集成时能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过揭示TPT的伪标签学习本质，统一优化与推理目标的自集成框架能有效提升CLIP模型在测试时自适应任务上的性能，且SE作为一种轻量级方法具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a popular paradigm for improving the performance of vision-language models (e.g., CLIP) on downstream tasks. Among existing CLIP-based TTA methods, Test-Time Prompt Tuning (TPT) is a pioneering work that optimizes textual prompts using multiple test-time augmentations and remains a strong baseline to date. In this work, we revisit TPT and reveal that its optimization can be interpreted as implicitly learning from self-generated pseudo labels. Building on this perspective, we propose a unified self-ensembling framework (USE) that ensures consistency between the optimization and inference stages. During optimization, we introduce a simple yet effective self-ensembling (SE) strategy that emphasizes the test image itself over its augmented views adaptively to obtain more reliable pseudo labels. To fully exploit the potential of augmentations, we further apply the same strategy at inference time, unifying the objectives of both stages. Notably, SE can also act as a lightweight optimization-free TTA method. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that SE and USE outperform their counterparts, respectively. Furthermore, SE yields consistent performance gains when integrated with existing TTA methods. The code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/USE.

</details>


### [176] [NavEYE: Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion-Based Situational Awareness System for Intelligent Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03915)
*Ryan Wen Liua,Junxiong Lianga,Haoyu Wanga,Mengwei Baoa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NavEYE的以视觉为中心的多传感器融合系统，通过多约束门控数据关联（MCGA）、距离感知自适应加权融合（DAWF）和时效性衰减拼接融合（TDSF）等方法，融合AIS、雷达和RGB相机数据，增强智能水面车辆（ISV）在复杂导航环境中的态势感知能力，并通过真实数据集验证了系统的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能水面车辆（ISV）的智能性、可靠性和安全性高度依赖于复杂导航环境中的态势感知能力，现有单一传感器存在局限性，亟需一种多传感器融合方法以提升感知质量。

Method: 提出NavEYE系统：1）多约束门控数据关联（MCGA）实现低时延AIS与高时延雷达数据的精确匹配；2）距离感知自适应加权融合（DAWF）和时效性衰减拼接融合（TDSF）处理数据丢失的不确定性；3）基于归一化方位和距离特征的联合约束，实现AIS、雷达与视觉数据的关联融合。

Result: 在真实AIS、雷达和相机数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法在定量和定性评估中的优越性能，系统在可行性、鲁棒性、可用性和有效性方面均表现出色。

Conclusion: NavEYE系统能够自动获取感兴趣船舶的全面信息，增强ISV的态势感知能力，降低碰撞风险，在复杂动态环境中促进航行安全。

Abstract: With the rapid development of sensor and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies, intelligent surface vehicles (ISVs) have gained increasing attention from academia and industry. Their intelligence, reliability, and safety depend heavily on situational awareness in complex navigational environments. To achieve high-quality perception, we develop a vision-centered multi-sensor fusion system, named NavEYE, by exploiting complementary sensors, including the automatic identification system (AIS), radar, and RGB camera. Specifically, we first propose a multi-constrained gated data association method (MCGA) to accurately match low-temporal-resolution AIS data with high-temporal-resolution radar data. Their fusion result is then obtained by selectively implementing distance-aware adaptively weighted fusion (DAWF) and timeliness decay-based stitching fusion (TDSF), which reduce the uncertainty caused by AIS or radar data loss in real-world sensing scenarios. Based on accurate and robust visual object detection, we further associate and fuse AIS, radar, and visual data through joint constraints of normalized bearing and distance features. According to the fusion results, comprehensive information related to ships of interest can be automatically obtained, helping enhance situational awareness and reduce collision risk for ISVs. The feasibility, robustness, usability, and effectiveness of the proposed multi-sensor fusion method and situational awareness system are demonstrated through extensive experiments on a real-world sensing dataset collected from AIS, radar, and camera. The experimental results show the superior performance of our fusion method in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations. In addition, the shipboard NavEYE system can promote navigational safety for ISVs in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [177] [EgoInertia-MI: A Multimodal Egocentric Vision and IMU Benchmark for Motor Impairment Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03934)
*Fatemah Alhamdoosh,Pietro Pala,Abduallah Mohamed,DK Arvind*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoInertia-MI，一个结合第一人称视频和可穿戴IMU信号的多模态基准数据集，用于运动障碍分析。数据集包含19个上下肢活动，三种严重程度（无、轻度、重度）。实验表明，第一人称视频提供强线索，多模态融合效果最佳（严重程度估计Macro-F1 0.78，动作识别Macro-F1 0.93）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床评估间歇性且无法捕捉运动行为的细微时间变化；现有可穿戴IMU和第三人称视频方法存在隐私问题和采集约束；第一人称视觉提供自然且隐私友好的替代方案，但缺乏相关基准数据集。

Method: 构建EgoInertia-MI多模态数据集，同步采集第一人称视频和可穿戴IMU信号；包含19个上下肢活动，由健康志愿者模拟三种严重程度（无、轻度、重度）；建立动作识别和运动障碍严重程度估计两个基准任务；评估多种单模态和多模态基线方法。

Result: 多模态融合取得最优性能：严重程度估计Macro-F1 0.78，动作识别Macro-F1 0.93。第一人称视频在运动障碍评估中提供强判别线索。

Conclusion: 结合第一人称视觉和可穿戴传感在生态有效性和隐私保护的运动评估方面具有潜力，为相关领域提供新的基准数据集。

Abstract: Motor impairments, including tremor, bradykinesia, gait abnormalities, and postural instability, are common across many neurological and movement-related conditions. Conventional clinical assessments are often intermittent and may fail to capture subtle temporal variations in motor behavior. While wearable IMUs and third-person video have shown promise for objective motor assessment, third-person recordings raise privacy concerns and require constrained acquisition setups. In contrast, egocentric vision provides a more naturalistic and privacyaware alternative. In this work, we introduce EgoInertia-MI, a multimodal benchmark dataset combining synchronized egocentric video and wearable IMU signals for motor impairment analysis. The dataset contains 19 upper- and lower-body activities performed by healthy volunteers simulating varying levels of motor impairment severity levels: no impairment, mild impairment, and severe impairment. We establish two benchmark tasks: action recognition and motor impairment severity estimation, and evaluate multiple unimodal and multimodal baselines. Experimental results show that egocentric video provides strong cues for motor impairment assessment, while multimodal fusion achieves the best overall performance, reaching 0.78 Macro-F1 for severity estimation and 0.93 Macro-F1 for action recognition. These findings highlight the potential of combining egocentric vision and wearable sensing for ecologically valid and privacy-aware motor assessment. Code and data are available at:https://fatemah-alh.github.io/EgoInertia-MI-Page/.

</details>


### [178] [A Large-Scale Dataset and a New Method for RemoteSensing Traffic Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03945)
*Zhigang Yang,Huiguang Yao,Linmao Tian,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了NWPU-Traffic大规模交通目标分割数据集，涵盖4类交通目标、来自7国49城市的多样化场景，并提出基于空间通道保持特征交互与自适应特征解码器的分割方法，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感影像分割数据集在目标类别和场景多样性方面存在不足，限制了模型在真实场景中全面评估交通能力的效果，亟需构建大规模、多样化的交通目标分割数据集。

Method: 1) 构建NWPU-Traffic数据集，包含car、airplane、ship、train四类交通目标，覆盖7国49城市，提供实例级标注；2) 提出空间通道保持特征交互与自适应特征解码器的分割方法，支持跨尺度及复杂环境下的鲁棒分割。

Result: 在NWPU-Traffic数据集上建立多个主流分割网络的基准，并通过大量实验和消融研究验证了所提方法在跨尺度及复杂环境下的分割有效性。

Conclusion: NWPU-Traffic数据集填补了现有数据集在分辨率和场景多样性方面的关键缺陷，所提分割方法在复杂场景下表现优越，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery plays a crucial role in evaluating regional transportation capacity. However, existing segmentation datasets often lack diversity in object categories and scenes, limiting the ability of models to comprehensively evaluate trans portation capacity in real-world scenes. To alleviate this gap, we construct a large-scale and diverse dataset for transportation object segmentation, named as NWPU-Traffic. This dataset encompass four traffic object categories (car, airplane, ship, and train) and a wide range of scenes from 49 cities across 7 countries, with instance-level annotations to ensure precise segmentation of individual objects, which bridges critical shortcomings in resolution and scene diversity in existing datasets. Leveraging this dataset, we establish a benchmark with several popular segmentation networks. Furthermore, we propose a novel segmentation method that leverages spatial-channel preserving feature interaction and an adaptive feature decoder, enabling robust segmentation across varying scales and complex environments. Extensive experiments and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our approach. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/CVer-Yang/NWPU-Traffic.

</details>


### [179] [TESSERA v2: Scaling Pixel-wise Earth Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03949)
*Zhengpeng Feng,Sadiq Jaffer,Ira Shokar,Jovana Knezevic,Mark Elvers,Clement Atzberger,Robin Young,Aneesh Naik,Niall Robinson,Andrew Blake,David Coomes,Anil Madhavapeddy,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过395次训练实验（1024个GH200超算芯片），系统研究了像素级地球观测基础模型的缩放规律。发现预训练损失难以预测下游性能（|Pearson r|<0.2），提出编码器与数据应同步增长、投影仪保持固定的计算分配规则。基于此规则训练的TESSERA v2-1B-M（2100万参数）蒸馏模型在15个下游任务上超越所有开源及专有模型，并支持Matryoshka表示（16维前缀保留全128维92%性能）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素级地球观测基础模型在生成空间嵌入方面取得了SOTA性能，但其缩放规律和预训练预算分配策略仍不明确，缺乏大规模的实证研究来指导计算资源的有效分配。

Method: 在固定Barlow Twins框架下进行395次受控缩放实验（1024个GH200超算芯片），系统评估不同计算预算下编码器、投影仪和数据规模的缩放策略；基于发现的规则训练大模型并蒸馏为紧凑学生模型，支持Matryoshka表示。

Result: 预训练损失与下游性能相关性极低（|Pearson r|<0.2）；编码器与数据应同步增长，投影仪固定；2100万参数的TESSERA v2-1B-M蒸馏模型在15个任务上超越所有对比模型；16维Matryoshka前缀保留128维92%性能。

Conclusion: 像素级EO基础模型的缩放应遵循：训练大编码器、按下游性能选模型、蒸馏为灵活学生模型。该经验性规则为计算分配提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Pixel-wise Earth-observation (EO) foundation models are now achieving state-of-the-art performance via generated spatial embeddings. However, how these models scale and how best to spend a pretraining budget remain poorly understood. We present the largest controlled scaling study for EO to date: 395 training runs on 1,024 GH200 superchips within a fixed pixel-wise Barlow Twins family, each evaluated on 15 downstream tasks. We find that pretraining loss barely predicts downstream performance (|Pearson r| < 0.2), so selecting models by loss wastes a large share of the compute. We also find that, as the training budget grows, the encoder and the data should grow together while the projector stays fixed, which gives a simple rule for allocating compute. Using this rule, we train a family of pixel-wise models (0.5B and 1B, with a 2B model in training) and distill them into compact students for embeddings-as-data deployment. The 21-million-parameter distilled TESSERA v2-1B-M in aggregate outperforms all open and proprietary models tested, some of which are orders of magnitude larger. These students produce Matryoshka representations that are inexpensive to serve: a 16-dimensional prefix keeps 92% of the full 128-dimensional performance at 1/8 of the storage. Upon completion of training we plan to release v2 global embeddings covering 2017-2025. Together, these results give a concrete, empirically grounded recipe for scaling pixel-wise EO foundation models: train large encoders, select by downstream performance, and distil into flexible student models. All code will be released at https://github.com/ucam-eo/tessera.

</details>


### [180] [Reward Lightning: Fast Video Generation via Homologous Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03960)
*Jiaxiang Cheng,Bing Ma,Xuhua Ren,Kai Yu,Peng Zhang,Tianxiang Zheng,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Reward Lightning框架，通过在共享潜在空间中统一偏好对齐与蒸馏加速，解决视频扩散模型中两目标优化冲突问题。核心包括潜在奖励模型(LRM)和同源偏好蒸馏(HPD)，实现1-4步生成高质量视频，VBench评分提升2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型中的偏好对齐与蒸馏加速是两个独立目标，现有方法在不相交的表征空间上优化，导致改进一个目标往往会损害另一个目标。亟需一种统一框架来同时实现两目标的协同优化。

Method: 1) 提出潜在奖励模型(LRM)，直接在潜在空间评分视频，无需解码回像素空间；2) 基于LRM提出同源偏好蒸馏(HPD)，共享同一骨干网络进行对抗性蒸馏和偏好对齐的联合优化，使梯度冲突最小化。

Result: LRM在偏好准确率上超越像素级和潜在级奖励基线分别达11.0%和14.7%；Reward Lightning在1-4步内生成高保真视频，平均VBench评分提升2.1%，在文本对齐、运动质量和视觉质量方面均领先。

Conclusion: 同源表征（在相同潜在特征上评估两目标）是解决多目标优化冲突的有效原则，为视频扩散模型的高效对齐与加速提供了新的统一范式。

Abstract: Achieving simultaneous preference alignment and distillation acceleration in video diffusion models remains an open challenge. Existing methods optimize the two objectives over mismatched representation spaces, where improving one objective often compromises the other. To overcome this, we propose Reward Lightning, a unified framework that aligns and accelerates a video diffusion model within a single shared representation. Its central principle is homology: both objectives are evaluated on identical latent features, which mitigates the gradient conflicts that arise when they are optimized over disjoint representations. As a foundational component, we first introduce a latent reward model (LRM) that scores videos directly in the latent space, without decoding back to the pixel space. Building on the LRM, homologous preference distillation (HPD) reuses this shared backbone to perform adversarial distillation and preference alignment jointly, yielding few-step generators that remain faithful and well aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the LRM surpasses pixel-level and latent-level reward baselines by $11.0\%$ and $14.7\%$ in preference accuracy, and that Reward Lightning generates high-fidelity videos in merely $1$ to $4$ steps, improving the average VBench score by $2.1\%$ while leading in text alignment, motion quality, and visual quality. Project page: https://reward-lightning.github.io.

</details>


### [181] [DS-SAC: Density Search for Sample Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03972)
*Suraj Thapa,Muhammad Aminul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-SAC，一种确定性鲁棒估计框架，通过搜索稠密区域替代RANSAC的随机最小采样，采用前向/后向搜索进行局部探索，并递归划分点集进行全局探索。实验表明，DS-SAC在单应性、基础矩阵和本质矩阵估计任务上，相比RANSAC等主流方法，取得了更高AUC分数、相当或更低的位姿误差以及更快的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RANSAC及其变体虽然广泛用于鲁棒几何模型估计，但依赖随机最小采样，存在效率低和不稳定性问题。本文旨在提出一种确定性方法，避免随机采样，同时保持高效和鲁棒性。

Method: DS-SAC采用确定性搜索策略：1) 从可用点估计初始模型；2) 通过前向和后向搜索进行局部探索；3) 利用符号残差递归划分点集，在每个有效分区中搜索高共识模型，实现全局探索。算法复杂度为点数的多项式级。

Result: 在大规模真实数据集上，对单应性、基础矩阵和本质矩阵估计任务进行实验，DS-SAC相比RANSAC、MAGSAC、LO-RANSAC和GC-RANSAC等主流方法，取得了更高的AUC分数，竞争性或更低的位姿中值误差，以及更快的运行时间。

Conclusion: DS-SAC作为确定性鲁棒估计框架，有效替代了基于随机共识的方法，在保持多项式时间复杂度的同时，在精度和效率上均优于主流随机采样方法。

Abstract: Robust geometric model estimation is a fundamental problem in computer vision. RANSAC and its variants remain widely used for this task; however, they rely on stochastic minimal sampling. In this article, we propose Density Search Sample Consensus (DS-SAC), a deterministic robust estimation framework, that avoids repeated random sampling by searching dense regions. Starting from an initial model estimated from the available points, the method performs local exploration via forward and backward search. To facilitate global exploration, DS-SAC recursively partitions the point set using signed residuals and searches each valid partition for high-consensus models. We show that DS-SAC has polynomial complexity with respect to the number of points, making it an efficient alternative to stochastic consensus-based methods. Experiments on large-scale real-world datasets for homography, fundamental matrix, and essential matrix estimation show that DS-SAC achieves higher AUC scores, competitive or lower median pose errors, and faster runtime compared with widely used robust estimators, including RANSAC, MAGSAC, LO-RANSAC, and GC-RANSAC.

</details>


### [182] [InSpace: Structure-Aware 3D Indoor Scene Generation from a Single 360° Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03990)
*Gwanhyeong Koo,Hyunsu Kim,Youngji Kim,Taejae Lee,Siwoo Lim,Sunjae Yoon,Suyong Yeon,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InSpace提出了一个基于360°全景图的结构感知三维室内场景生成框架，通过分阶段的空间先验估计、视图选择性交叉注意力和全局-局部混合注意力机制，实现从单张ERP图像生成完整的室内场景结构和带有纹理的独立资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图转3D方法主要关注资产级别的生成，忽略了室内场景的结构化布局。单张有限视场的图像缺乏空间覆盖能力来恢复连贯的全局布局，因此需要利用360°全景图来获取更完整的空间信息。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 估计部分场景几何作为空间先验；2) 使用视图选择性交叉注意力生成粗场景结构；3) 通过全局-局部混合注意力结合流匹配生成详细的布局、资产几何和纹理。同时提出了基于3D-FRONT的ERP-FRONT成对数据集。

Result: InSpace能够从单张ERP图像生成具有结构布局的完整3D室内场景以及分离的带纹理资产，在3D和2D指标上均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: InSpace通过利用360°全景图并设计结构感知的生成框架，有效解决了单图室内场景生成中结构布局缺失的问题，为下游应用提供了空间锚点。

Abstract: Recent advances in single image-to-3D generation have enabled high-quality asset synthesis, yet extending these capabilities to indoor scene generation remains challenging. Existing methods focus on asset-level generation while neglecting the structural layout, which is essential for downstream applications and serves as the spatial anchor for grounding assets. However, a single image with a limited field of view lacks the spatial coverage to recover a coherent global layout. To this end, we use a 360° image represented in equirectangular projection (ERP) and propose InSpace, a structure-aware framework for 3D indoor scene generation. InSpace comprises three stages: (1) estimating partial scene geometry as spatial priors, (2) generating coarse scene structure with view-selective cross-attention, and (3) producing detailed layout and asset geometry with textures through a global-local hybrid attention, using flow matching. We also propose ERP-FRONT, a paired ERP-Image-to-3D indoor scene dataset based on 3D-FRONT. Experiments show that InSpace generates complete 3D indoor scenes with structural layout, along with separate textured assets from a single ERP image, achieving strong performance across 3D and 2D metrics. Project Page: https://kookie12.github.io/InSpace-Project-Page/

</details>


### [183] [Full Glyph Images Beat Token Embeddings: A Controlled Study for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03994)
*Shuyang Xiang,Hao Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用视觉图像替代离散token嵌入来表示中文文本，通过共享ResNet和浅层ViT处理字符序列的栅格化图像。在控制实验中，基于视觉的模型在所有解码器骨干上持续优于基于索引的基线（峰值准确率0.429 vs 0.355，提升21%），收敛速度更快（约一半训练轮次），且对字符损坏具有鲁棒性。消融实验表明优势依赖空间连贯输入和2D位置编码。跨文字比较发现该优势不直接迁移到英语字母系统，表明汉字均匀的视觉密度和部首结构是使能条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战语言模型将文本表示为离散token序列的默认假设，探究视觉输入能否替代离散Token化用于中文语言建模。

Method: 构建双分支控制框架：视觉分支将字符序列栅格化为图像，经共享ResNet+浅层ViT编码；索引分支使用标准token嵌入。两分支共享相同解码器骨干、训练目标、优化器和数据课程，确保性能差异仅源于输入模态。

Result: 视觉模型峰值准确率0.429 vs 基线0.355（相对提升21%），收敛约需一半训练轮次。前5轮（总数据21%以内）优势即显现。字符损坏下视觉模型性能与无损坏的索引基线持平。

Conclusion: Transformer比通常假设的更加模态无关；离散token化并非中文语言建模的基本要求；汉字均匀的视觉密度和部首结构是视觉输入有效的重要条件。

Abstract: Modern language models generally represent text as sequences of discrete token embeddings, an assumption deeply rooted in current practice but rarely questioned. We challenge this representation, especially for Chinese, by replacing index-based token embeddings entirely with a single rasterized image of the character sequence, processed by a vision encoder composed of a shared ResNet and a shallow Vision Transformer. To isolate the role of input representation, we construct a dual-branch controlled framework in which both a Vision-based model and an index-based baseline share an identical decoder backbone, training objective, optimizer, and data curriculum. Any performance difference is therefore attributable to the input modality only. Across all tested decoder backbones, the Vision-based model consistently outperforms the baseline, reaching a peak accuracy of 0.429 versus 0.355 for the index-based baseline,that is, a 21% relative improvement, while converging in about half the number of training epochs. The advantage emerges especially within the first five epochs (under 21% of total data) and persists under moderate character corruption: the corrupted Vision model matches the clean index-based baseline. Ablation studies reveal that the advantage requires both spatially coherent input and a ViT encoder with 2D positional encodings. A cross-script comparison on English shows the advantage does not transfer directly to alphabetic writing systems, suggesting that the uniform visual density and radical structure of Chinese characters are enabling conditions. These findings suggest that transformers are more modality-agnostic than commonly assumed, and that discrete tokenization is not a fundamental requirement for Chinese language modeling.

</details>


### [184] [SAGE: Synchronized Action-Gaze Recognition and Anticipation for Human Behavior Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04017)
*Chenyi Kuang,Nakul Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架SAGE，通过Transformer架构将人-物交互（HOI）与人类注视的识别和预测联合建模，在三个基准数据集上超越现有专用模型，并建立了新的Exo-Cook基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常将注视与动作分开处理，忽略了两者间的相互依赖关系及统一解法的优势。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，基于Transformer架构，将注视数据融入时空注意力机制，实现HOI与注视的同步识别与预测；支持第一人称（egocentric）和第三人称（exocentric）双视角；并建立新的Exo-Cook基准数据集。

Result: 在VidHOI、EGTEA Gaze+和Exo-Cook三个数据集上，联合建模当前与未来帧的注视和动作始终取得强一致结果，常超越针对单一任务的专用SOTA模型。

Conclusion: 统一建模注视与动作为更直观的人机交互奠定了基础。

Abstract: Human object interaction (HOI), gaze pattern, and their anticipation are intricately linked, providing valuable insights into cognitive processes, intentions, and behavior. However, most existing models handle gaze and actions separately, missing both their interdependence and the advantages of a unified solution. This paper presents a novel unified framework, SAGE (Synchronized Action-GazE), which integrates simultaneous recognition and anticipation of both HOI and human gaze into a single unified end-to-end trainable model. Our approach leverages a transformer-based architecture and incorporates gaze data into spatiotemporal attention mechanisms to simultaneously predict current and future human actions and gaze behavior. We explore this bidirectional relationship between gaze and actions under different scenarios, whether requiring a close-up, detailed view (egocentric) or a wider, more contextual view (exocentric), making our framework versatile for various applications. Additionally, due to lack of datasets for comprehensive analysis of both HOI and gaze in exocentric videos, we establish a new benchmark Exo-Cook to facilitate further research in this domain. Experiments on three benchmark datasets: VidHOI, EGTEA Gaze+, and Exo-Cook show that jointly modeling gaze and actions across current and future frames achieves consistently strong results, often surpassing specialized state-of-the-art models tailored to individual tasks. By unifying actions and attention in a comprehensive way, our work lays the groundwork for more intuitive human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [185] [Paired Uterine Whole-Slide Images and Pathology Reports for Multimodal Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04020)
*Han Li,Jingsong Liu,Ayako Ura,Junlin Hou,Zhengyang Xu,Azar Kazemi,Oskar Thaeter,Christian Grashei,Fabian Gülhan,Reza Nasirigerdeh,Xun Ma,Rui Yan,Hao Chen,S. Kevin Zhou,Nassir Navab,Carolin Mogler,Peter Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TUM-Uteria是一个包含216例临床病例、455对全切片图像与病理报告配对的子宫病理数据集，经过病理学家多阶段验证，支持计算病理学中的多模态学习与自动报告生成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理数据集多聚焦于单标签的图像级或切片级标注（如疾病类别），缺少将全切片图像与完整临床诊断病理报告配对的数据集，限制了多模态AI模型在病理报告自动生成和辅助诊断方面的发展。

Method: 从三级医疗中心收集子宫病理数据，构建了包含216例临床病例、455对切片级WSI-报告配对的数据集，并经过委员会认证的病理学家进行多阶段结构化验证以确保注释可靠性。

Result: 成功构建了TUM-Uteria数据集，包含216例临床病例、455对切片级WSI-病理报告配对，通过了病理学家的多阶段验证。

Conclusion: TUM-Uteria为计算病理学研究提供了宝贵的资源，支持全切片图像分析、多模态学习和自动病理报告生成等研究方向，有望推动AI辅助诊断的发展。

Abstract: Uterine diseases represent an important category of gynecologic pathology and require accurate histopathological assessment for diagnosis and treatment planning. Whole-slide images (WSI) have enabled the digital transformation of pathology workflows and provided new opportunities for artificial intelligence (AI) in computational pathology. In particular, multimodal models that jointly analyze histopathology images and pathology reports have shown promising potential for automated pathology report generation and AI-assisted diagnosis.
