However, the development of such systems remains limited by the scarcity of datasets that pair whole-slide images with clinically meaningful pathology reports. Instead, existing pathology datasets focus on patch- or slide-level annotations of a single endpoint (e.g., disease class), which do not fully capture the rich information in full clinical diagnostic workflow reports. Here, we introduce TUM-Uteria, a uterine pathology dataset comprising WSIs paired with diagnostic pathology reports at both the case and slide levels, collected from a tertiary medical center. The dataset contains 216 clinical cases, comprising 455 slide-level WSI-report pairs. The dataset underwent a structured multi-stage validation procedure involving board-certified pathologists to ensure reliable annotations. TUM-Uteria supports research in computational pathology, including whole-slide image analysis, multimodal learning, and automated pathology report generation.

</details>


### [186] [SiamJEPA: On the Role of Siamese Student Encoders in JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04044)
*Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SiamJEPA，在JEPA框架中引入孪生学生编码器，验证了其作为有效正则化器的作用，在ImageNet线性探测上优于单编码器JEPA变体，并能加速早期学习、提高表示可分离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA模型（如I-JEPA、V-JEPA）在学生网络中仅使用单编码器，而孪生编码器更自然地契合脑启发表示学习框架，但其在JEPA中的作用尚未被探索。

Method: 提出SiamJEPA，采用掩码孪生学生编码器结合指数移动平均（EMA）教师网络，可视为脑启发模型PhiNet的JEPA形式化。

Result: 在ImageNet线性探测上，SiamJEPA相比单编码器JEPA变体持续表现更优，在有限训练预算下取得更高准确率，且优于需要更长训练的MAE；孪生编码器作为有效正则化器提升了表示可分离性并加速了早期学习。

Conclusion: 孪生学生编码器不仅是架构选择，更是预测性表示学习中重要的归纳偏置；将孪生学生架构融入JEPA是提升自监督表示学习的简单有效方法。

Abstract: Recently, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have attracted significant attention in the computer vision and machine learning communities as a promising framework for self-supervised representation learning. Unlike masked autoencoders that reconstruct pixels, JEPA models learn representations by predicting latent embeddings of masked regions. Existing JEPA-based methods, such as I-JEPA and V-JEPA, typically employ a single encoder in the student network. In contrast, using Siamese encoders for student network is more naturally aligned with brain-inspired representation learning frameworks, yet their role in JEPA models remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the effect of Siamese student encoders in JEPA-based representation learning. To this end, we propose SiamJEPA, masked Siamese student encoders equipped with an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher network. SiamJEPA can also be viewed as a JEPA formulation of the brain-inspired representation learning model PhiNet. Through extensive experiments on ImageNet linear probing, we demonstrate that Siamese encoders act as an effective regularizer for the JEPA objective, improving representation separability and accelerating learning during the early stages of training. Furthermore, SiamJEPA consistently outperforms comparable single-encoder JEPA variants under limited training budgets and achieves higher linear probing accuracy than Masked Autoencoders (MAE) which requires longer training. Our findings reveal that Siamese student encoders are not merely an architectural choice but constitute an important inductive bias for predictive representation learning. These results provide new insights into the design of JEPA-based models and suggest that incorporating Siamese student architectures offers a simple yet effective approach for improving self-supervised representation learning.

</details>


### [187] [PreSIST: Vision-Language-Informed Object Persistence Prediction in Open-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04057)
*Amanda Adkins,Tarunvidyut Ravisankar,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PreSIST方法，利用视觉语言模型和场景上下文预测物体在环境中的持久性，无需长期观察即可推断物体在任意未来时间是否仍留在原位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期感知系统只能被动地在重新访问场景后才更新地图，缺乏主动推理物体持久性的能力。同一语义类别的物体（如停车位上的车 vs 红绿灯前的车）在不同场景下持久性差异巨大，需要利用场景上下文进行主动预测。

Method: PreSIST包含两个变体：PreSIST-Lang使用视觉语言模型（VLM）基于物体属性和场景上下文估计持久性先验；PreSIST-Vis是仅视觉的模型，利用PreSIST-Lang生成的伪标签训练。两者结合概率持久性滤波器，在获得观测数据后更新预测。

Result: 在野外物体持久性标注的新数据集上，PreSIST-Lang和PreSIST-Vis在开放世界持久性预测任务中均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: VLM的推理能力能将场景上下文与物体用途和人类活动关联起来，在长期观测可用之前实现持久性预测，为长期自主机器人提供了主动感知环境变化的新范式。

Abstract: Robots deployed over long periods must reason about environments that change over time. Existing long-term perception systems often address object change reactively, updating their maps only after revisiting a scene and observing that an object has moved. Instead, robots should reason proactively about how long objects are likely to persist using the context in which they appear. For example, a car at a traffic light and a car in a parking spot share the same semantic class, but their contexts imply different persistence durations. We propose PreSIST (Predictive Scene-conditioned Instance Survival over Time), a method for predicting whether an observed object will remain in its last seen pose at arbitrary future times. PreSIST estimates instance-level persistence priors from object properties and scene context, then integrates these priors with a probabilistic persistence filter as observations become available. Its key insight is that the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) can relate scene context to likely object use and human activity, enabling persistence prediction before long-term observations are available. We develop two interchangeable variants: PreSIST-Lang, which estimates persistence priors using a VLM, and PreSIST-Vis, a novel vision-only model trained using PreSIST-Lang pseudo-labels for efficient deployment. Experiments on a new dataset of in-the-wild object persistence annotations show that PreSIST-Lang and PreSIST-Vis outperform baselines on open-world persistence prediction.

</details>


### [188] [Enhancing Implicit Neural Representations with Image Feature Embedding for Unsupervised Cardiac Cine MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04069)
*Donghang Lyu,Marius Staring,Yiming Dong,Keupp Jochen,Hildo J. Lamb,Mariya Doneva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种图像域双分支隐式神经表征框架I-FP-INR，通过增加特征处理分支提取互补特征嵌入，提升心脏电影MRI重建质量，在公共和内部数据集上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速心脏电影MRI采集需从欠采样k-space数据重建图像，现有方法中监督学习依赖全采样参考数据（常不可得），无监督方法中的隐式神经表征（INR）架构简单且重建质量好，但仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出I-FP-INR框架，在原始INR基础上引入额外的特征处理分支（dual-branch），用于提取互补特征嵌入以增强整体表征能力，在图像域进行重建。

Result: 在公共数据集和内部数据上的广泛评估表明，该方法在重建质量上持续优于基线方法，并展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双分支INR设计通过互补特征提取有效提升了心脏电影MRI的无监督重建性能，无需全采样参考数据，且适用于多种场景。

Abstract: Cardiac cine Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is a critical diagnostic tool that provides dynamic insights for radiologists. To accelerate acquisition, under-sampled k-space data is often used, requiring reconstruction methods that combine coil sensitivity encoding with prior information to recover missing data. Deep learning approaches have gained more attention for leveraging data-adaptive priors. While supervised learning approaches are a common choice, they depend on fully sampled reference data, which is not always available. Unsupervised methods eliminate the need for fully sampled reference data, which can be advantageous in cardiac cine MRI reconstruction. Among them, implicit neural representations (INRs) have shown great potential due to their simple architecture and good quality reconstructions. In this work, we propose an image-domain dual-branch INR framework, termed I-FP-INR, which extends the original INR design by introducing an additional feature-processing branch. This design aims to extract complementary feature embeddings to enhance the overall representation, thereby benefiting reconstruction. Extensive evaluations on both public datasets and in-house data show consistent improvements over baseline methods in reconstruction quality, with strong robustness across varied scenarios.

</details>


### [189] [Seeing Once is Enough? Online Geometry-Aware Token Pruning for 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04079)
*Ruei-Chi Lai,Bolivar Solarte,Chin-Hsuan Wu,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个无需额外训练的在线token剪枝方法，利用深度信息和相机位姿将输入帧投影到共享体素空间，识别跨帧空间重叠区域并选择性剪枝冗余图像token，在3D问答任务中减少高达50% token用量，并在ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D基准上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D问答任务中需处理多视点场景，导致推理时计算成本高昂；现有离线方案需预处理全部帧，缺乏在线高效处理能力。

Method: 将每帧输入通过深度信息和相机位姿投影至共享体素空间，识别跨帧空间重叠区域，在图像token进入语言模型前选择性剪枝冗余token，实现无需额外训练的在线token剪枝。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和Qwen3-VL-8B上应用该方法，于ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D基准上性能提升，同时减少高达50% token用量。

Conclusion: 提出的在线token剪枝方法能有效降低3D问答任务的计算开销，无需额外训练即可与现有MLLM无缝集成，兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: Recent Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on 2D question answering tasks. However, extending these models to the 3D question answering remains challenging, as they typically require multiple views of the scene, which incurs substantial computational cost at inference. To mitigate this issue, existing solutions rely on strategic frame selection or token-merging algorithms that require preprocessing in advance all frames of the scene, i.e., an offline fashion. In contrast, we propose the first online token-pruning method that can be integrated seamlessly with current MLLM models for 3D question answering tasks, without additional training and with lower memory usage.Our key insight is to project each input frame into a shared voxel space using depth information and camera pose, identifying spatially-overlapped regions across frames and selectively pruning redundant image tokens before they enter the language model. Our method enables efficient online processing while reducing up to 50% of token usage. We apply this approach to Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, demonstrating improved performance on the ScanQA, SQA3D, and OpenEQA-HM3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [190] [Sparse4D-Radar: An Efficient and Robust Framework for Surround-View 3D Object Detection via 4D Radar-Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04098)
*Fuyuan Ai,Yuchen Tan,Jiehui Chen,Zhiwei Xu,Chunyi Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse4D-Radar，一种基于稀疏查询的环绕感知4D雷达-相机融合框架，通过Deformable Fusion模块、Velocity-Consistency Sampling和Adaptive Modality Gating等设计，在OmniHD-Scenes数据集上实现了SOTA的环绕3D检测性能，速度接近10 FPS，较之前方法mAP提升超7%、ODS提升超10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D雷达-相机融合方法仅支持前视感知，缺乏成熟的环视感知方案；直接扩展到360°覆盖会引入过高计算成本，限制实际部署。

Method: 提出Sparse4D-Radar框架，包括：(1) Deformable Fusion模块将雷达-相机特征嵌入稀疏查询，构建轻量版Base；(2) Velocity-Consistency Sampling (VCS)利用雷达速度信息细化特征以实现运动感知；(3) Adaptive Modality Gating (AMG)根据特征置信度动态调整跨模态融合权重；(4) 组合上述组件构建高精度版本Sparse4D-Radar-Acc。

Result: 在OmniHD-Scenes数据集上实现SOTA环绕3D检测性能，在复杂驾驶场景下mAP提升超7%、ODS提升超10%，推理速度接近10 FPS，在检测精度、环境鲁棒性和推理效率间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: Sparse4D-Radar是一种高效鲁棒的环绕感知多模态融合框架，解决了现有方法仅支持前视感知和计算成本高的问题，代码已开源。

Abstract: In recent years, 4D imaging radar has gained wide attention in autonomous driving for its robustness against harsh weather and ability to output target velocity. Nevertheless, mainstream 4D radar-camera fusion methods only support front-view perception, lacking mature solutions for surround-view sensing. Directly expanding these pipelines to full 360° coverage introduces excessive computation cost and limits real-world deployment. To tackle these limitations, this work proposes Sparse4D-Radar, an efficient robust surround-view multi-modal fusion framework. We first design a Deformable Fusion module to embed radar-camera features into sparse queries, constructing the lightweight base version Sparse4D-Radar-Base. Two dedicated modules are further introduced to boost localization accuracy and modality stability: Velocity-Consistency Sampling (VCS) refines features via radar velocity cues for motion awareness, and Adaptive Modality Gating (AMG) dynamically adjusts cross-modal fusion weights according to feature confidence. Combining all components, we build Sparse4D-Radar-Acc for high-precision detection demands. Comprehensive experiments on OmniHD-Scenes verify that our approach achieves state-of-the-art surround-view 3D detection performance. Compared with prior arts, our method obtains over 7% mAP and 10% ODS improvements under complex driving scenes while running at nearly 10 FPS, striking a favorable trade-off among detection accuracy, environmental robustness and inference efficiency. Our open-source code is available at https://github.com/Aiuan/Sparse4D-Radar.

</details>


### [191] [The Multipath Blind Spot: $K$-Agnostic Robust Calibration for Sparse-Anchor Metric Depth from Frozen Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04101)
*Sohag Roy,Rajesh Misra,Swami Shastravidyananda,Tamal Maharaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRAC方法，通过一致性筛选（Theil-Sen拟合+MAD检验）去除有噪声的稀疏度量锚点，解决现有多路径干扰下锚点标定失效问题，无需重新训练即可显著提升单目深度估计的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时标定方法假设锚点干净，但真实传感器的多路径干扰、混合像素等会产生离群值；VI-Depth等方法对多路径离群点有结构性盲区，在有离群锚点时甚至不如未受保护的基线。

Method: 提出MRAC，一种无参数、推理时包装器：先用Theil-Sen拟合获取基础深度与锚点的稳健关系，再用中位数绝对偏差（MAD）检验筛选与基础深度顺序一致的锚点，最后用筛选后的锚点调用标定头。

Result: 在320个单元的基准测试中，MRAC在84%的同骨干单元上胜出；在全部12个多路径干扰单元和16个KITTI单元中优于VI-Depth；KITTI多路径的AbsRel从0.489降至0.151（降低3.2倍），无需重新训练。

Conclusion: MRAC是一种轻量、高效、零训练的鲁棒锚点标定方案，能够抵御真实传感器中的离群值干扰，显著提升单目深度估计在实际场景中的可用性。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundations predict domain-general relative depth but lack absolute scale; a handful of sparse metric anchors from a range sensor can calibrate them to metric depth, an attractive alternative to metric-supervised training. Existing sparse-anchor calibration methods, however, assume the anchors are clean, whereas real sensors produce outliers that are present with the wrong value -- time-of-flight multipath, mixed pixels -- not merely missing. We show that the established residual-on-CFA calibration recipe collapses under such outliers, and that the strongest publicly deployed method, VI-Depth, has a structural multipath blind spot: robust to missing anchors, it falls behind an unprotected baseline on three of four datasets when anchors are present but wrong. We propose Multipath-Robust Anchor Calibration (MRAC), a parameter-free, inference-time wrapper that gates anchors by foundation consistency -- a Theil--Sen fit and a median-absolute-deviation test against the foundation's own relative-depth ordering -- before a single call to the calibration head. MRAC adds no learned parameters, runs its selection in $\approx 50\,μ$s on CPU, and serves anchor budgets $K \in [5,200]$ from one checkpoint. On a $320$-cell benchmark with a same-backbone, same-architecture control, MRAC strictly wins $84\%$ of same-backbone cells across all four outlier families and, against VI-Depth, wins all twelve corrupted multipath cells and all sixteen KITTI cells, reducing KITTI multipath AbsRel by $3.2\times$ ($0.489$ to $0.151$) at zero retraining.

</details>


### [192] [SOV-CAD: Stepwise Orthographic Views Guided CAD Modeling Sequence Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04119)
*Zhaopeng Feng,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Zhengwen Feng,Xinkui Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SOV-CAD框架，利用逐步视觉监督和离线强化学习（Decision Transformer）从图像重建CAD建模序列，实现比现有方法更准确、类人且数据高效的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法整体生成完整CAD序列，忽略了人类设计工作流中迭代、反馈驱动的特性，缺乏逐步视觉监督机制。

Method: 将CAD重建建模为序列决策任务，采用离线强化学习与Decision Transformer架构，在每个建模步骤中利用目标正交投影、增量模型的投影以及活动草图作为视觉反馈，并通过几何对齐奖励引导动作选择。

Result: 在CAD序列重建任务上超越当前最先进方法，并展现出较强的数据效率。

Conclusion: 引入步骤级视觉监督和决策Transformer的离线强化学习框架能有效提升CAD建模序列重建的准确性和类人性，为参数化编辑和设计意图保留提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reconstructing Computer-Aided Design (CAD) modeling sequences from images is crucial for preserving design intent and supporting parametric editing. However, existing methods typically generate full CAD sequences holistically, overlooking the iterative, feedback-driven nature of human design workflows. We address this limitation by introducing the rich stepwise visual supervision: at each modeling step, the system observes the target's orthographic projections, the projections of the incrementally constructed model, and the active sketch, enabling informed action selection. To effectively leverage this on-the-fly feedback, we propose SOV-CAD, a framework that formulates CAD reconstruction as a sequential decision-making task and employs offline reinforcement learning with a Decision Transformer architecture. This design incorporates continuous visual feedback guided by geometric alignment rewards, resulting in a more accurate and human-like modeling process. Extensive experiments show that SOV-CAD surpasses state-of-the-art methods in CAD sequence reconstruction while exhibiting strong data efficiency. Code of SOV-CAD is available at: https://github.com/LukePhong/SOV-CAD

</details>


### [193] [FRFDet: Efficient UAV Small Object Detection with Symmetric Sampling and Scalable Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04125)
*Yunzhong Si,Huiying Xu,Xinzhong Zhu,Yang Liu,Yao Dong,Wenhao Zhang,Hongbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FRFDet，一种针对无人机航拍图像中小目标检测的轻量级单阶段检测器，通过逆双向采样（IBS）和尺度特征关系交叉融合（SFRCF）两个即插即用模块，在复杂天气、低光照等恶劣条件下实现SOTA性能，兼具低计算量、小参数量和快速推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍图像中的小目标检测在复杂天气、低光照和传感器噪声等恶劣条件下仍面临挑战，主要源于背景杂波严重、细粒度细节退化和次优的语义-空间特征融合，这些问题共同阻碍了鲁棒的小目标表示。

Method: 提出FRFDet轻量级单阶段检测器，包含两个即插即用模块：(1) 逆双向采样（IBS），通过通道扩展-压缩和双向模式重建保留关键空间细节，改善特征对齐；(2) 尺度特征关系交叉融合（SFRCF），显式建模尺度依赖的融合行为，发现组间逐元素乘法适合紧凑模型，而组间加法融合更适合大型架构。

Result: 在VisDrone、UAVDT、HazyDet和MS COCO数据集上的大量实验表明，FRFDet在轻量级检测器中达到SOTA性能，具有低计算成本、紧凑参数量和快速推理速度。

Conclusion: FRFDet适用于资源受限的无人机平台，有效解决了恶劣条件下小目标检测的难题。

Abstract: Small object detection in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery remains challenging under adverse conditions, including complex weather, low illumination, and sensor noise. These challenges mainly stem from severe background clutter, fine-grained detail degradation, and suboptimal semantic-spatial feature fusion, which jointly hinder robust small-object representation. To this end, we propose FRFDet, a lightweight yet effective single-stage detector tailored for UAV-based small object detection. FRFDet proposes two plug-and-play modules: Inverse Bidirectional Sampling (IBS) and Scale-Feature Relationship Cross-Fusion (SFRCF). IBS preserves critical spatial details via channel expansion-compression and bidirectional pattern reconstruction, improving feature alignment. SFRCF explicitly models scale-dependent fusion behaviors, revealing that inter-group element-wise multiplication favors compact models, while inter-group additive fusion benefits larger architectures. Extensive experiments on VisDrone, UAVDT, HazyDet, and MS COCO demonstrate that FRFDet achieves state-of-the-art performance among lightweight detectors with low computational cost, compact parameters, and fast inference, making it well suited for resource-constrained UAV platforms.

</details>


### [194] [Real-Time LiDAR Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04127)
*Seungjun Tak,Yewon Jeon,Jaeik Hwang,SukMin Hwang,Seongbo Ha,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LiDAR的实时高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过紧耦合快速G-ICP配准与球形光栅化稠密建图，在Newer College数据集上以超过20 FPS的速度实现了86.78%的F-score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的Gaussian Splatting SLAM方法利用外观信息，而LiDAR几何信息未被充分利用；同时缺乏实时、大规模场景下的LiDAR高斯泼溅SLAM框架。

Method: 1) 紧耦合快速G-ICP配准与球形光栅化稠密建图；2) 复用跟踪估计的局部协方差初始化高斯体，实现距离感知尺度；3) 推导表面法向量用于几何感知地图优化；4) 引入协方差推导的几何分数，驱动平面区域剪枝和结构丰富区域选择性稠密化；5) 优化后的高斯体与LiDAR置信度反馈提升跟踪鲁棒性。

Result: 在Newer College数据集上，纯在线轨迹下F-score达到86.78%，速度超过20 FPS；其他数据集上验证了稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 利用LiDAR几何特性设计的高斯泼溅SLAM框架能够实现实时、高精度的稠密建图与定位，且具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: We present a real-time LiDAR-based framework for Gaussian Splatting SLAM that tightly couples fast G-ICP registration with spherical rasterization-based dense mapping for large-scale sequences. Leveraging LiDAR geometry rather than appearance, we reuse tracking-estimated local covariances to initialize Gaussians with range-aware scales and to derive surface normals for geometry-aware map optimization. We further introduce a covariance-derived geometry score that measures local complexity and drives pruning in planar regions and selective densification in structurally rich areas, while optimized Gaussians and LiDAR-specific confidence cues are fed back to improve tracking robustness. On the Newer College dataset, our method achieves an F-score of 86.78\% using purely online trajectories at real-time speed ($>$20 FPS), and additional experiments on other datasets confirm its stability and scalability.

</details>


### [195] [HCSU: A Dataset and Benchmark for Fine-Grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04147)
*Yinsheng Yao,Yan Liu,Chen Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HCSU，首个面向历史书法风格细粒度理解的综合数据集，包含39,307张来自49位书法家的字符图像，区分墨迹与碑拓，提供分层专家美感描述，并揭示了当前大型视觉-语言模型在书法风格感知上的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化书法风格细粒度感知对文化遗产保护至关重要，但现有数据集存在模态混合（墨迹与碑拓混杂）和标签扁平化（缺乏层次化描述）两大问题，限制了大型视觉-语言模型的能力。

Method: 构建HCSU数据集：收集49位知名书法家的39,307张字符图像，系统分离墨迹与碑拓；提供层次化专家撰写的美感描述；设计细粒度风格判别和可解释审美推理两种评估协议。

Result: 当前最优大型视觉-语言模型在书法风格感知上表现有限：对字体、文本内容和来源线索敏感，难以将审美判断建立在细粒度笔法证据上，揭示了书法知识与视觉风格感知之间的持续差距。

Conclusion: HCSU基准暴露了当前多模态架构的根本局限性，旨在推动面向文化遗产保护的专家级视觉推理能力发展。

Abstract: Automated fine-grained perception of calligraphy styles--a task vital to cultural heritage preservation--remains a critical challenge for Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), largely constrained by existing datasets that suffer from modal mixture and flattened labels. To bridge this gap, we introduce HCSU, the first comprehensive dataset tailored for fine-grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding. HCSU comprises 39,307 meticulously curated character images from 49 historically prominent calligraphers across 10 dynasties, systematically decoupling authentic ink manuscripts (Tie) from stone rubbings (Bei) to resolve the long-standing modal mixture problem. Moving beyond conventional flattened labels, HCSU provides hierarchical expert-written aesthetic descriptions, enabling two rigorous evaluation protocols: fine-grained style discrimination and interpretable aesthetic reasoning. Extensive evaluations reveal a persistent gap between calligraphy-related knowledge and visually grounded style perception: state-of-the-art LVLMs show non-trivial performance but remain sensitive to script-level, textual, and source-specific cues, and often struggle to ground aesthetic judgments in fine-grained brushwork evidence. Ultimately, the HCSU benchmark exposes fundamental limitations in current multimodal architectures, aiming to inspire the evolution of expert-level visual reasoning for cultural heritage preservation. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tongji209/HCSU.

</details>


### [196] [Beyond Scene Priors: Fine-Grained Traffic Scene Reasoning with Benchmarking and Query-Guided Small-Object Focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04149)
*Waikit Xiu,Qiang Lu,Zian Wang,Xinjie Yang,Zhiwei Chen,Chen Sun,Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型在交通安全场景中因过度关注背景而忽视关键小目标（即“关键证据稀释”问题），本文构建了细粒度交通推理基准FGTR-Bench（40,236道选择题）并提出TSR-MLLM模型，通过在解码器边界引入文本引导的小目标聚焦图（TG-SOF），以稀疏Top-K门控残差增强问题相关视觉槽位，在不增加外部检测器或重新编码图像的前提下，在4B参数规模下达到74.1%准确率，超越最强基线2.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态大模型在处理交通安全场景中的复杂问题时，倾向于过度关注背景信息，导致关键的小目标视觉线索在视觉-语言对齐过程中被淹没（即“关键证据稀释”问题）。同时，现有VQA数据集缺乏大规模、多干扰物的细粒度评估来暴露这一缺陷。

Method: 1) 构建FGTR-Bench基准：通过多智能体生成、一致性检查和专家审核，构建40,236道单图多选题及4,947样本盲测集。2) 提出TSR-MLLM：基于Qwen3-VL-4B，在解码器边界引入查询条件化的文本引导小目标聚焦图（TG-SOF），通过稀疏Top-K门控残差增强最问题相关的视觉槽位，同时保持文本token不变，结合轻量化解码器适配，实现单次前向推理，无需外部检测器或图像重新编码。

Result: 在匹配设置下，TSR-MLLM在FGTR-Bench上以74.1%的整体准确率超越最强4B基线2.1个百分点，在证据定位路径上获得更大增益；同时在DriveQA-V (CARLA Signs)数据集上，在贪心解码下无需任务特定微调仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多模态大模型在交通安全场景中的“关键证据稀释”问题，通过构建细粒度基准和提出文本引导的小目标聚焦机制，有效提升了模型在复杂交通场景中定位和推理关键视觉证据的能力。

Abstract: In safety-critical traffic scenarios, answering complex questions relies on minute, localized visual cues. However, standard Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) tend to over-attend to backgrounds, overwhelming crucial small objects during visual-language alignment, a failure mode we term 'critical evidence dilution.' Furthermore, existing visual question answering (VQA) datasets rarely expose this flaw, as they lack large-scale, distractor-heavy evaluations that require pinpointing local evidence. To bridge this evaluation and architecture gap, we introduce the Fine-Grained Traffic Reasoning Benchmark (FGTR-Bench) and the Text-Guided Small-Object Reasoning MLLM (TSR-MLLM). FGTR-Bench comprises 40,236 single-image Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQs) created via multi-agent generation, consistency checks, and expert audits, alongside a disjoint 4,947-sample blind test split. To resolve evidence dilution, TSR-MLLM, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, uses a query-conditioned Text-Guided Small-Object Focus (TG-SOF) map. Applied once at the decoder boundary, the map adds sparse Top-K gated residuals to the most question-relevant vision slots while leaving text tokens unchanged. Together with lightweight decoder adaptation, TSR-MLLM preserves single-pass inference without external detectors or image re-encoding. Under matched settings, TSR-MLLM outperforms the strongest 4B baseline by 2.1 points on FGTR-Bench (74.1% overall), with larger gains on evidence-local tracks. Furthermore, it remains competitive on DriveQA-V (CARLA Signs) under greedy decoding without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [197] [Perceiving Better Moments: Cover Frame Reselection and Enhancement for Live Photos with the Live2K Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04151)
*Junyu Lou,Kai Chen,Weiyi You,Hui Zeng,Lei Zhang,Shuhang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文定义了Live Photo封面帧重新选择与增强（LPRE）任务，针对智能手机Live Photo中封面图与视频帧之间的质量差距问题，提出了一个统一的单阶段基线方法，整合了多帧融合、引导色彩增强和超分辨率，并构建了包含2,042张Live Photo的真实世界数据集Live2K，建立了该领域的首个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机Live Photo的封面照片和视频帧由两条不同成像管线生成，导致两者在分辨率、色彩保真度和动态范围上存在显著质量差距。当用户从视频中重新选择替代帧替换不理想的封面时，所选帧往往质量严重退化，直接替换视觉效果不佳。现有方法难以同时提升空间细节和色彩外观。

Method: 定义了LPRE任务，利用Live Photo内部的高质量封面图作为结构和色彩参考、用户重新选择的低质量帧作为重建目标、相邻视频帧提供时序线索；构建了Live2K真实世界数据集；开发了集成多帧融合、引导色彩增强和超分辨率的统一单阶段基线方法。

Result: 构建了包含2,042张Live Photo的Live2K数据集，建立了LPRE任务的第一个基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的LPRE任务和基线方法有效解决了Live Photo封面帧重新选择时的质量退化问题，为Live Photo增强研究提供了首个基准平台。

Abstract: Modern smartphones capture Live Photos, short video bursts surrounding a still image, offering a dynamic and engaging photographic experience. However, the cover photo and video components are generated by two distinct imaging pipelines: the photo stream undergoes full computational photography processing, while the video stream is constrained by real-time efficiency and heavy compression. This intrinsic separation produces a substantial quality gap in resolution, color fidelity, and dynamic range between the cover photo and video frames. When users reselect an alternative frame from the video to replace an imperfect cover, the chosen frame often suffers from severe degradation, making direct replacement visually unsatisfactory. Restoring such frames requires simultaneous enhancement of spatial detail and color appearance, a task considerably more challenging than ordinary super-resolution or color enhancement. To address this, we define the Live Photo Cover Frame Reselection and Enhancement (LPRE) task, which leverages the intrinsic cues available within each Live Photo: the high-quality cover image as a structural and color reference, the user-reselected low-quality frame as the reconstruction target and several adjacent video frames providing temporal cues. Building upon this formulation, we construct Live2K, a real-world dataset of 2,042 Live Photos, and develop a unified one-stage baseline that integrates multi-frame fusion, guided color enhancement and super-resolution, establishing the first benchmark for Live Photo enhancement research.

</details>


### [198] [SeeMe: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Effective Visual Token Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04163)
*Kai Tang,Jinhao You,Bohua Zhang,Yichen Guo,Yiding Sun,Dongxu Zhang,Chenxi Li,Xiande Huang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeeMe提出一种无训练框架，通过三阶段特征工程重构视觉token，抑制LVLM中的幻觉现象，在多个基准和模型上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM缓解幻觉的方法主要在解码阶段干预，忽略了无关或噪声视觉token误导解码过程这一关键源头。

Method: 提出训练无关的SeeMe框架，通过三阶段token工程（特征工程思想）重构视觉token，抑制幻觉源同时保留信息性视觉证据。

Result: 在MME、POPE、AMBER基准上，跨越四个LVLM模型，SeeMe一致地减少了幻觉并提升了输出一致性。

Conclusion: SeeMe从特征工程视角提供了一种新颖的、无需额外训练的LVLM幻觉缓解方法。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. However, they remain susceptible to hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the actual visual input. Existing methods primarily intervene at the decoding stage, while overlooking a critical source of hallucinations: irrelevant or noisy visual tokens that mislead the decoding process. To address this issue, we propose SeeMe, a training-free framework that introduces the concept of feature engineering from traditional machine learning into LVLMs. SeeMe restructures visual tokens through a three-stage token engineering process to suppress hallucination sources while preserving informative visual evidence. Experiments on MME, POPE, and AMBER benchmarks across four LVLMs demonstrate that SeeMe consistently reduces hallucinations and improves output consistency, providing a novel perspective for mitigating hallucinations in LVLMs.

</details>


### [199] [CritiqueDriveVLM: From Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning to Latent Thought Distillation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04179)
*Zhaohong Liu,Hao Ye,Xianlin Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CritiqueDriveVLM提出了一个三阶段框架，通过批评驱动多轮强化学习和潜在思维蒸馏，在自动驾驶VLM中实现高准确率与低延迟的平衡，将MCQ从55.54%提升到76.54%，同时将推理延迟降低88%至416ms，平均生成仅28个token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端视觉语言模型在自动驾驶中潜力巨大，但标准SFT存在推理幻觉和保守偏差问题。传统工具增强框架和CoT方法虽然缓解了问题，但产生了过高的token消耗和不可接受的延迟，难以实现实时部署。因此需要解决可靠性与效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出统一的三阶段框架：1) 批评驱动多轮强化学习，使用多维验证器提供细粒度标量反馈和多轮惩罚，内化逻辑推理能力，训练出无需外部工具的System-2 Teacher；2) 潜在思维蒸馏，通过对齐Student的潜在表示与Teacher的收敛推理状态，将深度逻辑能力压缩为快速、无CoT的System-1 Student。

Result: 在DriveLMM-01基准上，Teacher模型将MCQ从55.54%提升至76.54%；蒸馏后的Student平均生成仅28个token，推理延迟从3482ms降至416ms，降低88%。

Conclusion: CritiqueDriveVLM有效地解决了可靠性-效率权衡问题，为低延迟自动驾驶提供了一条高度鲁棒的路径，实现了高准确率与极低延迟兼得。

Abstract: End-to-end Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show immense potential in autonomous driving. However, standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) often suffers from reasoning hallucinations and conservative biases. While traditional tool-augmented frameworks and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) approaches mitigate these issues, they incur exorbitant token consumption and unacceptable latency, rendering real-time deployment impractical. To resolve this reliability-efficiency trade-off, we propose CritiqueDriveVLM, a novel unified three-stage framework internalizing reasoning directly into the VLM. First, we introduce Critique-Driven Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning (RL) guided by a multi-dimensional verifier. By providing granular scalar feedback and a multi-turn penalty, we force the policy to internalize logical deduction, cultivating a robust System-2 Teacher that achieves high accuracy without fragile external tools. Subsequently, we propose Latent Thought Distillation to overcome the latency bottleneck. By aligning the Student's latent representations with the Teacher's fully converged reasoning states, we compress deep logical capabilities into a fast, CoT-free System-1 Student. Extensive experiments on the widely-used DriveLMM-01 benchmark demonstrate remarkable improvements. Compared to the base model, our tool-free Teacher significantly boosts Multiple Choice Quality (MCQ) from 55.54% to a state-of-the-art 76.54%. Crucially, our distilled Student preserves competitive reasoning depth while drastically minimizing generation length to an average of merely 28 tokens. This slashes inference latency by 88% (from 3482 ms to 416 ms), paving a highly robust pathway for low-latency autonomous driving.Our source code is available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/CritiqueDriveVLM.

</details>


### [200] [Topology-Driven Transferability Estimation for 3D Medical Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04199)
*Jiaqi Tang,Shaoyang Zhang,Fandong Zhang,Shu Zhang,Yang Liu,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拓扑的非参数化迁移性估计框架，通过最小生成树对齐稠密特征的稀疏1-骨架图与语义标签，在114,000个3D医学体素上实现SOTA迁移性估计（加权Kendall提升0.36），且加速56倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移性估计方法依赖昂贵的微调，且图像级分类指标无法保留分割任务所需的细粒度边界和空间关系，3D医学影像的密集预测需要体素级评估。

Method: 提出拓扑驱动框架：1) 局部边界感知拓扑一致性(LBTC)通过MST泄漏率评估边界可分离性，理论上证明泄漏率是贝叶斯误差的有限样本下界；2) 全局表征拓扑散度(GRTD)评估整体解剖布局；3) 利用随机初始化解码器作为拓扑保持空间投影器稳定全局对齐；4) 任务自适应门控融合双指标。

Result: 在114,000个3D医学体素的大规模基准上，加权Kendall提升0.36，评估速度提升56倍。

Conclusion: 非参数拓扑方法有效替代了昂贵的微调选择方案，为医学视觉基础模型的迁移性评估提供了高效准确的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing number of medical vision foundation models highlights the need for effective model selection. However, mainstream selection methods rely on exhaustive fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Most of the existing Transferability Estimation (TE) metrics are primarily designed for image-level classification. They fail to preserve spatial relationships and fine-grained boundary details, which are crucial for the segmentation task. Additionally, while image-level tasks typically process a single feature vector per input, dense prediction tasks in 3D medical imaging require voxel-wise evaluation against dense annotations. To bridge these gaps, we propose a \textit{non-parametric, topology-driven} framework that estimates transferability directly from the alignment between the sparse 1-skeleton graph of dense features and semantic labels via Minimum Spanning Trees (MST). We decouple the alignment into two complementary geometric scales: Local Boundary-Aware Topological Consistency (LBTC) to assess boundary separability, where we prove that the MST leakage rate serves as a finite-sample lower bound on the Bayes error; and Global Representation Topology Divergence (GRTD) to evaluate the overall anatomical layout. Crucially, we formally justify a counterintuitive mechanism: Although without fine-tuning, the randomly initialized segmentation decoder acts as a topology-preserving spatial projector, reducing the variance of pairwise distance estimates and stabilizing global alignment evaluation. Fused via a task-adaptive gating mechanism, these dual metrics adapt to diverse clinical complexities. Evaluated on a large-scale benchmark of 114,000 3D medical volumes across diverse anatomical tasks, our topological framework achieves state-of-the-art transferability estimation with an average weighted Kendall (outperforming by 0.36) while accelerating evaluation by 56 times.

</details>


### [201] [Hierarchical Multi-to-Single-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Disruption Prediction in EAST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04241)
*Qiang Chen,Xiao Wang,Hao Si,Qingquan Yang,Meiwen Chen,Jianhua Yang,Xiaofeng Han,Yunhu Jia,Ran Chen,Liang Wang,Jin Tang,Guosheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层次化多模态到单模态知识蒸馏框架，用于EAST托卡马克等离子体破裂预测。训练时使用可见光图像和时间序列信号训练多模态教师模型，推理时仅保留时间序列学生模型，通过图结构、表示和决策三个层次的蒸馏传递多模态知识，在640次放电数据集上验证了该方法能保持多模态学习的判别优势同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体破裂对托卡马克安全构成严重威胁。现有数据驱动预测器主要依赖时间序列诊断信号，可见光图像虽能提供空间线索并提升判别能力，但显著增加了推理计算成本。为解决这一效率问题，需要一种能在保持多模态学习优势的同时降低推理开销的方法。

Method: 提出层次化多模态到单模态知识蒸馏框架：训练阶段，可见光图像和时间序列信号联合训练多模态教师模型，该模型通过Transformer编码器和原型引导的时空超图模块学习破裂前兆表示；推理阶段仅保留时间序列学生模型，通过图结构级、表示级和决策级三个层次的蒸馏传递多模态知识。

Result: 在EAST 640次放电数据集上的实验结果表明，所提框架能够保持多模态学习的判别优势，同时显著降低推理成本，为EAST高效破裂预测提供了有效途径。

Conclusion: 该层次化多模态到单模态知识蒸馏框架在保持多模态学习判别能力的同时大幅降低了推理成本，为托卡马克等离子体破裂预测提供了一条高效、实用的技术路线。代码已开源。

Abstract: Plasma disruption is a critical threat to tokamak safety. Existing data-driven predictors mainly rely on time-series diagnostic signals, while visible images provide complementary spatial cues including plasma deformation, local brightening, and radiation-structure evolution. Although the image modality improves the model's discriminative capability, it also substantially increases the computational cost during inference. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical multi-to-single-modal knowledge distillation framework for disruption prediction on a synchronized EAST multimodal dataset. During training, visible images and time-series signals are used to train a multimodal teacher, which learns disruption precursor representations through Transformer-based encoders and a prototype-guided spatiotemporal hypergraph module. During inference, only the time-series student is retained, with multimodal knowledge transferred through graph-structure-level, representation-level, and decision-level distillation. On the 640-discharge EAST dataset, the results demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve the discriminative advantages of multimodal learning while substantially reducing inference cost, and providing an effective route for efficient disruption prediction in EAST. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion.

</details>


### [202] [HeartVolMesh: Cardiac Volumetric Mesh Reconstruction via Covariance-Guided Graph Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04243)
*Fengming Lin,Arezoo Zakeri,Haoran Dou,Zherui Zhou,Shaokun Lan,Jinming Duan,Alejandro Frangi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了HeartVolMesh方法，通过将模板顶点提升为各向异性高斯核，使用3D CNN-GNN从体积图像预测位移和协方差，并通过模板变形生成具有跨病例对应关系的患者特异性四面体心脏网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割-建模管线在处理薄壁心脏结构时模糊不清，且缺乏跨病例的网格对应关系，阻碍了基于硅实验的准确模拟。

Method: 将每个模板顶点表示为各向异性高斯核，用3D CNN-GNN预测每位移和Cholesky分解参数化的协方差；训练使用协方差感知的负对数似然损失配合轻量网格正则化；体网格生成通过分阶段对齐、非刚性配准和变形传播将固定四面体模板变形到重建表面。

Result: 在表面网格精度和体网格保真度上，一致优于基于变形的基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能生成精确且具有拓扑一致性的患者特异性四面体心脏网格，且通过模板密度控制分辨率，天然保持了网格连接性和跨病例对应关系。

Abstract: Accurate patient-specific tetrahedral cardiac meshes are essential for in-silico trials, yet common segmentation-then-modelling pipelines can blur thin-wall anatomy and offer limited cross-case correspondence. We propose HeartVolMesh, which lifts each template vertex to an anisotropic Gaussian kernel and uses a 3D CNN-GNN to predict per-vertex displacements and Cholesky-parameterized covariances from volumetric images. Training is guided by a covariance-aware negative log-likelihood loss with lightweight mesh regularization. For volumetric meshing, we warp a fixed tetrahedral template to the reconstructed surface via staged alignment, non-rigid registration, and deformation propagation, preserving connectivity and correspondence by construction, with resolution controlled by template density. Experiments show consistent gains over deformation-based baselines in surface mesh accuracy and volumetric mesh fidelity.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Random Sampling: Distribution-Aware Alignment for Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04249)
*Weihao Yan,Yeqiang Qian,Yi Dong,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布对齐的高数据效率半监督医学图像分割框架，通过分布感知样本选择（结合视觉基础模型和密度K中心算法）和记忆引导复制粘贴模块（MCP）解决低数据 regime 下的表征偏差和类别不平衡问题，在极低标注比例（如1/16）下表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督医学图像分割默认随机采样，在低数据量下存在严重表征偏差，无法捕捉异质医学数据流形，导致数据效率低下。

Method: 1) 离线分布感知样本选择：利用视觉基础模型(VFMs)和设计的密度K中心算法识别代表性结构锚点，建立更具代表性的标注域；2) 记忆引导复制粘贴模块(MCP)：通过语义记忆机制检索历史一致先验进行跨域对齐，配合由易到难的渐进式训练策略抑制早期伪标签噪声。

Result: 在六个不同2D和3D数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在极低标注比例（如1/16）下取得了强分割性能。

Conclusion: 所提出框架有效解决了低数据 regime 下半监督医学图像分割的表征偏差和类别不平衡问题，显著提升了数据效率。

Abstract: Precise medical image segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning, yet relies heavily on expensive expert annotations. Semi-supervised medical image segmentation (SSMIS) offers a cost-effective solution but typically operates under the assumption of independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data, defaulting to random sampling. While statistically valid at scale, this strategy suffers from severe representation bias in low-data regimes, failing to capture the heterogeneous medical data manifold. To address this, we propose a highly data-efficient framework driven by distribution alignment. First, we introduce an offline Distribution-Aware Sample Selection strategy. By leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) and our designed Density-K-Center algorithm, we explicitly identify representative structural anchors, establishing a more representative labeled domain. Second, to bridge the remaining distribution gap, we propose the Memory-guided Copy-Paste (MCP) module. Tailored for the inherent class imbalance in medical scans, MCP leverages a semantic memory mechanism to retrieve historically consistent priors for cross-domain alignment, encouraging semantic consistency. Coupled with an easy-to-hard progressive schedule, this framework effectively mitigates early-stage pseudo-label noise. Extensive experiments on six diverse 2D and 3D datasets demonstrate strong segmentation performance, particularly in extremely low-labeled scenarios (\eg, 1/16 ratio).

</details>


### [204] [AdaptiveSplat:Texture Aware Controllable 3D Gaussian Allocation for Feed-Forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04256)
*Badrinath Singhal,Srihari K G,Sreehari Iyer,Ankit Dhiman,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用局部纹理信息显式控制高斯原语数量的前馈3D重建方法，通过纹理估计、纹理感知剪枝和自适应高斯头部三个关键组件，在不破坏前馈范式的情况下减少冗余高斯原语，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈3D重建方法预测像素对齐的高斯原语，导致高度冗余的表示。简单剪枝会引入严重伪影且需要推理时微调，破坏了前馈范式。高频区域需要更多高斯原语，而低频区域可以用更少的原语表示，因此需要一种能显式控制高斯原语数量的方法。

Method: 提出纹理感知的高斯剪枝方法，包含三个关键组件：(1) 纹理估计——捕捉场景细节的空间变化；(2) 纹理感知剪枝——从低频区域移除冗余高斯原语；(3) 自适应高斯头部——在保持前馈范式的前提下预测保留原语的修正属性。

Result: 在RE10K、ACID、DL3DV、Tanks and Temples以及DTU等多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性，消融实验证实了各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部纹理信息显式控制高斯原语数量，在减少冗余的同时保持了前馈范式的效率，实现了高质量的重建结果。

Abstract: Current feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods predict pixel aligned Gaussian primitives, resulting in highly redundant representations. A natural solution is to prune the redundant Gaussians, but naive pruning introduces severe artifacts and often requires inference time fine-tuning, breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Based on previous works, high frequency regions require more Gaussian primitives, while low frequency regions can be represented with significantly fewer primitives. Motivated by this, we propose a novel approach to explicitly control the number of Gaussians by leveraging local texture information. Our approach achieves this through three key components: (1) texture estimation to capture spatial variation in scene detail, (2) texture-aware pruning that removes redundant Gaussians from low frequency regions, and (3) an adaptive Gaussian head that predicts the modified attributes of the retained primitives without breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Experiments on RE10K, ACID, DL3DV, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, while ablation studies validate the contributions of its key components.

</details>


### [205] [EMPURPLE: A Free Lunch for Diffusion Distillation based on the Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04276)
*Zilai Li,Lujia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文分析了扩散蒸馏模型FID退化的原因，提出了一种无需训练的EMPURPLE方法，通过重用原始模型的中间潜在变量，将FID提升7%-20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理成本高，扩散蒸馏虽能减少采样步数，但许多蒸馏模型（如SDXL-Lightning、DMD）的FID指标显著退化，亟需理解其原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 通过PAC风格泛化界分析，发现激进的早期速度场重定向导致蒸馏目标难以学习，扩大训练-测试差距，造成中间噪声潜变量的分布不匹配。据此提出EMPURPLE：一种无需训练的、模型无关的方法，通过重用原始模型采样的中间潜在变量来缓解分布漂移。

Result: EMPURPLE在DMD2、Hyper-SD、FlashSD、SDXL-Lightning等多个蒸馏模型上实现FID提升7%-20%。

Conclusion: 蒸馏模型的FID退化源于早期步的分布不匹配，EMPURPLE通过简单有效的中间变量重用法可显著提升生成多样性，且无需重新训练。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve impressive image-generation quality but remain expensive at inference time. Diffusion distillation reduces sampling steps, yet many distilled models, including SDXL-Lightning and distribution matching distillation methods, suffer from degraded Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). We analyze this phenomenon through a PAC-style generalization bound. Our analysis suggests that aggressive early-step redirection of the velocity field makes the distillation target harder to learn, enlarging the train-test gap. As a result, early-step output distributions differ between training and inference, causing distribution mismatch in the intermediate noisy latent used as next-step inputs. We empirically validate this mechanism by showing reduced diversity in both intermediate features and final outputs. To address this issue, we propose EMPURPLE, a simple training-free method that recycles intermediate latents sampled from the original model. EMPURPLE is model-agnostic and improves FID by 7\% to 20\% across DMD2, Hyper-SD, FlashSD, and SDXL-Lightning. The repo is: https://github.com/TheLovesOfLadyPurple/Empurple-Training-Free-Algorithm-To-enhance-Diversity-of-The-Diffusion-Distillation-Model

</details>


### [206] [AquaStereo: Enabling Underwater Stereo Matching via Depth-Conditioned Diffusion and Geometry Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04303)
*Qizhe Wei,Yingping Liang,Shaodi You,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AquaStereo提出了一个结合数据模拟流程和自我蒸馏策略的框架，通过深度条件扩散管道生成水下立体对，并利用可学习感知帧融合时域线索与语义特征来增强特征稳定性，显著提升了水下环境中的立体匹配鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的立体匹配模型在水下环境中面临数据稀缺和特征退化两大挑战：一是缺乏领域内训练数据，二是水下图像的退化特性导致难以提取判别性对应关系。

Method: (1) 深度条件扩散管道渲染水下立体对并保持双目几何结构，结合轻量级左右一致性模块确保几何对齐；(2) 冻结的陆地双目教师通过阶段加权序列损失和学生伪目标共享进行自蒸馏；(3) 引入可学习感知帧，融合视频骨干提取的辅助视图时域线索与图像编码器提取的语义特征，构建鲁棒匹配描述子。

Result: 在挑战性水下场景中，AquaStereo显著提升了鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: AquaStereo通过数据模拟和自我蒸馏策略有效解决了水下立体匹配中的数据稀缺和特征退化问题，为水下环境中的立体匹配提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Learning-based stereo matching models struggle in underwater environments due to scarce in-domain data and the difficulty of extracting discriminative correspondences from degraded imagery. In this work, we present $\textbf{AquaStereo}$, a perception-enhanced framework with a data simulation pipeline and a self-distillation strategy that jointly address data scarcity and feature degradation in underwater stereo matching. First, a depth-conditioned diffusion pipeline renders underwater stereo pairs while preserving binocular geometry, with a lightweight left-right consistency module ensuring geometric alignment. Training on this synthetic corpus effectively narrows the terrestrial-underwater gap and improves zero-shot robustness. Second, a frozen binocular teacher trained on clean terrestrial pairs guides a student exposed to rendered underwater pairs with perturbations. A stage-weighted sequence loss is performed to align the student's disparities with the teacher's geometry, while a clean-branch supervision with shared pseudo targets prevents scale drift. To further enhance feature stability under turbidity and low texture, we introduce learnable perception frames, a perception-enhanced feature formulation that constructs robust matching descriptors by fusing temporal cues from two auxiliary views encoded by a video backbone with semantic features extracted by a strong image encoder. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $\textbf{AquaStereo}$ substantially improves robustness and zero-shot generalization in challenging underwater scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/qz-wei/AquaStereo.

</details>


### [207] [Road-Aware Anomaly Segmentation with Query-Guided Polygons and CLIP in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04304)
*Zhiran Yan,Gordon Elger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、后处理的道路异常分割框架，基于mask transformer分割网络，利用查询级掩码置信度和多边形道路先验检测异常区域，并通过CLIP零样本语义过滤抑制假阳性，无需重新训练、OOD数据或辅助监督，在多个基准测试上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统语义分割模型在封闭集假设下无法识别未知或意外目标，在自动驾驶开放环境中存在安全隐患，常误分类或忽略分布外道路异常。

Method: 基于mask transformer分割网络，利用查询级掩码置信度推导多边形道路先验检测间隙异常区域；引入CLIP零样本语义过滤模块，使用分布内提示和可选广义OOD提示抑制假阳性；无需重新训练、OOD数据或辅助监督。

Result: 在Fishyscapes、SMIYC和RoadAnomaly三个基准上表现优异，在多数指标上超越训练无关基线Maskomaly，在Fishyscapes LostAndFound上取得最高AP。

Conclusion: 该框架具有实用性和可部署性，适用于真实世界自动驾驶系统中的异常检测。

Abstract: Traditional semantic segmentation models operate under a closed-set assumption and struggle to recognize unknown or unexpected objects-an essential capability for autonomous driving. As a result, such models often misclassify or overlook out-of-distribution (OOD) road anomalies, posing safety risks in open-world environments. We present a lightweight, postprocessing, road-aware anomaly segmentation framework that requires no retraining, no OOD data, and no auxiliary supervision. Our approach builds on a mask transformer-based segmentation network by exploiting query-level mask confidence and deriving a polygonal road prior to detect gap regions that may correspond to anomalies. To further suppress false positives, we introduce a CLIP-based zero-shot semantic filtering module using in-distribution prompts, with optional generalized OOD prompts. By jointly leveraging spatial priors and semantic verification, our framework produces robust and interpretable anomaly predictions. Evaluation on three public benchmarks-Fishyscapes, SMIYC, and RoadAnomaly-shows consistently strong performance. In particular, our method outperforms the training-free baseline Maskomaly on most metrics and achieves the highest AP on Fishyscapes LostAndFound. These results demonstrate the practicality and deployability of our approach for real-world autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [208] [Aura: Consistent Multi-Subject Video Generation via VLM-Grounded Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04311)
*Zixiang Zhou,Zhentao Yu,Yifeng Ma,Hongmei Wang,Wenqing Yu,Cong Wang,Zilin Yang,Rui Chen,Jiarong Ou,Yezhou Liu,Yuan Zhou,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Aura统一框架，通过AI导演级描述、VLM特征提取、两阶段对齐、主题感知RoPE-Shift和渐进式APG等机制，实现高保真、身份一致的单主体和多元素视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以保持身份一致性，且无法建模多主体间的复杂关系。

Method: 1) AI导演级密集结构化描述；2) VLM+可学习查询提取多模态语义特征；3) 两阶段对齐策略将VLM特征映射到DiT空间；4) token拼接注入视觉参考；5) 主题感知RoPE-Shift机制减少伪影；6) 主题感知可学习token区分不同主体类型；7) Memory Tokens平衡训练信号；8) 渐进式APG缓解过饱和问题；9) 高质量数据构建流水线。

Result: 在单主体生成和更具挑战性的多主体场景中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: Aura通过多组件协同设计，有效解决了视频生成中的身份一致性和多主体关系建模难题，验证了统一框架的优越性。

Abstract: Subject-driven and multi-element video generation are central to controllable video synthesis, but existing methods still struggle to preserve identity consistency and model complex relationships among multiple subjects. In this paper, we propose Aura, a unified framework for high-fidelity and identity-consistent video generation. To better capture scene dynamics and subject interactions, we introduce AI director-level captions that provide dense and structured descriptions of video content. We further leverage a vision-language model (VLM) with learnable queries to extract multimodal semantic features from textual and visual references, covering both global semantics and fine-grained visual cues. To bridge the representational gap between the VLM and the Diffusion Transformer (DiT), we design a two-stage alignment strategy that progressively maps VLM features into the DiT feature space. For visual conditioning, we adopt token concatenation to inject reference information directly into the generation process. To distinguish heterogeneous subject types and reduce common copy-paste artifacts, we develop a subject-aware RoPE-Shift mechanism. To further differentiate reference images of different categories, we introduce subject-aware learnable tokens. In addition, we introduce Memory Tokens to balance the training signal across examples with different numbers of reference subjects. During inference, Progressive-APG (Adaptive Prompt Guidance) further alleviates oversaturation and improves semantic alignment with user prompts. Finally, we build a high-quality video-subject image dataset through a dedicated data construction pipeline. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on both single-subject generation and more challenging multi-element scenarios.

</details>


### [209] [Framework and Multi-modal Dataset for Roadwork Zone Detection and Geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04330)
*Zhiran Yan,Yutong Xin,S Shyam Shenoi,Rui Song,Gordon Elger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RZDG数据集（含模拟与真实数据）及基于AB3DMOT扩展的追踪式道路施工区检测与地理定位管道，在1米精度阈值下实现了真实数据Precision 0.565/Recall 0.898/F1 0.597和模拟数据Precision 0.615/Recall 0.809/F1 0.665的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高清地图更新频率低且缺乏半静态信息（如临时施工区），现有公开数据集缺失导致道路施工区检测与地理定位研究进展受限，亟需标准化评估基准。

Method: 构建RZDG多模态数据集（模拟+真实数据，含语义分割、3D检测、地理定位标注）；提出基于AB3DMOT扩展的追踪式检测与地理定位管道，实现局部坐标系到全局坐标系的坐标变换。

Result: 在1米定位精度阈值下：真实数据Precision 0.565/Recall 0.898/F1 0.597；模拟数据Precision 0.615/Recall 0.809/F1 0.665。

Conclusion: 所提管道能有效检测道路施工区并实现精确的全局地理定位，填补了该领域数据集与基准方法的空白。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles often rely on high-definition (HD) maps for navigation; however, these maps are not frequently updated and often lack semi-static information, such as temporary roadwork zones, which can significantly alter the road network. This limitation underscores the urgent need for an accurate global position of roadwork zones. However, the absence of publicly available datasets for evaluating roadwork zone detection and geo-localization models has hindered the development of reliable autonomous driving systems. To address this challenge, we propose the Roadwork Zone Detection and Geo-localization (RZDG) dataset, which includes both simulated and real-world data, providing multimodal sensor inputs along with comprehensive annotations. The dataset supports multiple perception tasks, including image semantic segmentation, 3D object detection, and object geo-localization. In addition, we introduce a tracker-based roadwork zone detection and geo-localization (RZDG) pipeline, an extension of AB3DMOT, for accurate object geo-localization in roadwork zones. We benchmark our approach on the RZDG dataset, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting roadwork zones and transforming object positions from the local coordinate system to the global coordinate system. A prediction is considered a true positive (TP) if its estimated position falls within one meter of the ground truth. Our experimental results show that our approach achieves high accuracy on both real and simulated data. Specifically, we report: Precision: 0.565 (real) / 0.615 (simulated) Recall: 0.898 (real) / 0.809 (simulated) F1-score: 0.597 (real) / 0.665 (simulated).

</details>


### [210] [IRIS: An Intelligent Vision-Language System for Ocular Surface Diseases via Topic Tree and Scene-Driven VQA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04344)
*Hao Wei,Wenjin Qi,Dasen Dai,Minqing Zhang,Wu Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对眼表疾病(OSD)领域缺乏高质量多模态指令微调数据的问题，本文提出IRIS系统，通过构建最大规模OSD VQA数据集IRIS-120K，并设计双分支数据生成范式（主题发现树+场景驱动策略）注入临床先验知识，使4B参数VLM在OSD理解任务上全面超越包括34B在内的通用及专用医学VLM，证明结构化知识注入比单纯扩大参数规模更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在眼表疾病等专业领域临床推理能力严重不足，根本原因在于缺乏高质量、多模态的指令微调数据。现有图像-文本对存在语义浅层化问题，无法支撑细粒度医学理解。

Method: 1) 构建IRIS-120K数据集，是目前最大最全面的OSD VQA数据集；2) 提出双分支协同数据生成范式：主题发现树(TFT)层次化锚定视觉特征到解剖病理概念，场景驱动策略合成角色自适应临床对话；3) 在结构化数据上微调4B参数VLM。

Result: 4B参数的IRIS在OSD理解任务上取得最先进性能，全面超越包括34B参数在内的通用及专用医学VLM。

Conclusion: 结构化知识注入比单纯扩大参数规模更能有效提升模型在专业领域的表现，为在移动边缘设备上实现资源高效、专家级的可扩展OSD筛查提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate remarkable generic capabilities, their clinical reasoning in specialized domains like ocular surface diseases (OSDs) is severely hindered by a paucity of high-fidelity, multimodal instruction-tuning data. To dismantle this data bottleneck, we introduce IRIS, an Intelligent Recognition and Interaction System tailored for fine-grained OSD understanding via external eye photography. First, we curate IRIS-120K, the largest and most comprehensive OSD visual question-answering (VQA) dataset to date. Crucially, to overcome the semantic shallowness of conventional image-caption pairs, we propose a synergistic data generation paradigm to explicitly inject clinical priors. Our data engine operates via a dual-branch framework: 1) a Topic Finding Tree (TFT) that hierarchically anchors visual features to precise anatomical and pathological concepts, enforcing rigorous medical deduction logic; and 2) a Scene-driven strategy that synthesizes role-adaptive clinical dialogues to ensure pragmatic generalization. By explicitly aligning a compact 4B-parameter VLM on this structurally enriched corpus, IRIS achieves state-of-the-art performance, comprehensively outperforming both generalist and specialized medical VLMs with up to 34B parameters. Our findings underscore that structured knowledge injection profoundly prevails over sheer parameter scaling, unlocking the potential for resource-efficient, expert-level AI deployment on mobile edge devices for scalable OSD screening. Code, datasets, and model weights will be publicly released by this repo.

</details>


### [211] [Last-Meter Precision Navigation for UAVs: A Diffusion-Refined Aerial Visual Servoing Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04352)
*Yaxuan Li,Jiarui Zeng,Shaofei Huang,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamNav提出了一种粗到精的扩散细化空中视觉伺服框架，通过三角函数参数化粗估计旋转，再利用预训练世界模型进行视觉想象精调轨迹，在PairUAV基准（480万图像对，72场景）上实现了优于现有方法的精度和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机最后10米精度导航面临尺度模糊、旋转不连续和细粒度空间推理的挑战，现有方法在大视角变化下容易失败且泛化到未见环境的能力不足。

Method: 提出DreamNav框架：1) 粗估计阶段采用三角函数参数化的鲁棒回归策略，联合建模正弦和余弦分量预测旋转；2) 扩散精化阶段利用预训练世界模型模拟候选动作的未来视觉观测，选择与目标视觉差异最小的轨迹。

Result: 在PairUAV基准（480万图像对，72场景）上，DreamNav在精度和泛化能力上优于强基线方法，并实现了零样本迁移到未见场景。

Conclusion: 提出的粗到精扩散细化框架有效解决了单目视觉下的末端精度导航问题，三角函数参数化缓解了角度周期性的优化不稳定，基于世界模型的视觉想象实现了更好的轨迹选择。

Abstract: In this work, we study the last-meter precision navigation for UAVs, e.g., autonomously reaching a target within the final 10 meters using monocular vision. This task is challenging due to scale ambiguity, rotation discontinuities, and the need for fine-grained spatial reasoning. Existing methods often fail under large viewpoint changes or lack generalization to unseen environments. To this end, we propose DreamNav, a coarse-to-fine diffusion-refined aerial visual servoing framework. In the first coarse-estimation stage, a robust regression policy employs a trigonometric parameterization to predict rotation by jointly modeling sine and cosine components, effectively mitigating optimization instabilities caused by angular periodicity. Given this coarse estimate, the second diffusion-refined stage utilizes a pre-trained world model to simulate future visual observations for candidate actions, selecting the trajectory that minimizes visual discrepancy with the target through a process of visual imagination. To support rigorous evaluation, we contribute PairUAV, a large-scale benchmark comprising 4.8 million image pairs across 72 scenes, curated from the University-1652 dataset. Extensive experiments show DreamNav outperforms strong visual servoing and foundation model baselines in accuracy and generalization, with zero-shot transfer to unseen scenes.

</details>


### [212] [HASSL: Hierarchy-Aware Self-Supervised Learning Framework for Single Cell Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04353)
*Julius Riel,Vishwa Mohan Singh,Sai Anirudh Aryasomayajula,Anuun Chinbat,Hannes Leonhard,Moritz Ladenburger,Frederik Alexander,Vishisht Choudhary,Fabio Laredo,Giacomo Masserdotti,Thorben Prein,Carsten Marr,Amirhossein Kardoost*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种层级感知的自监督学习框架，通过结合分割教师蒸馏和基于HDBSCAN的层级对比损失，解决自监督模型抑制细胞图像中细粒度层级结构的问题。在230万单细胞、208个类别、20个数据集的语料上，该方法在top-K准确率、层级检索和药物分类F1分数上分别提升2.8%、6.3%和7.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞图像中天然存在层级结构（细粒度形态→粗粒度语义组），但现有自监督学习模型往往过度强调成像模态等粗粒度因素，忽略了细粒度形态特征，导致潜在空间中层级信息被抑制。

Method: 1) 分割教师蒸馏框架：增强潜在空间对形态特征的感知能力；2) 基于HDBSCAN的层级对比损失：在层次结构的不同层级上优化决策边界，使嵌入对齐语义和形态线索。

Result: 在230万单细胞、208个类别、20个数据集的语料上，平均top-K准确率提升2.8%，最深层级数据集的top-9检索提升6.3%，药物分类下游F1分数提升7.8%。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效缓解了自监督学习对粗粒度因素的过度强调，生成了具有生物学意义的细粒度形态子簇，在多个评价指标上优于基线方法。

Abstract: Hierarchical structure is common in image data, where fine-grained clusters often merge into larger, coarser semantic groups. In biological cell images, current self-supervised learning models often suppress this hierarchy, as coarse factors such as imaging modality can obscure finer morphological attributes in the latent space. We propose a hierarchy-aware self-supervised training framework to address this problem. Our method combines two components: a distillation framework with a segmentation teacher to improve morphological awareness in the latent space, and a hierarchy-aware contrastive loss based on HDBSCAN to improve decision boundaries between closely related subtypes at different hierarchical levels. Together, these components reduce the tendency of self-supervised learning to overemphasize coarse factors and instead align embeddings with semantic and morphological cues. This yields biologically meaningful sub-clusters driven by fine morphological detail. We train and evaluate our method on a curated corpus of 2.3 million single cells aggregated from 20 microscopy datasets, both labeled and unlabeled, covering 208 cell classes. Our method improves over baseline and counterpart methods, increasing average top-K accuracy by 2.8%, top-9 retrieval on the dataset with the deepest hierarchy by 6.3%, and downstream F1-score for biologically relevant drug classification from perturbed cell morphology by 7.8%.

</details>


### [213] [Event Detection in Videos: A Framework for the Development of New Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04372)
*Anastasia Zakharova,Thierry Bouwmans,Anthony Cioppa,Adrien Deliège,Antonio Greco,Anaïs Halin,Kamil Jeziorek,Meghna Kapoor,Tomasz Kryjak,Islam Osman,Sébastien Piérard,Carlo Sansone,Mohamed S. Shehata,Renaud Vandeghen,Marc Van Droogenbroeck,Bruno Vento*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于数据集、性能评估和部署场景三大支柱的严谨框架，用于规范视频事件检测方法的开发与比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频事件检测方法众多，但缺乏大规模数据集和严格的性能评估方法，导致算法比较存在偏差，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 提出了一个三维框架，包含：(1) 数据集规范；(2) 性能评估标准；(3) 方法部署场景。

Result: 提供了一个系统化的框架，帮助研究者在丰富的现有方法中正确定位自己的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架有助于推动视频事件检测领域的规范化发展，促进方法间的公平比较和实际应用。

Abstract: Event detection tasks in videos, the most important aspect of video surveillance, aim to detect events either at the pixel-level, frame-level, or clip-level. Plenty of methods intended for event detection in different environments, for various applications, and within different acquisition techniques were introduced. Naturally, the attempts were made as well to classify these algorithms in terms of detection of performance or in terms of real-time abilities. Nevertheless, the lack of a large-scale dataset as well as rigorous performance evaluation methods have biased such comparisons as well as the development of the methods.
  Given the diversity of existing approaches, we believe it is essential for researchers to position their work within such a rich landscape. Thus, we propose a rigorous framework for developing new methods in event detection for videos. Specifically, this framework is based on three main pillars: datasets, performance evaluation, and scenarios for deploying methods.

</details>


### [214] [The Good, the Bad, and the Brittle: Benchmarking Robustness and Generalisation of Histopathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04401)
*Dhyey Yajnik,Amina Asif,Fayyaz Minhas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过REET工具箱和NR-Kfold验证协议，系统评估了12种病理基础模型（PFMs）在临床相关扰动下的鲁棒性和泛化能力，发现PFMs优于CNN但参数规模扩大带来的收益递减，提出未来应优先关注数据质量、多模态信息和域对齐而非单纯增加参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型（PFMs）的鲁棒性和泛化能力尚未被系统评估，且参数规模扩大是否已达到极限尚不明确，亟需建立统一的评估框架以指导下一代模型发展。

Method: 使用Robustness Evaluation and Enhancement Toolbox (REET)对12种PFMs和ResNet基线进行11种临床现实扰动的基准测试；提出Perturbation Performance Index (PPI)汇总扰动扫描下的精度趋势；采用非冗余K折交叉验证（NR-Kfold）协议破坏训练-测试相似性以评估分布偏移。

Result: PFMs在鲁棒性和域泛化上一致优于CNN；模型规模扩大呈现收益递减，中等规模模型（如UNI2/Virchow-2）的韧性达到或超过更大模型；NR-Kfold分析显示打破训练-测试相似性会导致系统性精度损失和变异性增加。

Conclusion: 下一代病理基础模型应将数据质量、多模态信息和域对齐置于参数数量之上，才能实现真正的临床可靠性。

Abstract: How robust and generalisable are pathology foundation models and have their scaling limites been reached? We benchmarked twelve pathology foundation models (PFMs) and ResNet baselines using our Robustness Evaluation and Enhancement Toolbox (REET) across eleven clinically realistic perturbations and a dissimilarity-driven Non-Redundant K-fold validation (NR-Kfold) protocol. We introduce a Perturbation Performance Index (PPI) to summarise accuracy trends under controlled perturbation sweeps and analyse robustness scaling with parameter count. We show that PFMs consistently outperform CNNs in both robustness and domain generalisation, yet model scaling shows diminishing returns: mid-sized models such (UNI2/Virchow-2 etc.) achieve comparable or greater resilience than larger systems. NR-Kfold analysis further reveals systematic accuracy loss and increased variability when training-test similarity is broken, underscoring the need for explicit distribution-shift evaluation. These findings suggest that the next generation of pathology foundation models must prioritise data quality, multimodality information and domain alignment over parameter count to achieve genuine clinical reliability.

</details>


### [215] [Transferability Between Understanding and Generation in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04423)
*Jiwon Kang,Heeji Yoon,Jaewoo Jung,Jaewon Min,Minkyeong Jeon,Biyeon Hwang,Sangwon Jung,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了统一多模态模型中跨任务迁移性，发现全共享Transformer架构能实现理解与生成任务间的正向迁移，并提出通过训练理解任务来间接提升生成性能的策略，避免了直接微调生成任务带来的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索统一多模态模型中图像理解与图像生成两个任务之间是否存在能力的迁移性，以及如何利用这种迁移性来改善模型训练效率与效果。

Method: 通过控制变量实验比较不同架构设计（全共享Transformer骨干 vs. 松散耦合设计）下的跨任务迁移效果；提出利用理解任务训练来间接提升生成任务能力的训练策略。

Result: 全共享Transformer骨干与统一视觉编码器的模型表现出一致的跨任务迁移，而松散耦合设计几乎没有迁移效果；提出的间接训练策略在计数、空间关系、文本识别/生成三个能力上均有效提升了生成性能同时减少了分布偏移。

Conclusion: 跨任务迁移性可以在统一多模态模型中被系统性利用，通过理解任务训练来提升生成能力是一种有效的训练策略。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) integrate image understanding and generation within a single architecture, yet how the two tasks interact remains understudied. We investigate $\boldsymbol{\mathsf{transferability}}$ in UMMs: whether training a capability on one task improves the same capability on the other without explicit supervision. Through controlled experiments, we empirically find that transferability depends on architecture-models with fully shared transformer backbone and a unified visual encoder exhibit consistent cross-task transfer, while loosely coupled designs show little or none. Leveraging this transferability, we propose a practical training strategy. The most straightforward way to improve a target generative capability (e.g., counting) is to fine-tune generation directly, but this can degrade visual quality due to distribution shift. Instead, we train the corresponding understanding task and let it transfer into generation, which improves capability-specific generative performance while minimizing distribution shift. We validate this across three capabilities-counting, spatial relation, and text recognition/generation-showing that cross-task transferability can be systematically exploited in UMMs.

</details>


### [216] [ResearchStudio-Reel: Automate the Last Mile of Research from Paper to Poster, Video, and Blog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04438)
*Lingao Xiao,Yalun Dai,Yangyu Huang,Qihao Zhao,Wenshan Wu,Hugo He,Ruishuo Chen,Jin Jiang,Qianli Ma,Jiahuan Zhang,Xin Zhang,Ying Xin,Yang Ou,Yan Xia,Scarlett Li,Longbo Huang,Zhipeng Zhang,Yang He,Yap Kim Hui,Yan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ResearchStudio-Reel，一种将论文自动转化为海报、视频、博客等可编辑传播物件的组合式技能框架，通过共享提取器、可编辑生成器和交互聚合层实现高质量、可往返编辑的多模态论文传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文传播（将论文转化为海报、演讲视频、博客等）仍是耗时的手动工作。现有自动化方法独立处理每种传播形式，重复提取论文信息，生成单向渲染结果（无法在PowerPoint或Word中重新编辑），且质量受限于软VLM偏好分数，导致关键部分内容空洞。

Method: 将论文传播构建为技能组合：1) 共享提取器Paper2Assets一次性提取论文为可复用包；2) 三个可编辑生成器（Paper2Poster、Paper2Video、Paper2Blog）产生事实一致且可往返编辑（PowerPoint/Word）的产物；3) 交互聚合层Paper2Reel将所有产物绑定为自包含HTML查看器，支持节级点击联动视频、幻灯片、字幕和博客。

Result: 在Paper2Poster基准上，海报在美学和信息子标准上均领先于先前自动化系统和前沿LLM，在两位VLM评审下美学指标超越作者原版海报，84%-93%的论文综合胜出。能力审计表明，配备旁白对齐的幻灯片高亮和布局感知DOCX修复的双语博客，是唯一能同时交付三种可编辑产物的管线。

Conclusion: 将论文传播构建为基于薄代理合约（thin agent-readable contracts）的技能组合，通过共享提取、可编辑生成和硬通过/失败渲染门控，能够替代手动传播工作，实现高质量、可往返编辑的多模态论文展示。

Abstract: Research dissemination, turning a paper into a poster, a talk video, and a blog post, is still a manual last mile. Prior automation treats each artifact in isolation that each re-extract the paper from scratch, usually ship one-way renders the author cannot reopen in PowerPoint or Word, and gates quality on soft VLM-preference scores that plateau while load-bearing sections still read as empty. We argue this last mile is best built as a composition of skills: thin agent-readable contracts that share one upstream extractor and wrap deterministic primitives in a measured-fill loop whose exits are hard pass/fail render gates. We instantiate this as ResearchStudio-Reel, five Claude Code and Codex skills organized into one shared extractor (Paper2Assets), three editable generators (Paper2Poster, Paper2Video, Paper2Blog), and one interactive convergence layer (Paper2Reel). Paper2Assets extracts each paper once into a shared bundle that can be reused by every downstream skill; The three generators produce a print-ready poster, a synchronized talk video, and a bilingual blog that stay factually consistent and round-trip through PowerPoint or Word; Paper2Reel then binds all three into a self-contained HTML viewer whose section-level clicks jump the video, slides, captions, and blog to matching content. On the Paper2Poster benchmark, our posters lead every aesthetic and information sub-criterion against both prior automated systems and single-shot frontier LLMs, surpassing the authors' own on aesthetics under two held-out VLM judges and winning overall on 84% to 93% of papers; capability audits further show that, by uniquely pairing narration-aligned on-slide highlights with a bilingual blog gated by layout-aware DOCX repair, ResearchStudio-Reel is the only pipeline to ship all three editable artifacts. Project is available at https://aka.ms/ResearchStudio

</details>


### [217] [Wan-Streamer v0.2: Higher Resolution, Same Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04443)
*Lianghua Huang,Zhi-Fan Wu,Yupeng Shi,Wei Wang,Mengyang Feng,Junjie He,Chen-Wei Xie,Yu Liu,Jingren Zhou,Ang Wang,Bang Zhang,Baole Ai,Chen Liang,Cheng Yu,Chongyang Zhong,Jinwei Qi,Kai Zhu,Pandeng Li,Peng Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Xinhua Cheng,Yitong Huang,Yun Zheng,Yuxiang Bao,Yuzheng Wang,Zoubin Bi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wan-Streamer v0.2 在保持约200ms模型端信号到信号延迟的前提下，将交互式输出流分辨率从192x336提升至640x368，通过思考者-执行者架构分离设计，在单GPU低延迟路径处理感知和缓存构建，多GPU执行者组通过Ulysses上下文并行处理高分辨率视频生成，实现总远程交互延迟约550ms（含350ms双向网络预算）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持低延迟的前提下提升音视频交互模型输出流的分辨率，使实时对话中场景锚定的中景智能体的姿态、视线、手部、附近物体和局部场景布局保持清晰可辨。

Method: 采用思考者-执行者架构分离：思考者在单GPU上执行低延迟的流式感知、短语言/状态Transformer生成缓存及最终解码；执行者为多GPU Ulysses风格上下文并行组，负责昂贵的下一单元隐变量生成，视频潜变量序列跨GPU分片去噪，音频潜变量序列不分片。

Result: 输出流分辨率从192x336提升至640x368，模型端信号到信号延迟保持在约200ms（25FPS），总远程交互延迟约550ms（含350ms双向网络预算）。

Conclusion: 通过思考者-执行者架构分离和Ulysses上下文并行，在保持低延迟的同时有效提升了音视频交互模型的分辨率，将额外硬件集中在视觉生成上，保持紧凑的思考者-执行者边界。

Abstract: We present Wan-Streamer v0.2, a latency-preserving upgrade of the native-streaming, end-to-end audio-visual interaction model. v0.2 keeps the v0.1 modeling formulation, but raises the interactive output stream from 192x336 to 640x368 while preserving approximately 200 ms model-side signal-to-signal latency at 25 FPS. The higher-resolution stream supports scene-grounded mid-shot agents whose posture, gaze, hands, nearby objects, and local scene layout remain legible during real-time conversation. To support the larger visual stream without adding user-visible delay, v0.2 keeps the thinker as a single-GPU low-latency path for streaming perception, the short language/state Transformer pass that builds the generation cache, and final decoding. The performer becomes a multi-GPU Ulysses-style context-parallel group for the expensive next-unit latent generation. Each performer rank writes incoming K/V into a pre-sharded local cache. The long high-resolution latent video sequence is split across ranks for denoising and gathered through Ulysses communication, while the much shorter audio latent sequence is generated without sequence sharding. In this split, the thinker's language/state computation reaches the performer only as K/V conditioning, so no separate language sequence has to be communicated inside the performer group. This concentrates additional hardware on visual generation while preserving the compact thinker-performer boundary, keeping total remote interaction latency at approximately 550 ms when a 350 ms bidirectional network budget is included.

</details>


### [218] [Fields of the Planet: Field Boundary Mapping Beyond 10m](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04449)
*Isaac Corley,Caleb Robinson,Jennifer Marcus,Hannah Kerner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Fields of the Planet (FTP)数据集，基于3米分辨率PlanetScope影像，与原有的10米Sentinel-2数据集FTW配对，覆盖24个国家。实验表明，3米影像相比10米影像，在农田地块分割的PQ从21.0提升至35.5，小地块PQ从5.8提升至15.7，边界误差从18.6米降至7.4米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多小农户地块仅覆盖几个10米Sentinel-2像素，导致地块边界提取精度不足。已有FTW数据集基于10米分辨率，对小地块的描绘能力有限，亟需更高分辨率的数据集以改善小地块的场界测绘。

Method: 构建FTP数据集：与FTW采用相同的多边形、季节性窗口和训练/测试划分，但使用3米PlanetScope影像，包含133,168个配准的patch-window目标，覆盖24个国家。评估时先对预测结果进行矢量化，再计算全景质量（PQ）、对象F1、按大小分层的PQ以及米级匹配边界误差。

Result: 在相同架构和训练配置下，3米影像相比10米影像：PQ从21.0提升至35.5；0.5公顷以下小地块PQ从5.8提升至15.7；匹配边界误差从18.6米降至7.4米。

Conclusion: 3米分辨率PlanetScope影像显著提升了农田地块边界测绘精度，尤其对小农户地块效果明显，为作物监测、灌溉规划和产量估算提供了更可靠的基础数据支持。

Abstract: Field-boundary maps support crop monitoring, irrigation planning, and yield estimation, but many smallholder parcels span only a few 10 m Sentinel-2 pixels. We introduce Fields of the Planet (FTP), a 3 m PlanetScope companion to Fields of The World (FTW) that pairs the same polygons, seasonal windows, and train/test splits with 133,168 co-registered PlanetScope patch-window targets across 24 countries. FTP evaluates field delineation as parcel recovery by vectorizing predictions before scoring panoptic quality (PQ), object F1, size-stratified PQ, and meter-scale matched-boundary error. Under matched architectures and training recipes, 3 m imagery raises PQ from 21.0 to 35.5, raises PQ on sub-0.5 ha fields from 5.8 to 15.7, and cuts matched-boundary error from 18.6 m to 7.4 m.

</details>


### [219] [CCFM: Collision-Constrained Flow Matching for Safety-Critical Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04451)
*Ke Li,Kaidi Liang,Yuxin Ding,Debojyoti Biswas,Xianbiao Hu,Ruwen Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CCFM框架，通过硬物理约束的流匹配采样器，实现可控碰撞类型的安全关键场景生成，在nuScenes和nuPlan上分别达到46.4%和83.1%的碰撞率，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶安全关键场景生成方法使用软引导，仅提供概率性偏好，无法保证特定碰撞类型的几何和严重性约束得到满足，难以实现可控的碰撞场景生成。

Method: CCFM包含三个组件：(i) 启发式碰撞选择器，通过复合评分最优选择对抗智能体和碰撞类型；(ii) 结构化硬约束，通过接触点、朝向和严重性要求明确定义四种碰撞类型（追尾、侧碰、切入、正面）；(iii) 碰撞约束流匹配采样器，通过高斯-牛顿流形投影强制执行约束。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上碰撞率达46.4%，在nuPlan数据集上达83.1%，显著优于基线方法，同时保持真实驾驶行为。

Conclusion: CCFM通过可控制的碰撞特性生成安全关键场景，为自动驾驶安全评估和sim-to-real碰撞数据生成提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Evaluation of autonomous vehicle (AV) planners in safety-critical closed-loop simulation is essential for real-world deployment. However, generating controllable safety-critical scenarios remains challenging. Existing approaches use soft guidance that provides only probabilistic preferences and cannot guarantee the satisfaction of geometric and severity constraints associated with specific collision types. We introduce Collision-Constrained Flow Matching (CCFM), a novel framework that guarantees precise collision control through hard physical constraints. CCFM consists of three key components: (i) a heuristic collision selector that optimally identifies an adversarial agent and collision type via composite scoring; (ii) structured hard constraints that explicitly define four collision types (rear-end, side, cut-in, head-on) through contact point, heading, and severity requirements; and (iii) a collision-constrained flow matching sampler that enforces the constraints via Gauss-Newton manifold projection. CCFM achieves collision rate up to 46.4% on nuScenes and 83.1% on nuPlan, significantly outperforming baselines while preserving realistic driving behavior. By enabling controllable collision characteristics in safety-critical scenario generation, CCFM provides a reliable foundation for AV safety evaluation and sim-to-real crash data generation. The code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/KELISBU/CCFM.

</details>


### [220] [Spatial Graph Representation and Morphometric Analysis of the Pulmonary Vascular Tree From Computed Tomography Using Multi-Scale Hessian-Based Filter Fusion and TEASAR Skeletonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04457)
*Piotr Mackiewicz,Jakub Kołyska,Radoslaw Roszczyk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、可解释的确定性流水线，用于从CT图像中重建肺血管树，通过多尺度Hessian滤波、血管中心线提取和几何合理性评估，生成具有大约2.3分形维数的形态学合理的肺血管模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从CT图像重建肺血管树对于定量肺分析、血管形态评估和患者特定建模至关重要，但由于血管跨度从近端动脉到远端微血管、CT分辨率有限、部分容积效应、图像质量异质性和呼吸运动伪影等因素，这一任务仍具挑战性。现有深度学习方法需要大量标注数据，因此需要一种确定性、无需训练的可解释方法。

Method: 1) 使用多尺度Hessian-based Frangi和Sato血管性滤波器，在1-8 mm的12个空间尺度上通过加权最大响应融合；2) 通过亨氏单位阈值分割、形态学后处理及Chan-Vese主动轮廓细化分割肺实质；3) 采用Kimimaro实现的TEASAR算法提取血管中心线；4) 分别构建左右肺血管图，进行剪枝和环性验证；5) 使用体积分形维数、Strahler阶数分析、Horton比率和Murray定律评估几何合理性。

Result: 分形维数约为2.3，与人类肺血管系统的文献报告值一致；残余的分支度量偏差反映了有限CT分辨率导致的远端血管截断效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的可解释流水线能够生成几何合理的肺血管树模型，可支持定量肺部成像、血管形态测量学和计算肺建模。

Abstract: Reconstructing the pulmonary vascular tree from computed tomography (CT) images is essential for quantitative lung analysis, vascular morphology assessment, and patient-specific modeling, yet it remains challenging because vessels span multiple scales, from proximal arteries to distal microvasculature. Clinical chest CT is further affected by limited spatial resolution, partial volume effects, heterogeneous image quality, and respiratory motion artifacts. Unlike deep learning-based pulmonary vessel segmentation methods that require large annotated datasets, we propose a deterministic, training-free, and explainable pipeline for CT-based pulmonary vascular tree reconstruction. The method fuses multiscale Hessian-based Frangi and Sato vesselness filters using a weighted maximum response across 12 spatial scales from 1 to 8 mm, enabling detection of large pulmonary arteries and peripheral branches. Lung parenchyma is segmented by Hounsfield unit thresholding, morphological post-processing, and Chan-Vese active contour refinement. Vascular centerlines are extracted using the Kimimaro implementation of the TEASAR algorithm; separate left- and right-lung vascular graphs are then constructed, pruned, and verified for acyclicity. Geometric plausibility is assessed using volumetric fractal dimension, Strahler order analysis, Horton ratios, and Murray's law. The resulting fractal dimension of approximately 2.3 is consistent with reported values for the human pulmonary vasculature. At the same time, residual deviations in branching metrics reflect distal-vessel truncation caused by finite CT resolution. These results indicate that the proposed explainable pipeline can generate geometrically plausible pulmonary vascular tree models and may support quantitative pulmonary imaging, vascular morphometry, and computational lung modeling.

</details>


### [221] [Flash-BoN: Instant Drafts for Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04461)
*Ruchit Rawal,Reza Shirkavand,Sayak Paul,Yuxin Wen,Heng Huang,Yizheng Chen,Tom Goldstein,Gowthami Somepalli*

Main category: cs.CV
