This strategy resolves intra-slice splits via Planar Ensemble Consensus and inter-slice splits via Axial Spatio-Temporal Propagation. Notably, we integrate the proposed workflow into the Neuroglancer visualization platform, enabling an interactive human-in-the-loop proofreading system. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms supervised baselines and reduces manual proofreading time by 33.4%. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/HeadLiuYun/Probe-EM.

</details>


### [256] [Learning Probabilistic Prompt for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04711)
*Hyekang Park,Sanghoon Lee,Geon Lee,Jongyoun Noh,Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率分布提示的持续学习框架，通过将每个提示建模为概率分布并构建混合分布来采样多样化提示，有效解决了现有提示方法中的提示崩溃问题（提示高度相似无法捕捉多样化数据分布），并在多个标准基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的持续学习方法存在提示崩溃问题（prompt collapse），即学习到的提示之间高度相似，无法捕捉持续学习场景中多样化的数据分布。

Method: 1) 将每个提示建模为一个概率分布；2) 构建这些分布的混合模型，从中采样多样化提示；3) 引入分布正则化损失，防止训练过程中提示分布的剧烈变化。

Result: 在ImageNet-R、CIFAR-100和CUB-200等标准持续学习基准上进行了大量实验，验证了所提框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过概率化提示表示和混合分布采样策略，有效缓解了提示崩溃问题，提升了模型在持续学习场景中捕捉多样化数据分布的能力。

Abstract: Continual learning aims to progressively learn from a sequence of tasks, each containing a disjoint subset of classes, while preserving previously learned knowledge. Prompt-based continual learning methods propose to learn a small set of parameters, i.e., prompts, by associating them with a query feature of an input image. These methods optimize the prompts, attempting to represent diverse patterns of images. However, we have observed that existing prompt-based methods suffer from a prompt collapse problem, that is, the prompts tend to be highly similar to each other, thereby failing to capture the diverse data distributions in continual learning scenarios. To address this issue, we propose in this paper a novel prompt-based continual learning framework that captures diverse patterns of images across a sequence of tasks. To this end, we model each prompt as a probabilistic distribution and construct a mixture of these distributions, from which we sample diverse prompts. This enables our model to effectively capture highly diverse image distributions in the continual learning process. We also present a distribution regularization loss to prevent abrupt changes in the prompt distributions throughout the training process. We show extensive experimental results for continual learning on standard benchmarks, including ImageNet-R, CIFAR-100, and CUB-200, demonstrating the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [257] [Reference-Induced Consensus for Selective Posed-Reference Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04722)
*Wonseok Kang,Jaehyun Kim,Jeongmin Lee,Tae-Wan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RIC-Loc是一种无需场景训练、无需SfM点云的定位方法，利用参考帧位姿和冻结VGGT网络预测的局部位姿、深度及查询-参考帧对应关系，通过鲁棒一致性估计生成多个SE(3)候选位姿，并从中导出空间分散度和轨迹协方差两种可靠性评分，在室内外及低纹理场景中实现有效的无真值故障检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参考帧的定位方法通常依赖SfM三维地图点、查询到地图的2D-3D匹配或PnP求解，这些步骤计算开销大且对场景结构敏感；同时缺乏有效的无真值故障检测机制。本文旨在设计一种无需SfM点云、无需场景训练、且具备内置可靠性评估的参考帧定位方法。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) 使用冻结的VGGT网络对查询图像和参考图像进行前向推理，预测局部相机位姿、深度以及查询-参考帧之间的对应轨迹；(2) 每个参考帧独立诱导一个SE(3)查询位姿假设；(3) 通过鲁棒一致性估计融合多个假设得到最终位姿；(4) 从假设结构本身导出两种可靠性评分——空间分散度（spatial dispersion）和轨迹协方差评分（track-conditioned covariance score），用于无真值情况下的故障检测。

Result: 在室内、室外及大规模低纹理数据集上，两种评分在协方差可计算的子集上互补：联合策略在纹理丰富场景中表现最优，协方差评分在低纹理场景中更有效。假设导出的评分在故障检测任务上持续优于标准的检索分数差距和随机排序。无需场景训练的一致性估计器定位精度与基于结构的室内定位方法相当，且优于同类前馈基线，实现了有效的选择性定位操作。

Conclusion: RIC-Loc证明了无需SfM点云、无需场景训练的参考帧定位是可行的，且通过假设导出的可靠性评分为定位系统提供了有效的无真值故障检测能力，在多个场景下具有竞争力。

Abstract: We present RIC-Loc (Reference-Induced Consensus localization), a scene-training-free posed-reference localizer that is SfM-point-map-free in its main estimator: it uses known reference poses, but not precomputed SfM 3D map points, query-to-map 2D-3D matches, or query-to-map PnP. A frozen VGGT pass predicts local camera poses, depth, and query-reference tracks for a query and selected references. Each reference induces one map-frame SE(3) query-pose hypothesis, robust consensus estimates the pose, and the preserved hypothesis structure yields two reliability scores: spatial dispersion and a track-conditioned covariance score. On the covariance-eligible set, the two scores are complementary for held-out, ground-truth-free failure detection across indoor, outdoor, and large-scale low-texture benchmarks: the joint policy is strongest in textured scenes and the covariance score in the low-texture regime, and the hypothesis-derived scores consistently outperform the standard retrieval-score gap and random rankings. Without per-scene training the consensus estimator remains accurate -- competitive with structure-based localization indoors and improving over a comparable feed-forward baseline -- giving an effective selective operating regime for posed-reference localization. Code is available at https://github.com/SNU-DLLAB/ric_loc.

</details>


### [258] [When Does High-CFG Diffusion Inversion Fail? A Controlled Study of Prompt--Latent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04731)
*Yan Zeng,Yusuke Hosoya,Huyen T. T. Tran,Takayuki Okatani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究高分类器无导引（CFG）尺度下文本引导扩散模型的逆过程。实验发现，不同提示词会导致三种重构行为（容易、困难、中等），提出"提示压力"指标衡量CFG对去噪轨迹的影响，并通过局部轨迹一致性干预改善高CFG逆过程的重构和编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中扩散模型的逆过程常使用比生成时更低的CFG尺度，这种不匹配虽有效但缺乏理论解释。本文旨在回答：当目标图像由高CFG轨迹生成时，该轨迹能否被成功逆推？

Method: 在受控的生成-逆推-重构框架中，使用已知的真实初始潜变量和去噪轨迹。采用扩散编辑基准的提示词，在高CFG下生成图像并用固定点逆推法重构。定义"提示压力"指标（逐步骤测量CFG使去噪更新偏离无条件轨迹的程度），并进行文本侧分析和局部轨迹一致性干预。

Result: 揭示了三种提示级重构行为：容易提示（多数初值成功）、困难提示（多数初值失败）、中等提示（成败取决于提示-潜变量配对）。总提示压力与重构质量相关，但无法解释中等配对的成败。文本分析表明主视觉主体和措辞影响逆推难度。局部轨迹一致性干预改善了高CFG下的重构和编辑效果。

Conclusion: 高CFG逆推失败需要局部、轨迹感知的分析；所提出的诊断性干预措施在受控设定下有效支持了这一观点。

Abstract: Text-guided diffusion inversion is central to image editing, where an image is mapped to an initial latent and then edited by replaying the denoising process under a modified prompt. In practice, however, inversion is often performed with a lower classifier-free guidance(CFG) scale than the one used for generation or editing. This mismatch is empirically useful but leaves a basic question unresolved: when a target image is generated by a high-CFG trajectory, when can that trajectory actually be inverted? We study this question in a controlled generation--inversion--reconstruction setting, where the true initial latent and denoising trajectory are known. Using prompts taken from an existing diffusion-editing benchmark, we generate images under high CFG and reconstruct them with fixed-point inversion using the same prompt and guidance setting. The results reveal three types of prompt-level reconstruction behavior: easy prompts that reconstruct for most initial latents, hard prompts that fail for most initial latents, and intermediate prompts whose success depends on the prompt--latent pairing. To analyze the generation side, we define prompt pressure, a step-wise measure of how strongly CFG moves the denoising update away from the unconditional trajectory. Total pressure correlates with reconstruction quality and separates easy from hard prompts, but it does not explain the success or failure of intermediate prompt--latent pairs. Text-side analyses further show that the main visual subject and wording can change inversion difficulty. Finally, we evaluate a compact trajectory-consistency intervention that relaxes guidance only at locally unstable inverse steps. This diagnostic check improves reconstruction and Prompt-to-Prompt editing in our controlled setting, supporting the view that high-CFG inversion failure requires local, trajectory-aware analysis.

</details>


### [259] [SparseOcc++: Geometry-Aware Sparse Latent Representation for Semantic Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04732)
*Pin Tang,Zhongdao Wang,Guoqing Wang,Xiangxuan Ren,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparseOcc++提出了一种几何感知的稀疏框架，通过符号距离回归的解耦式场景补全和几何引导的语义分割，在3D语义占用预测任务上实现了SOTA性能，比SparseOcc快3.9倍，比OccFormer快5.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D语义占用预测方法存在计算浪费（密集体素表示）、3D结构信息丢失（BEV/TPV投影）或计算开销大（SparseOcc将场景补全与语义预测耦合，向空区域传播高维特征导致过多激活和几何模糊）。

Method: 1) 提出场景补全场（SCF），在稀疏锚点体素上进行符号距离回归，显式解耦场景补全与语义分割；2) 结合正交分解与离散距离学习来建模复杂室外几何结构；3) 设计几何引导传播模块，将SCF转换为完整体素场景，并将语义分割限制在几何验证的区域。

Result: 在nuScenes上IoU提升2.3个点，速度比SparseOcc快3.9倍；在SemanticKITTI上比OccFormer快5.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过解耦场景补全与语义预测，结合稀疏几何感知的设计，SparseOcc++在性能和效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Vision-based 3D semantic occupancy prediction is essential for autonomous driving, yet dense voxel representations waste computation on largely empty space, while BEV and TPV projections compromise fine-grained 3D structure. Fully sparse representations offer an attractive alternative, but existing methods, including SparseOcc, entangle scene completion with semantic prediction by indiscriminately propagating high-dimensional features into empty regions and applying voxel-wise classification. This creates excessive activations, computational overhead, and geometric ambiguity. We present SparseOcc++, a geometry-aware sparse framework that explicitly decouples scene completion from semantic segmentation. SparseOcc++ reformulates completion as signed-distance regression on sparse anchor voxels through a scene completion field (SCF). To model complex outdoor geometry robustly, it combines orthogonal decomposition with discretized distance learning. A geometry-guided propagation module then converts the SCF into a complete volumetric scene and restricts semantic segmentation to geometrically verified regions. Experiments establish new state of the art: SparseOcc++ improves IoU by 2.3 points and is 3.9x faster than SparseOcc on nuScenes, while achieving a 5.9x speedup over OccFormer on SemanticKITTI.

</details>


### [260] [DriftST: One-Step Generative Inference of Spatial Transcriptomics from H\&E Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04740)
*Yuhang Yang,Yonggan Bu,Shengyuan Zhou,Yiming Luo,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DriftST框架，通过构建从组织学条件化源到表达分布的直迁移生成模型，结合STransformer捕获基因间依赖性和差异化重要性，实现H&E图像到空间分辨基因表达的高效统一推断，在spots和cells两个分辨率上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学成本高、通量低、公共数据集规模小；从H&E染色组织学图像推断基因表达是一种低成本替代方案，但现有方法存在三个局限：回归方法过平滑、生成方法推理慢；将基因视为独立且同等重要，忽略基因间依赖性和差异化信息量；大多针对单一分辨率（spots或cells）设计。

Method: 提出DriftST框架，包含：(1) Cellular Drifting生成模型，学习从组织学条件化源到表达分布的直迁移，实现高效单步生成；(2) STransformer模块，包含共表达注意力机制捕获基因间依赖关系，以及基因残差门控处理差异化基因重要性；(3) 基于通用基因面板表示，统一适用于spots和cells两种分辨率。

Result: 在多种组织类型和平台上的大量实验表明，DriftST在spots和cells两个分辨率上均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: DriftST通过统一的生成式框架，同时解决了现有方法在表达分布保真度、基因结构建模和多分辨率适配方面的局限性，为从H&E图像推断空间基因表达提供了高效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial Transcriptomics (ST) measures gene expression while preserving spatial context, but its high cost and low throughput leave public datasets small. Inferring expression directly from widely available Hematoxylin and Eosin (H&E) stained histology offers a cost-effective alternative. However, existing approaches face several limitations: regression methods over-smooth toward the conditional mean, while generative methods are faithful but require slow multi-step inference; most methods treat genes as independent and equally important, ignoring inter-gene dependencies and heterogeneous gene informativeness; and most are tailored to a single resolution, either spot-level or cell-level. To address these issues, we propose DriftST, a unified framework for inferring spatially resolved gene expression from H&E images. DriftST builds on a Cellular Drifting generative model that learns a direct drift from a histology-conditioned source to the expression distribution, retaining generative expressiveness while enabling efficient one-step generation. To capture gene structure, we introduce the STransformer, which combines a co-expression attention module for inter-gene dependencies with a gene residual gate for differential gene importance. Operating on a generic gene-panel representation, DriftST applies directly to both spot-level and cell-level data in one framework, and extensive experiments across diverse tissues and platforms show that it achieves state-of-the-art performance at both resolutions.

</details>


### [261] [MergeSurv: Merging-Based Continual Learning for Survival Analysis on Whole-Slide Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04747)
*Vu Minh Tran,Doanh C. Bui,Maï K. Nguyen,Khang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MergeSurv，一种基于模型合并的持续学习框架，用于全切片图像（WSI）的生存分析。该方法独立微调病理视觉-语言基础模型，然后顺序合并参数，无需存储历史数据。实验表明，MergeSurv在四个TCGA数据集上优于传统持续学习方法，并有效减少灾难性遗忘，为计算病理学中可扩展且隐私保护的持续学习提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WSI生存分析模型通常为每个癌症队列独立训练，导致针对千兆像素级别WSI的持续更新计算成本极高，缺乏高效的持续适应机制。

Method: 提出MergeSurv框架：1）对每个任务独立微调病理视觉-语言基础模型；2）按顺序合并学习到的参数，无需存储历史训练数据；3）提出两种推理策略：One-for-All (OFA)和Voting-Expert Aggregation (VEA)。

Result: 在四个TCGA队列上的实验表明，MergeSurv优于朴素微调、基于正则化和基于回放的持续学习方法，同时有效减少了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 模型合并是计算病理学中实现可扩展、隐私保护的持续学习的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Survival analysis on Whole Slide Images (WSIs) is important in computational pathology for prognosis estimation and treatment planning. However, existing survival models are typically trained independently for each cancer cohort, making continual adaptation computationally expensive for gigapixel-scale WSIs. In this study, we propose MergeSurv, a merging-based continual learning framework for WSI survival analysis. A pathology vision-language foundation model is independently fine-tuned on each task, and the learned parameters are sequentially merged into a unified model without storing previous training data. We further investigate two inference strategies: One-for-All (OFA) and Voting-Expert Aggregation (VEA). Experiments on four TCGA cohorts demonstrate that MergeSurv outperforms naive fine-tuning as well as representative regularization-based and rehearsal-based continual learning methods, while effectively reducing catastrophic forgetting. The results suggest that model merging is a promising direction for scalable and privacy-preserving continual learning in computational pathology.

</details>


### [262] [Continual Model Merging with Test-Time Adaptation for Whole-Slide Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04755)
*Duc-Thanh Le,Doanh C. Bui,Maï K. Nguyen,Khang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基准研究，将测试时自适应引导的模型合并方法映射到无排练的持续全切片图像分类任务，并与传统持续学习方法进行比较。实验表明该方法在任务特定性能和知识保留方面表现出色，但对任务顺序和分布自适应与知识积累之间的交互敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并作为持续学习的实用替代方案已被提出，但现有测试时自适应引导的模型合并方法主要在多头或单目标设置下研究，其在序列持续学习场景下的行为尚未被充分理解。

Method: 构建了一个基准研究，将测试时自适应引导的模型合并方法映射到无排练的持续全切片图像分类任务，在六个TCGA癌症分型数据集上评估，覆盖CLASS-IL和TASK-IL场景、域内和域外评估以及不同任务顺序。

Result: 测试时自适应模型合并能够提供强大的任务特定性能，并在不存储历史WSI的情况下改善先前知识的保留。但性能对任务顺序以及当前分布自适应与累积知识之间的交互敏感。

Conclusion: 模型合并与测试时自适应是持续计算病理学中一个有前景的方向，未来需要开发能平衡域偏移自适应与历史知识显式保留的方法。

Abstract: Model merging offers a practical alternative to conventional continual learning by integrating independently fine-tuned models without retaining previous training data. Recent state-of-the-art model merging methods employ test-time adaptation (TTA-guided merging) to address distribution shifts by adjusting merging-related variables using unlabeled target data. However, these methods have primarily been studied in multi-task or single-target settings, and their behavior under sequential continual learning remains insufficiently understood. We present a benchmark study that maps this family of methods to rehearsal-free continual Whole Slide Image classification and evaluates them against traditional continual-learning approaches. Experiments on six TCGA cancer-subtyping cohorts cover CLASS-IL and TASK-IL scenarios, in-domain and out-of-domain evaluation, and different task orders. The results show that adapting model merging at test time can provide strong task-specific performance and improve retention of previously acquired knowledge without storing historical WSIs. Nevertheless, performance remains sensitive to task order and to the interaction between adaptation on the current distribution and accumulated knowledge. This benchmark identifies model merging with test-time adaptation as a promising direction for continual computational pathology and motivates future methods that balance adaptation to domain shift with explicit preservation of historical knowledge.

</details>


### [263] [DeGenseGS: Geometrically and Semantically Decoupled Surgical Scene Understanding in 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04761)
*Yimo Wang,Bin Kang,Shuojue Yang,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeGenseGS提出了一种几何与语义解耦的4D重建框架，通过HexPlane时空纠缠模块、光栅化原生语义提取和角度对齐优化策略，在手术场景中实现了语义-解剖结构的精准对齐，在CholecSeg8k和EndoVis18数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉-语言模型刚性耦合到可变形场中，导致语义特征与几何形变紧密绑定，造成语义含义与物理解剖结构之间的严重错位，无法满足实时、文本可提示的4D重建在自主手术交互中的需求。

Method: 1) HexPlane时空纠缠模块：使用共享运动学潜在变量同步语义变化与场景动态，同时显式解耦语义更新与几何形变；2) 光栅化原生语义提取机制：从拓扑连续的特征图中推断语义，增强对重建伪影的鲁棒性；3) 角度对齐优化策略：顺应原生超球面潜在空间，防止语义失真。

Result: 在CholecSeg8k和EndoVis18数据集上的大量评估表明，DeGenseGS达到了最先进的性能，在剧烈组织形变和拓扑变化下仍能实现空间连续的语义分割和鲁棒的语义-解剖对齐。

Conclusion: 提出的几何与语义解耦框架有效解决了4D手术场景中语义与几何的错位问题，实现了增强的几何完整性和鲁棒的语义-解剖对齐，为自主手术交互提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time, text-promptable 4D reconstruction is indispensable for autonomous surgical interaction. Severe misalignment between semantic meaning and physical anatomy still persists, largely because existing solutions integrate Vision-Language Models into deformable fields via a rigid coupling scheme that tightly binds semantic features to geometric warping. In this paper, we propose DeGenseGS, Geometrically and Semantically Decoupled Surgical Scene Understanding in 4D Gaussian Splatting, a novel framework that independently models semantic evolution and geometric deformation. Specifically, we propose a HexPlane-based spatiotemporal entanglement module that uses shared kinematic latents to synchronize semantic mutations with scene dynamics, while explicitly disentangling semantic updates from geometric deformation. To further ensure robustness against reconstruction artifacts, we devise a Rasterization-Native Semantic Extraction mechanism that infers semantics from topologically continuous feature maps. Additionally, we incorporate an angular-aligned optimization strategy that conforms to the native hyperspherical latent space, thereby preventing semantic distortion. Extensive evaluations on the CholecSeg8k and EndoVis18 datasets demonstrate that DeGenseGS achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our framework yields enhanced geometric completeness and robust semantic-anatomic alignment, enabling spatially continuous segmentation despite drastic tissue deformation and topological transitions.

</details>


### [264] [DGSeg: Dynamic Gating of Semantic-Spatial Guided Predictions for Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04779)
*Ruizhe Zeng,Siyu Cao,Lu Zhang,Zhiyong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DGSeg提出了一种推理分割框架，通过多模态大语言模型生成互补的语义和空间线索，并利用动态门控模块自适应融合不同分割分支的预测，有效抑制噪声和歧义，在ReasonSeg等基准上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法将复杂推理压缩为稀疏线索（如点或框）时存在歧义和噪声，无法准确保留推理意图；虽然多线索可丰富目标信息，但现有方法将线索联合输入单一分割流程，导致模糊或错误线索影响整体预测。

Method: 1) MLLM联合推理目标身份和空间位置，生成互补的语义线索和空间线索；2) 将两类线索分别输入独立的分割分支进行预测；3) 设计轻量级动态门控模块，通过相对分支质量监督训练，自适应集成两个分支的预测结果，抑制噪声和冲突区域。

Result: 在ReasonSeg验证集和测试集上分别达到69.6%和67.3% gIoU，在多个基准上一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过分离语义和空间线索并动态融合，DGSeg有效缓解了线索压缩带来的噪声问题，提升了推理分割的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to predict pixel-wise masks for targets given complex language queries. Existing approaches leverage Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for vision-language reasoning and generate intermediate target cues (e.g., points or boxes) to guide a segmentation model. However, compressing rich reasoning into sparse cues often introduces ambiguity and noise, preventing these cues from accurately preserving the reasoning intent. While multiple complementary cues can enrich target information, existing methods typically feed them jointly into a single segmentation process, allowing ambiguous or erroneous cues to affect the entire prediction. Therefore, we propose DGSeg, a reasoning segmentation framework that learns to fuse predictions guided by semantic and spatial cues. Specifically, the MLLM jointly reasons about both target identity and spatial location, producing complementary semantic and spatial cues that are fed into separate segmentation branches. Their predictions are adaptively integrated by a lightweight dynamic gating module trained with relative branch-quality supervision to suppress noisy or conflicting regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DGSeg consistently outperforms strong baselines on multiple benchmarks and achieves 69.6% and 67.3% gIoU on the challenging ReasonSeg validation and test splits. Code is available at https://github.com/RZZeng/DGSeg.

</details>


### [265] [LILAC: Layer-Wise Independent LoRAs and Cascaded Conditioning for Multi-Concept Customization of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04801)
*Marian Lupascu,Sebastian Ripa,Mihai Trascau,Mariana-Iuliana Georgescu,Ionut Mironica*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LILAC 提出了一种无需联合训练、以图像分层方式组合多个独立低秩适配器（LoRA）的方法，避免了参数级干扰，在多个主体个性化图像生成中显著优于现有融合方法（如 Orthogonal Adaptation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在融合多个独立训练的概念适配器时（通过联邦平均、梯度融合或正交约束）存在身份混淆、风格渗染问题，且需要联合重训练。

Method: LILAC 将每个主体作为独立的图像层处理，推理时每次仅激活一个适配器，后一个主体以前一主体的冻结复合结果为条件进行生成，避免参数级干扰；无需联合训练，概念数量线性扩展，与骨干网络无关。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image-Edit 上应用 LILAC，ArcFace 检测率达到 0.861，而 Orthogonal Adaptation 原设置下仅为 0.745。

Conclusion: LILAC 通过图像级分层组合而非参数空间融合，有效解决了多主体个性化生成中的身份混淆问题，且无需联合训练，具备良好的可扩展性和骨干网络无关性。

Abstract: Personalizing text-to-image diffusion models to render several specific subjects in a coherent image remains challenging: the model must preserve each subject's identity while keeping the scene spatially and visually coherent. Methods that fuse independently trained concept adapters in a shared weight space (via federated averaging, gradient fusion, or orthogonality constraints) suffer from identity confusion and style bleeding and require joint retraining. In this work, we show that composing concepts as separate image layers, instead of merging their adapters in a shared weight space, avoids parameter-level interference. We introduce LILAC, a framework that composes independently trained low-rank adapters at inference time: each subject is conditioned on the frozen composite of previously placed subjects, with exactly one adapter active at a time, therefore identities never interfere at the parameter level. LILAC composes the adapters without any joint training, scales linearly with the number of concepts, and is backbone-agnostic. Under the Orthogonal Adaptation protocol, LILAC applied on Qwen-Image-Edit reaches an ArcFace detection rate of 0.861, while Orthogonal Adaptation reports 0.745 in its original setting. Adaptation reports 0.745 in its original setting. Code is available at https://github.com/marianlupascu/LILAC.

</details>


### [266] [Hybrid Deep Learning for Traceability and Classification of Industrial Slate Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04811)
*Soren Antebi,Stefan Eickeler,Sandra Halscheidt,Rene Schmitz,Michael Muellers,Dirk Hecker,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级混合深度学习框架，结合XFeat特征匹配和MobileNetV3分类，在板岩瓷砖的实例重识别和产地分类任务上分别提升AUC 15.4%和准确率10.9%，并在包含2,610张图像的工业数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 板岩瓷砖这类天然材料视觉变异性大，人工检测成本高且易出错，需要自动化的实例重识别和产地分类方法来提升生产效率和品控水平。

Method: 提出轻量级混合深度学习框架，集成XFeat特征匹配分支（结合LightGlue匹配头）用于实例重识别，以及MobileNetV3分类分支用于产地分类；两个分支的特征共享与融合以提升分类性能。

Result: 实例匹配AUC提升15.4%，分类准确率相比标准MobileNetV3提升10.9%；在包含2,610张图像、来自6个产地的工业数据集上验证有效。

Conclusion: 该混合方法在工业场景下的物体重识别和分类任务中表现出有效性，能够提升板岩瓷砖行业的生产效率和品控水平。

Abstract: Applying deep learning to instance-aware reidentification of slate tiles and extraction site classification can improve production efficiency and quality control in the slate tile industry. These tasks are particularly important for handling natural materials where visual variability can make manual inspection costly and error-prone. We present a lightweight, hybrid deep learning approach that combines image matching and classification within a single framework. The system integrates a feature-matching branch based on XFeat with a MobileNetV3- based classification branch. The XFeat branch, combined with a LightGlue matching head, improves instance matching performance by +15.4% AUC. For classification, features from both backbones are shared and fused, resulting in a +10.9% accuracy improvement over a standard MobileNetV3 model. Our approach is evaluated on a newly created industrial dataset consisting of 2,610 slate tile images from six extraction sites. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for object re-identification and classification in an industrial setting.

</details>


### [267] [TGRIP: A Text-Guided Approach to Vehicle Instance Prediction in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04812)
*Miguel Antunes-García,Santiago Montiel-Marín,Fabio Sánchez-García,Rodrigo Gutiérrez-Moreno,Rafael Barea,Luis M. Bergasa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TGRIP框架，通过视觉-语言基础模型引入语义先验到BEV实例预测中，采用教师-学生流水线生成语义增强的BEV地图作为辅助监督，在nuScenes数据集上达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV端到端实例预测方法主要依赖几何监督（如占用率回归、光流），将场景智能体视为通用移动障碍物，缺乏显式语义感知能力，导致在复杂场景（如超车、交叉路口）中难以解决歧义。

Method: 提出TGRIP框架，采用教师-学生流水线：利用视觉-语言基础模型从多相机图像生成密集语义增强的BEV地图，作为训练时的辅助监督，引导学生网络学习既几何一致又语义可辨别的时空表征。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，TGRIP超越现有SOTA模型，验证了语义增强对鲁棒端到端运动预测的基础性作用。

Conclusion: 这是首次将语义引导与未来实例预测的时间任务统一起来的尝试，证明语义丰富化是实现鲁棒端到端运动预测的关键要素。

Abstract: Bird's-Eye View (BEV) end-to-end instance prediction has emerged as a robust paradigm for autonomous driving perception, effectively mitigating the error propagation inherent in traditional modular pipelines. However, current state-of-the-art approaches rely predominantly on geometric supervision, such as occupancy regression and optical flow, effectively treating scene agents as generic moving obstacles. This absence of explicit semantic awareness imposes limitations on the capacity of the model to solve ambiguities in complex scenarios, particularly those where object-specific behavior is essential for accurate forecasting (e.g. overtaking, intersections). In this paper, we introduce Text-Guided Representation for Instance Prediction (TGRIP), a novel framework that bridges this gap by injecting rich semantic priors into the instance prediction loop. The proposed teacher-student pipeline employs Vision-Language Foundation Models to generate dense, semantic-enhanced BEV maps from multi-camera images. These maps serve as auxiliary supervision during training, guiding the network to learn spatio-temporal representations that are not only geometrically consistent but also semantically discriminative. To the best of our knowledge, this represents the first attempt to unify semantic guidance with the temporal task of future instance prediction. The experimental results demonstrate that TGRIP surpasses existing state-of-the-art models in nuScenes, validating the hypothesis that semantic enrichment is a fundamental element for robust, end-to-end motion prediction. Code is available on https://github.com/miguelag99/TGRIP.

</details>


### [268] [PAGE: Towards Practical Human-level Gaze Target Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04860)
*Zhoutong Ye,Chengwen Zhang,Zhaibin Cui,Mingze Sun,Jiaqi Liu,Xiangwu Li,Qingyang Wan,Chang Liu,Xutong Wang,Huan-ang Gao,Yu Mei,Chun Yu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PaGE（Practical Gaze Estimator）模型，通过显式建模场景与头部特征的复杂交互实现注视目标估计，并利用大模型蒸馏出轻量学生模型。该模型在7/9项指标上超越人类表现，将剩余2项指标的人机差距减少至少60%，同时蒸馏后的轻量模型适合实际部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注视目标估计任务需要同时理解全局场景语义和进行精确定位推理，现有模型难以达到人类水平，限制了实际应用。本文旨在弥合人机性能差距并实现轻量化部署。

Method: 提出PaGE模型，显式建模场景特征与头部特征的复杂交互；采用大型ViT-H+骨干网络作为教师模型，在更大规模、更多样化的无标签数据集上蒸馏出轻量骨干的学生模型。

Result: 在多项注视估计任务上达到SOTA，7/9项指标超越人类表现，剩余2项指标将人机差距减少至少60%；蒸馏后的轻量学生模型保留了教师模型大部分性能。

Conclusion: PaGE通过显式特征交互建模和大规模蒸馏训练，显著缩小了注视估计任务的人机差距，且蒸馏学生模型在保持高性能的同时实现了轻量化，适用于机器人和消费设备的实际部署。

Abstract: Gaze target estimation, the task of predicting where a person is looking in a scene, is crucial to understanding human attention and intent. It is a challenging task that combines high-level understanding of global scene semantics and precise spatial reasoning using human appearance (e.g. pose, eye orientation). As a result, human-level performance remains elusive for existing models, limiting their practical application. To this end, we propose PaGE (Practical Gaze Estimator), a gaze estimation model that explicitly models the complex interaction between scene and head features. Using a PaGE model with a large ViT-H+ backbone as the teacher, we further distill student models with lighter backbones on a much larger and more diverse unlabeled dataset. The architectural improvements and novel training recipe allow PaGE to achieve state-of-the-art performance on several gaze estimation tasks, outperforming humans in 7 out of 9 metrics while reducing the human-AI gap by at least 60% in the remaining 2. The distilled student models retain most of the teacher's performance while being lightweight enough for practical deployment on robots and consumer devices. The code and model checkpoints are available at our project page.

</details>


### [269] [EventCoT: Event-centric Video Chain-of-thought for Reasoning Temporal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04872)
*Youngkil Song,Yoonjae Baek,Dongwon Kim,Inho Kim,Dongkeun Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventCoT是一个面向推理时间定位的事件中心视频思维链框架，通过事件级标记化、事件推理和嵌入匹配实现高效的时间定位，在ActivityNet-RTL上达到SOTA，在ReXTime上取得强零样本结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时间定位(RTL)任务要求模型在单次响应中同时生成答案及其支撑时间区间，涉及高层推理与精确时间定位的联合生成，现有方法难以高效解决这一挑战。

Method: 1) 事件中心标记化：将输入视频转换为紧凑的事件令牌，实现问题相关事件的高效识别；2) 事件推理：在识别出的事件内进行推理以生成答案；3) 嵌入匹配对齐：通过将占位符令牌与视觉嵌入对齐来定位时间区间。

Result: 在ActivityNet-RTL推理时间定位任务上达到SOTA，且使用的视觉令牌数显著少于先前工作；在ReXTime接地视频问答基准上取得强零样本结果。

Conclusion: EventCoT通过事件中心视角和思维链推理，有效解决了推理时间定位任务中推理与时间定位的联合生成问题，同时大幅提升了视觉令牌的利用效率。

Abstract: Reasoning temporal localization (RTL) requires a model to generate an answer that itself contains the time interval supporting it, so high-level reasoning and precise temporal grounding must be produced jointly in a single response. To tackle this challenging task, we propose the first event-centric video chain-of-thought framework, dubbed EventCoT. EventCoT first performs event-centric tokenization of the input video to convert it into compact event tokens, enabling efficient identification of question-relevant events. It then reasons within the identified events to generate the answer, grounding the time interval via embedding matching that aligns placeholder tokens with visual embeddings. EventCoT achieves state-of-the-art results on ActivityNet-RTL for reasoning temporal localization while using substantially fewer visual tokens than previous work. To verify its general performance, we further evaluate EventCoT on the grounded video question answering benchmark ReXTime, where it attains strong zero-shot results.

</details>


### [270] [Unsupervised Detection of Underground Tunnels in Ground-Penetrating Radar Using Depth-Restricted Reconstruction Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04882)
*Muhammad Junaid,Shoab A. Khan,Nisar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种完全无监督的管道下方隧道检测管线，利用去噪卷积自编码器学习正常地质雷达图像的重建，通过深度限制的Top-k异常评分策略，在不使用任何隧道标签的情况下，在野外测试集上实现了AUC 0.994、F1 0.975的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 油气管道下方的秘密隧道可导致燃料盗窃、走私和破坏，传统监测只能在管道受损后发现问题。探地雷达可非侵入性成像隧道，但人工解读雷达图无法扩展到连续走廊监控，而有监督检测器需要大量隧道样本，现实中难以获取。

Method: 基于去噪卷积自编码器学习无异常地下结构，在推理时通过重建误差标记隧道。核心创新是深度限制的Top-k异常评分：仅在地理物理上隧道可能出现的深度带内池化最高重建误差，并与空间投票策略结合。

Result: 深度限制Top-k评分将AUC从0.986提升至0.994，漏检从74降至17（634个隧道窗口）；最优Top-k比例与深度限制交互（全图1%，深度限制后5%）；最终系统在1600个野外测试窗口上达到AUC 0.994，F1 0.975，召回率0.973，精确率0.976，虚警率1.6%。

Conclusion: 无需任何隧道标签即可实现高精度检测，基于物理约束的评分策略显著优于全局评分，空间投票在强评分规则下无额外收益。

Abstract: Clandestine tunneling beneath oil and gas pipelines enables fuel theft, smuggling, and sabotage, yet conventional monitoring detects damage only after a pipeline has been compromised. Ground-penetrating radar (GPR) can image such tunnels non-invasively, but manual radargram interpretation does not scale to continuous corridor surveillance, and supervised detectors require tunnel examples that are scarce in practice. We present a fully unsupervised detection pipeline trained exclusively on normal subsurface radargrams collected at a purpose-built field site containing three buried tunnels at 1.5-3 m depth. A denoising convolutional autoencoder learns the structure of anomaly-free ground; at inference, tunnels are flagged by reconstruction error. Our central contribution is a depth-restricted top-k anomaly score, which pools the highest reconstruction errors only within the depth band where tunnels can physically occur. This physically motivated rule raises AUC from 0.986 to 0.994 and cuts missed detections from 74 to 17 of 634 tunnel windows, relative to whole-image scoring, without any retraining or labels. We further show that the optimal top-k fraction interacts with the depth restriction - 1% pooling is best on full images, 5% once scoring is depth-restricted - and that spatial voting across overlapping survey windows helps weak per-image detectors but offers no benefit once the scoring rule is strong. The final system attains AUC 0.994, F1 0.975, recall 0.973, and precision 0.976 on 1,600 field test windows spanning 55 survey lines, at a 1.6% false-alarm rate, using no tunnel labels for training, scoring, or threshold calibration.

</details>


### [271] [HunyuanOCR-1.5: Making Lightweight OCR VLMs Faster and Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04884)
*Gengluo Li,Xingyu Wan,Shangpin Peng,Weinong Wang,Hao Feng,Yongkun Du,Binghong Wu,Zheng Ruan,Zhiqiong Lu,Liang Wu,Pengyuan Lyu,Huawen Shen,Zibin Lin,Shijing Hu,Jieneng Yang,Hongbing Wen,Guanghua Yu,Hong Liu,Bochao Wang,Can Ma,Han Hu,Chengquan Zhang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HunyuanOCR-1.5是一个轻量级端到端OCR专用视觉语言模型，通过DFlash解码加速和Agentic Data Flow数据构建系统，在保持轻量架构的同时实现了6.37倍推理加速，并在古文字OCR、图表表格解析、多图像问答等多类长尾任务上达到新性能标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轻量级OCR模型基础上，进一步提升推理效率并扩展长尾OCR能力覆盖，解决传统OCR模型在密集文档、表格、公式等长结构化输出场景下的延迟问题，以及古文字、低资源多语言等长尾任务的性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 效率改进：将DFlash适配到OCR解码，在不改变输出分布的前提下显著降低长结构化输出延迟，实现Transformer推理6.37倍加速、vLLM下2.14倍加速。2) 能力改进：提出Agentic Data Flow，一种智能体驱动的数据构建系统，将模型弱点转化为可执行的数据需求，自主完成素材搜索、质量验证和流水线开发，专门改进古文字OCR、细粒度图表表格解析、多图像文本QA、低资源多语言解析等长尾能力。

Result: OmniDocBench v1.6上达到顶级端到端OCR方案水平，在各长尾任务上取得新的性能里程碑；在轻量级OCR VLM中实现最快推理速度。

Conclusion: HunyuanOCR-1.5证明了在轻量端到端架构下，通过解码加速和数据构建系统的协同改进，可以实现更快的推理速度、更广的OCR能力覆盖以及部署优势。将开源模型权重和训练代码以支持后续研究和应用。

Abstract: We present HunyuanOCR-1.5, a lightweight end-to-end OCR-specialized vision-language model. HunyuanOCR unifies document parsing, text spotting, information extraction, text-image translation, and multi-image document understanding within a single end-to-end VLM. Building upon the lightweight architecture of HunyuanOCR-1.0, HunyuanOCR-1.5 does not redesign the backbone, but systematically improves both efficiency and capability. For efficiency, we adapt DFlash to OCR decoding, significantly reducing the latency of long structured outputs such as dense documents, tables, and formulas while preserving output distribution. Powered by DFlash, HunyuanOCR-1.5 achieves a 6.37x Transformer inference speedup and a 2.14x speedup under vLLM, delivering the fastest inference among lightweight OCR VLMs. For capability, we propose Agentic Data Flow, an agent-driven data construction system that transforms model weaknesses into executable data requirements and autonomously performs material search, quality verification, and pipeline development. It substantially improves long-tail capabilities in ancient-script OCR, fine-grained chart and table parsing, multi-image text-centric QA, low-resource multilingual parsing, and document hallucination evaluation. HunyuanOCR-1.5 ranks among the top-tier end-to-end OCR solutions on OmniDocBench v1.6 while achieving new performance milestones across these long-tail tasks. Combined with an upgraded pretraining and post-training recipe, HunyuanOCR-1.5 further extends its capability in high-resolution, long-context, and multi-task scenarios. Experiments demonstrate faster inference, broader OCR capability coverage, and the deployment advantages of a lightweight end-to-end model. We will release the model weights and training code to support future research and real-world OCR applications.

</details>


### [272] [ProCon: Projection-Consistency Memory for Training-Free Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04894)
*Joongwon Chae,Lihui Luo,Yang Liu,Dongmei Yu,Peiwu Qin,Runming Wang,Ilmoon Chae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProCon是一种无需训练的记忆式异常检测框架，通过软投影替代硬检索，利用种子扰动分层记忆和层共识聚合实现稳定残差计算，在MVTec-AD、VisA和Real-IAD数据集上取得SOTA性能（AUROC分别达99.8%、99.2%、93.2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的异常检测方法将记忆库视为最近邻查找表，采用硬检索方式判断测试补丁是否正常，存在假阳性匹配问题，且未检验补丁是否被局部正常邻域一致支持。

Method: ProCon提出三个关键技术：(1) 软正常投影——将测试补丁软投影到邻近正常记忆向量上，用投影残差作为异常证据；(2) 种子扰动分层记忆——构建扰动后的层级记忆以稳定残差；(3) 层共识聚合——通过中位数聚合记忆残差，融合深度特异性残差图。无需解码器训练、骨干微调、学习融合权重或伪异常监督。

Result: 在MVTec-AD、VisA和Real-IAD数据集上，单类别评估协议下图像AUROC分别达到99.8%、99.2%和93.2%；在七项标准指标上均表现优异。消融实验证实性能提升来源于硬检索替换为软正常投影以及记忆与深度共识的残差稳定化。

Conclusion: ProCon通过软投影和共识机制有效解决了记忆检索中的假阳性问题，实现无需训练的强性能异常检测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Memory-based anomaly detection is attractive because it localizes defects from normal images without training a decoder or synthesizing pseudo anomalies. However, most memory methods still use the memory bank as a nearest-neighbor lookup table: a test patch is treated as normal if it has one nearby normal anchor. This hard retrieval view is vulnerable to false-normal matches and does not test whether the patch is consistently supported by a local normal neighborhood. We propose ProCon, a training-free framework that turns memory retrieval into decoder-free reconstruction. ProCon softly projects each test patch onto nearby normal memory vectors and uses the projection residual as anomaly evidence. To stabilize this residual, it constructs seed-perturbed layer-wise memories, aggregates bank residuals by a median, and fuses depth-specific residual maps by layer consensus. ProCon requires no decoder training, backbone fine-tuning, learned fusion weights, or pseudo-anomaly supervision. Across MVTec-AD, VisA, and Real-IAD under the single-category evaluation protocol, ProCon achieves strong image- and pixel-level performance under seven standard metrics, including image AUROC scores of 99.8%, 99.2%, and 93.2%, respectively. Ablations show that the gains come from replacing hard retrieval with soft normal projection and stabilizing the residuals through memory and depth consensus. The code is available at https://github.com/jw-chae/Procon

</details>


### [273] [3DMPE: 3D Multi-Perspective Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04898)
*Vahan Huroyan,Md Rahat-uz-Zaman,Stephen Kobourov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3DMPE，一种基于优化、无需训练的方法，从多个部分观测的2D投影中重建3D点云，支持固定投影和可变投影设置，并在ShapeNet和Pix3D上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云重建方法多依赖学习范式，需要大量类别特定的训练数据；而多视角几何重建中，不同视图往往包含不同的点集（部分观测），缺少能够处理这种不完全跨视角距离信息且无需训练的重建方法。

Method: 提出3DMPE（3D Multi-Perspective Embedding），扩展了多视角同步嵌入（Multi-Perspective Simultaneous Embedding），通过优化方式处理缺失点和跨视图不完全成对距离信息。在固定投影设置中直接重建点云，在可变投影设置中联合估计投影映射。

Result: 在ShapeNet和Pix3D数据集上，使用Chamfer Distance、Earth Mover Distance和RMSE-Optimize-Align (ROA)评估重建质量，并分析了初始化、视图数量、点可见性以及多种噪声（距离噪声、错误对应）的影响。

Conclusion: 3DMPE能够从部分多视图几何观测中有效重建点云，无需训练数据和类别先验，对噪声具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We study 3D point cloud reconstruction from multiple partially observed 2D projections. Given two or more projections of an unknown 3D point cloud, together with cross-view point correspondences and visibility information, our goal is to recover a consistent 3D configuration when different views contain different subsets of points. We propose 3D Multi-Perspective Embedding (3DMPE), an optimization-based, training-free method that reconstructs the 3D point cloud and, in the variable-projection setting, jointly estimates the projection maps. 3DMPE extends Multi-Perspective Simultaneous Embedding to accommodate missing points and incomplete pairwise distance information across views. We consider both fixed-projection and variable-projection settings. Unlike learning-based reconstruction methods that infer shape from raw images and often depend on training data, 3DMPE operates on geometric observations with established correspondences and does not require category-specific training. Experiments on ShapeNet and Pix3D evaluate reconstruction quality using Chamfer Distance, Earth Mover Distance, and RMSE-Optimize-Align (ROA), and examine the effects of initialization, the number of views, point visibility, and several noise regimes, including noisy distances and erroneous correspondences. The results demonstrate that 3DMPE can effectively reconstruct point clouds from partial multi-view geometric observations.

</details>


### [274] [Graph Representation Learning of Longitudinal Medical Imaging Trajectories for Treatment Response Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04912)
*Johannes Kiechle,Richard Osuala,Daniel M. Lang,Stefan M. Fischer,Ivana Janíčková,Karim Lekadir,Julia A. Schnabel,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于影像的3D时空框架，利用图神经网络和自监督学习，从纵向DCE-MRI数据预测乳腺癌新辅助化疗后的病理完全缓解（pCR），在585名患者的ISPY-2数据集上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺癌患者对新辅助化疗的疗效反应个体差异大，现有方法难以准确预测个性化治疗反应，需要能够建模纵向医学影像时序信息的预测模型。

Method: 构建了一个3D时空框架，核心包括：（1）基于图神经网络建模不同时间点间的时序交互关系；（2）提出三个新颖的互补性自监督治疗轨迹表征学习目标；（3）利用纵向DCE-MRI影像数据进行pCR预测。

Result: 在ISPY-2数据集585名患者上，该方法在多个分类指标上显著优于视觉基线和自监督学习基线。还系统评估了DCE-MRI时间点数量和扫描间时间差异对预测性能的影响。

Conclusion: 验证了临床有意义的纵向医学影像建模在预测NACT诱导的pCR中的实用性，将开源代码和数据集工具以促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: In patients with breast cancer, pathological complete response (pCR) has been established as a clinically meaningful surrogate marker for long-term outcomes. While commonly treated with neoadjuvant chemotherapy (NACT), effective treatment decision-making remains challenging, as therapeutic response can vary substantially across patients, calling for predictive models capable of accurately estimating individualized treatment response. To address this, we propose an imaging-based 3D spatio-temporal framework for treatment response prediction that integrates a state-of-the-art graph neural network with relational modeling of temporal interactions across timepoints alongside three novel complementary self-supervised treatment trajectory representation learning objectives. Experiments across a cohort of 585 patients from the public ISPY-2 dataset demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms both vision and self-supervised learning baselines across several classification metrics. Alongside establishing a breast cancer pCR prediction benchmark, we include a principled ablation of our method and further introduce and empirically assess the impact of the available number of DCE-MRI timepoints per patient trajectory and the inclusion of inter-scan time-differences. Overall, our study substantiates the utility of clinically meaningful longitudinal medical imagaging modeling for predicting NACT-induced pCR. We will publicly share our code repository and a user-friendly PyPI library for dataset curation upon publication, effectively promoting reproducible open-source research.

</details>


### [275] [Efficient Perception in Automotive Detection and Tracking Using Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04921)
*Manish Kolachalam,Rani Malhotra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次全面评估了脉冲神经网络（SNN）在自动驾驶多目标检测与跟踪任务中的表现，使用SpikeYOLO架构在KITTI和BDD100K数据集上取得了与深度学习方法相竞争的结果，同时具备更高能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习算法能耗高、计算需求大，限制了其在边缘设备上的部署并引发可持续性担忧。神经形态计算和SNN作为传统冯·诺依曼架构的节能替代方案，在自主机器领域具有重要应用潜力，但缺乏在真实自动驾驶场景中的系统评估。

Method: 采用SpikeYOLO架构，结合迁移学习，在KITTI和BDD100K MOT2020两个真实自动驾驶数据集上进行多目标检测与跟踪任务的全面评估。

Result: 目标检测：KITTI数据集mAP达0.937，BDD100K MOT2020达0.771；目标跟踪：HOTA评分分别为0.701（KITTI）和0.445（BDD100K MOT2020），与深度学习方法结果相当。

Conclusion: SNN能够在保持高精度目标检测与跟踪性能的同时实现高能效，证明了其在真实世界自主系统感知任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Deep learning algorithms are notorious for their high carbon footprint and computational demands that limit their deployment on edge devices and raise concerns about their long-term sustainability. Neuromorphic computing and Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer a promising alternative to traditional Von Neumann architectures, providing energy-efficient performance, massively parallel computation, and on-chip learning capabilities. Autonomous machines represent a critical application domain where these advantages are particularly valuable. We present the first comprehensive evaluation of SNNs for real-world automotive multi-object detection and tracking. Using transfer learning with the SpikeYOLO architecture, we achieve mean Average Precision of 0.937 on the KITTI dataset and 0.771 on BDD100K MOT2020 dataset for object detection and a Higher Order Tracking Accuracy score of 0.701 (KITTI) and 0.445 (BDD100K MOT2020) for object tracking--results competitive with conventional deep learning methods. Our results demonstrate that SNNs can deliver high-performance object detection and tracking in an energy efficient manner, establishing their viability for perception in real-world autonomous systems.

</details>


### [276] [UniSpine-GS: An Efficient Physics-Aware Gaussian Framework for Cross-Modality Multi-view Spine Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04923)
*Qiuhua Chen,Changning Yu,Na Huang,Chao Sun,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniSpine-GS，一种基于3D感知的高斯表示框架，用于多视角脊柱成像的新视角投影渲染，无需显式3D重建，在CT和超声数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D脊柱成像在临床诊断中受限于硬件成本高以及不同成像模态间的物理差异导致模型泛化性差。

Method: 提出UniSpine-GS框架，学习几何感知的高斯表示以保持多视图间的解剖一致性；引入SPWM结构引导损失重加权策略提升边界保真度和局部细节。

Result: 在CTSpine3D数据集和新构建的3D胎儿超声数据集FeSpine3D上，所有评估指标显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UniSpine-GS提供了一种实用且成本高效的统一多视角医学成像解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: The diagnosis of spinal diseases is often assisted by 3D imaging techniques in clinical practice. However, precise 3D spinal assessment is limited by the high costs of 3D imaging hardware and the challenges posed by the physical differences between imaging modalities, which hinder the generalizability of models. To address these issues, we propose UniSpine-GS, an efficient, physics-aware Gaussian framework designed for novel-view projection rendering in multi-view spine imaging via a 3D-aware representation. Instead of performing explicit 3D reconstruction, our approach learns a geometry-aware Gaussian representation that ensures anatomical consistency across different views. We introduce SPWM, a structure-guided loss reweighting strategy to improve boundary fidelity and local details. We evaluate our method on the CTSpine3D dataset and a newly constructed 3D fetal ultrasound dataset, FeSpine3D. Our results demonstrate that UniSpine-GS significantly outperforms existing methods across all metrics, offering a practical and cost-effective solution for unified multi-view medical imaging. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/orangeisland66/UniSpine-GS.

</details>


### [277] [MemPose: Category-level Object Pose Estimation with Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04930)
*Xiao Lin,Minghao Zhu,Yun Peng,Liuyi Wang,Qiyi Wang,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemPose提出了一种基于记忆增强的类别级物体姿态估计框架，通过外部记忆缓冲区分存储和动态更新先前实例的结构表征，在四个基准数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别级姿态估计方法大多采用参数化形式，将类别级模式编码为固定的形状先验或静态参数权重，限制了其对高度多样化实例的可扩展性。

Method: 提出MemPose框架，引入外部记忆缓冲区，存储并动态更新来自先前观测实例的结构表征，使模型能够利用累积的经验辅助当前感知任务。

Result: 在REAL275、CAMERA25、Housecat6D和Wild6D四个具有挑战性的基准数据集上，MemPose超越了之前的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 从记忆中心的角度重新思考类别级姿态估计问题，证明了显式引入类别级几何记忆可以有效提升模型在多样化实例上的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: In the pursuit of robust and generalizable category-level object pose estimation, most existing methods adopt parametric formulations that learn effective representations from data, yet they primarily encode category-level patterns into fixed shape priors or static parameter weights, which limits their scalability to highly diverse instances. In this paper, we rethink category-level pose estimation from a memory-centric perspective and present MemPose, a memory-augmented framework that explicitly incorporates category-level geometric memory into the pose estimation pipeline. We introduce an external memory buffer that stores and dynamically updates structural representations from previously observed instances, enabling the model to leverage accumulated experience to support current perception. Extensive experiments on four challenging benchmarks (REAL275, CAMERA25, Housecat6D and Wild6D) demonstrate the superiority of our proposed method over previous state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [278] [Virtual Category-Guided Continual Generalized Category Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04984)
*Jiahui Xiong,Qiuxia Lai,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出虚拟类别引导的持续广义类别发现方法，通过虚拟类别学习（VCL）处理不确定性样本，并结合扩展邻域对比学习（ENCL）增强特征判别性，在CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet和ImageNet-100上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续广义类别发现（C-GCD）中，模糊的不确定性样本难以被可靠分配为已知类或新类，导致伪标签脆弱且学习偏向已知类别，亟需一种能安全利用未标注流数据的方法。

Method: 1) 将虚拟类别学习（VCL）适配到持续学习场景，识别不确定样本并分配临时虚拟类别，避免注入噪声标签；2) 引入扩展邻域对比学习（ENCL），利用扩展邻域关系和自适应边距学习更具判别性和分离性的表示。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、Tiny ImageNet和ImageNet-100三个数据集上，该方法持续优于现有最先进方法，证明了其可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展且有效的C-GCD解决方案，通过虚拟类别引导和对比学习增强，解决了不确定性样本处理和新旧类别分离的关键问题。

Abstract: Continual Generalized Category Discovery (C-GCD) aims to incrementally identify novel categories from sequential unlabeled data while preserving recognition of known classes, which is an essential capability for open-world visual learning. A major bottleneck lies in ambiguous unlabeled samples that cannot be confidently assigned to known classes nor reliably grouped as novel ones, making pseudo-labeling brittle and often biasing learning toward familiar categories. In this work, we introduce Virtual Category-Guided Continual Generalized Category Discovery by adapting Virtual Category Learning (VCL) to the continual setting. Our method identifies uncertain samples and assigns them to temporary virtual categories, enabling safe and informative learning from unlabeled streams without injecting noisy labels, while improving unlabeled data utilization and mitigating prediction bias. To further stabilize discovery across sessions and enhance class separation, we augment VCL with Expanded Neighborhood Contrastive Learning (ENCL), which exploits extended neighborhood relations and an adaptive margin to learn more discriminative and well-separated representations for both old and emerging classes. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-100, Tiny ImageNet, and ImageNet-100 demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, establishing a scalable and effective solution for C-GCD.

</details>


### [279] [Geometry-aware Depth-guided Representation Learning for Structure-preserving Low-light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05005)
*Fang Gao,Jiongkai Qin,Jiabao Wang,Jingfeng Tang,Ming Cheng,Hanbo Zheng,Qingbao Huang,Cheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度引导的多尺度注意力网络（DMSA-Net），通过引入深度结构先验实现几何感知的低光图像增强，在恢复外观的同时保持结构一致性，并构建了带有深度标注的低光数据集LOL-D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光图像增强方法主要关注外观恢复，未能充分利用场景几何信息来保持结构一致性，导致在弱光条件下结构线索被削弱的问题未能有效解决。

Method: 提出DMSA-Net，首先使用基于Retinex理论的分解模块获取光照不变的反射率表示，从中推断深度线索表征场景结构；然后采用多尺度深度引导融合策略嵌入分层编码器-解码器架构，通过深度感知注意力机制自适应融合几何特征与外观特征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上实验表明，DMSA-Net在实现有效低光恢复的同时改善了结构保持效果。此外，构建了LOL-D深度增强低光数据集。

Conclusion: DMSA-Net通过将深度结构先验引入低光增强，有效解决了现有方法在结构一致性保持方面的不足，为几何感知的低光视觉任务提供了新思路和数据基础。

Abstract: Low-light degradation reduces image visibility and weakens structural cues that are important for visual representation and scene understanding. Existing low-light image enhancement methods mainly focus on appearance restoration, while insufficiently exploiting scene geometry to preserve structural consistency. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a Depth-guided Multi-scale Attention Network (DMSA-Net) for geometry-aware low-light image enhancement. DMSA-Net introduces depth-related structural priors into low-light representation learning through reflectance-geometry interaction. A Retinex-based decomposition module is first used to obtain illumination-invariant reflectance representations, from which depth cues are inferred to characterize scene structure under degraded illumination. A multi-scale depth-guided fusion strategy is then embedded into a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture, where depth-aware attention adaptively integrates geometric and appearance features. Experiments on several benchmark datasets show that DMSA-Net achieves effective low-light restoration while improving structural preservation. Moreover, we construct LOL-D, a depth-augmented low-light dataset, to facilitate research on geometry-aware low-light vision.

</details>


### [280] [Unsupervised Pixel-Level Semantic Left-Right Understanding of In-the-Wild Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05006)
*Weikang Wang,Tobias Weißberg,Florian Bernard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督学习框架，结合3D形状数据和自然图像，实现单视图图像中像素级语义左右预测，即使对未见过的3D物体类别（如汽车、火车）也能取得高质量结果，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽涉及3D数据和图像中的反射对称性理解，但野外图像中像素级语义左右预测仍具挑战，原因包括缺乏3D信息、遮挡、物体姿态变化和局部性等。

Method: 提出无监督学习框架，利用3D数据中顶点级语义左右理解的最新进展，联合使用3D形状数据集和图像数据集，推断单视图图像中像素级语义左右预测。

Result: 使用主要包含人类和四足动物形状的中等规模3D数据集，结合多样化自然图像数据，即可在图像中实现高质量语义左右预测，甚至对未见过的3D类别（如汽车、火车）也有效。在渲染图像和自然图像数据集上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该无监督方法能有效利用3D形状数据与图像数据的协同作用，实现跨类别的像素级语义左右预测，展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: While various works address reflective symmetry understanding in 3D data and images, pixel-level semantic left-right prediction of in-the-wild images remains challenging, due to certain difficulties including the lack of 3D information, occlusion, object pose variation, partiality, etc. In this work, we propose an unsupervised learning framework to tackle this challenge. Leveraging recent advances in vertex-wise semantic left-right understanding of 3D data, our unsupervised learning method jointly utilises 3D shape and image datasets to infer pixel-wise semantic left-right predictions in single-view images. In particular, we show that a medium-scale 3D shape dataset comprising mainly of human- and quadruped animal-like shapes, combined with diverse in-the-wild image data, are sufficient to achieve high-quality semantic left-right prediction in images, even for entirely unseen 3D object categories, such as cars or trains. Overall, our approach achieves superior performance in dense pixel-wise semantic left-right predictions on both rendered and in-the-wild image datasets when compared to existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [281] [Comparison of Loss Functions for Robust Deep Learning-based Echocardiography Segmentation when Learning with Partially Labelled Data from Multiple Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05008)
*Iman Islam,Esther Puyol-Antón,Bram Ruijsink,Andrew J. Reader,Andrew P. King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种损失函数（aCCE、边际损失、aBCE）在超声心动图部分标注数据分割任务中的表现，发现边际损失在多标签缺失和跨域场景中最稳健，aBCE在单标签缺失的跨域任务中表现优异，而三种损失在域内任务中均表现良好。这是首次系统比较不同损失函数处理多域部分标注超声心动图数据的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图是评估心脏功能的首选影像学手段，准确分割心脏结构对获取生物标志物至关重要。然而，来自不同数据源的部分标注数据使得训练有效的多结构自动分割模型面临挑战，现有研究缺乏对处理此类问题的损失函数的系统比较。

Method: 本研究系统比较了三种损失函数（自适应分类交叉熵aCCE损失、边际损失、自适应二元交叉熵aBCE损失）在处理部分标注数据时的表现。实验覆盖多种场景：域内与跨域任务、单标签与多标签缺失、不同比例的全标注与部分标注数据，并采用多种网络架构进行验证。

Result: 三种损失函数在域内分割任务中均表现优异。在跨域任务中，当仅缺失一个标签时，aBCE和边际损失表现更优；当缺失多个标签时，边际损失显著优于其他方法，展现出最强的鲁棒性。不同损失函数在不同标注场景下各具优势。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统探讨了超声心动图分割中处理多域部分标注数据的技术，提供了基于损失函数解决方案的全面比较。应根据具体标注场景选择合适的损失函数以优化模型性能。

Abstract: Echocardiography is the first imaging modality used for assessing cardiac function, and accurate segmentation of cardiac structures is essential for deriving biomarkers. However, the development of effective automated segmentation models for multiple cardiac structures is challenged by the difficulty of training on datasets from different sources that are often partially-labelled. This study aims to address this challenge by evaluating the performance of three loss functions - adaptive categorical cross entropy (aCCE) loss, marginal loss, and the adaptive binary cross entropy (aBCE) loss - in handling partially-labelled data. We conduct a comprehensive comparison of these loss functions across multiple scenarios and network architectures: intra-domain and inter-domain tasks, with both single and multiple partial-labels, and varying proportions of fully-labelled to partially-labelled data.
  Our experiments reveal that all three loss functions exhibit strong performance in intra-domain segmentation tasks, effectively handling label variations within the same domain. For inter-domain tasks, where models are trained on datasets with a domain shift, the aBCE and marginal losses show superior performance when dealing with the case of one label being missing from some training examples. In scenarios involving more than one label being missing, marginal loss outperforms the other methods, demonstrating its robustness in such complex conditions. These results highlight the strengths of each loss function depending on the labelling scenario, emphasizing the importance of selecting the appropriate loss function to optimize model performance. This study represents the first investigation of techniques for handling partially-labelled data from multiple different domains in echocardiography segmentation and provides a comprehensive comparison of loss-based solutions.

</details>


### [282] [RUFNet: Query-Guided Support Mask Refinement and Uncertainty Fusion based on Hybrid Mamba for Few-Shot Brain Tumor Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05035)
*Dongyi He,Xiangkai Wang,Binbing Xu,Bin Jiang,Hongjie Yan,Weixiang Liu,Wai Ting Siok,Nizhuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RUFNet是一个基于混合Mamba架构的少样本脑肿瘤分割框架，通过支持掩码细化、不确定性感知后验融合和线性复杂度的交互骨干网络，在BraTS 2020数据集上实现了84.3%（1-way 1-shot）和86.1%（1-way 5-shot）的Dice系数，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本脑肿瘤分割面临支持掩码噪声大、支持与查询图像间存在患者差异、以及缺乏像素级置信度估计等挑战，现有方法难以有效应对这些问题。

Method: 提出RUFNet框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 线性复杂度的混合Mamba交互骨干网络用于保持支持-查询依赖关系；2) 注意力引导掩码细化模块(AGMR)利用查询特征重新校准支持掩码以增强原型一致性；3) 不确定性感知后验融合模块(UAPF)估计像素级方差并自适应平衡少样本预测与查询对齐先验。

Result: 在BraTS 2020数据集上，1-way 1-shot设置下Dice系数达84.3%，1-way 5-shot设置下达86.1%，均优于对比的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 混合Mamba交互、掩码细化和不确定性建模能够提升少样本医学图像分割的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Few-shot brain tumor segmentation remains challenging due to noisy support masks, inter-patient variations between support and query images, and the lack of pixel-wise confidence estimation. This study proposes RUFNet, a Hybrid Mamba-based few-shot framework that combines support mask refinement with uncertainty-aware posterior fusion. To preserve support-query dependencies with manageable cost, RUFNet adopts a Hybrid Mamba interaction backbone with linear complexity. To reduce support-mask noise, an Attention-Guided Mask Refinement module (AGMR) uses query features to recalibrate support masks and improve prototype consistency. To handle ambiguous predictions, an Uncertainty-Aware Posterior Fusion module (UAPF) estimates pixel-wise variance and adaptively balances few-shot predictions with query-aligned priors. On the Brain Tumor Segmentation Challenge (BraTS) 2020 dataset, RUFNet achieves Dice coefficients of 84.3% and 86.1% in the 1-way 1-shot and 1-way 5-shot settings, respectively, outperforming the compared state-of-the-art methods. These results suggest that Hybrid Mamba interaction, mask refinement and uncertainty modelling can improve the robustness of few-shot medical image segmentation. The official implementation code is available at https://github.com/hdy6438/RUFNet.

</details>


### [283] [Consistent and Editable: A Balanced Framework for Text-Guided Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05056)
*Tao Jin,Li Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EquiEdit提出了一种基于时序Mamba模块和频谱变换噪声注入策略的视频编辑框架，协同提升编辑视频的时间一致性和可编辑性，并实现两者之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导视频编辑方法难以平衡时间一致性与可编辑性，两者通常呈负相关关系。

Method: 1) 时序Mamba模块：采用四方向时序感知扫描策略增强帧间一致性；2) 基于频谱变换的噪声注入策略：利用傅里叶变换保留初始潜空间噪声中的隐藏结构，提升编辑灵活性并确保帧间一致性与保真度。

Result: 定性与定量实验均证明了EquiEdit在时间一致性、可编辑性以及对输入视频保真度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: EquiEdit通过协同优化一致性与可编辑性，有效解决了两者之间的权衡问题，实现了高质量的视频编辑。

Abstract: Recently, diffusion models have achieved considerable success in the text-guided video editing domain. However, existing works often struggle to balance the trade-off between temporal consistency and editability in video editing, with consistency and editability typically being inversely related. To address this, we propose a high-quality video editing framework enhanced for consistency and editability, named EquiEdit, which improves coordinatively the temporal consistency and editability of the edited videos while achieving a balance between the two. In terms of temporal consistency, the proposed temporal Mamba module with a tailored temporal-aware scanning scans fused video sequences following four designed directions, effectively enhancing the inter-frame consistency of edited videos. For editability, we design a noise injection strategy based on the spectral transformation to increase editing flexibility, where the Fourier transform is used to preserve the hidden structure in the initial latent noise used for editing, ensuring inter-frame consistency of the edited video and fidelity to the input video. Extensive qualitative and quantitative experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in terms of temporal consistency and editability, as well as its great fidelity to the input video itself.

</details>


### [284] [LangLoc: "Tell Me What You See"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05077)
*Shaurya Kishore Panwar,Roham Zendehdel Nobari,Shirley Feng Yi Lau,Abu Bakr Rahman Shaik,Manuel Günther,Marc Pollefeys,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LangLoc，一个三阶段流水线，实现从自然语言描述进行细粒度室内定位（2D位置和朝向），无需摄像头，通过场景检索、密集网格评分和贝叶斯对话模块，最终中位误差0.95米，并贡献了13000+条自然语言描述基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作仅能实现粗粒度的场景检索，无法在场景内精确定位。自然语言查询具有轻量、保护隐私、无需摄像头等优势，但缺乏细粒度定位能力。作者旨在填补这一空白，实现从自由形式语言描述中估计观察者在已知3D环境中的精确位置和朝向。

Method: LangLoc采用三阶段流水线：(i) 场景检索：使用双分支GATv2编码器结合CLIP语义特征，Top-1召回率提升8个百分点；(ii) 位置与朝向估计：通过射线投射物体可见性对密集地板网格进行评分，中位误差0.95米；(iii) 贝叶斯对话模块：通过提问是/否问题并更新位姿后验分布，消除剩余歧义。

Result: 场景检索阶段Top-1召回率超越此前最优方法8个百分点；位置和朝向估计中位误差0.95米；构建了包含1300+室内3D扫描、13000+条位姿索引的自然语言描述基准数据集。

Conclusion: LangLoc首次实现了从自然语言描述到室内细粒度定位的完整解决方案，验证了无需视觉传感器的语言定位可行性，并为后续研究提供了标准化基准。

Abstract: We tackle fine-grained indoor localization from natural language: given a free-form description of one's surroundings, estimate the observer's 2D position and heading within a known 3D environment. Language queries are lightweight, privacy-preserving, and need no camera - yet prior work stops at coarse scene retrieval and cannot resolve an intra-scene pose. We close this gap with LangLoc, a three-stage pipeline that (i) retrieves the correct scene via a dual-branch GATv2 encoder with CLIP semantic features, surpassing the previous best by 8 percentage points in Top-1 recall; (ii) estimates position and heading by scoring a dense floor grid through ray-cast object visibility, reaching a median error of 0.95 m; and (iii) resolves residual ambiguity through a Bayesian dialog module that asks targeted yes/no questions and updates a pose posterior until the location is pinpointed. To support this task we contribute a benchmark of $13{,}000{+}$ pose-indexed natural-language descriptions over $1{,}300{+}$ indoor 3D scans.

</details>


### [285] [RADIANCE: Relative Adaptive Denoising with IP-Adapter for Novel Concept Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05088)
*Zi-Xiang Ni,Bo-Lun Huang,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Bo-Kai Ruan,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RADIANCE是一个无需额外训练的框架，通过闭环反馈过程增强预训练文本到图像扩散模型的组合能力，在稀有概念合成和多对象提示生成上显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型在合成包含罕见属性-对象组合的稀有概念时存在概念遗漏或语义漂移问题，根源在于去噪轨迹中缺乏组合平衡。

Method: 提出RADIANCE框架，包含三个模块：(1) 组合相似度监控器(CSM)通过CLIP反馈追踪中间隐层中对象和属性的出现；(2) 双向尺度控制器(BSC)利用正负IP-Adapter尺度施加"恢复力"重新平衡偏置轨迹；(3) 反馈引导调度器(FGS)跨时间步协调更新。针对多对象提示扩展了延迟适配器激活(DAA)和逐层交替引导(LAG)，并通过流水线执行重叠监控与去噪过程以保持低延迟。

Result: 在RareBench和T2I-CompBench上的实验表明，RADIANCE在组合对齐和感知质量上持续超越最先进基线方法，显著提升了单样本成功率和有效吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过将推理过程建模为闭环反馈系统，无需额外训练即可显著改善扩散模型在复杂组合场景下的生成质量，有效解决了罕见概念合成中的组合失衡问题。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models have achieved striking progress but still struggle to synthesize rare concepts involving unusual attribute-object pairings, often resulting in concept omission or semantic drift where a dominant entity overwhelms the generation. Tracing these failures to a lack of compositional balance during the denoising trajectory, we propose RADIANCE, a training-free framework that treats inference as a closed-loop feedback process. RADIANCE augments pretrained backbones with three modular components: (1) a Compositional Similarity Monitor (CSM) that tracks the emergence of objects and attributes in intermediate latents via CLIP-based feedback; (2) a Bidirectional Scale Controller (BSC) that applies a reactive "restoring force" using positive and negative IP-Adapter scales to rebalance biased trajectories; and (3) a Feedback Guidance Scheduler (FGS) that coordinates these updates across timesteps without additional training. We further extend the framework to multi-object prompts via Delayed Adapter Activation (DAA) and Layer-wise Alternating Guidance (LAG) to prevent premature concept fusion. By overlapping monitoring and denoising through pipelined execution, RADIANCE maintains competitive latency while significantly enhancing the per-sample success rate and effective throughput. Experiments on RareBench and T2I-CompBench demonstrate that RADIANCE consistently enhances compositional alignment and perceptual quality over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [286] [TimeThink: Reasoning with Time for Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05089)
*Handong Li,Longteng Guo,Zikang Liu,Dongze Hao,Yepeng Tang,Zijia Zhao,Jie Jiang,Zhiwei Jin,Chen Chen,Haonan Lu,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeThink是一个面向视频大语言模型的强化学习框架，通过将时间线索步骤作为视频推理的基本优化单元，引入步骤级时间过程奖励和联合过程-结果优化目标，显式引导模型发现视频中的时间证据，在视频推理、时间定位和通用视频理解基准上取得开源视频RL模型中的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在强化学习对齐中通常仅使用基于结果的奖励（监督最终预测），缺乏对模型在中间推理过程中如何发现相关时间证据的引导，导致时间证据发现能力不足。

Method: 提出TimeThink框架：1) 将时间线索步骤作为视频推理的基本优化单元，每个推理步骤引用视频中的一个候选时间区间；2) 引入步骤级时间过程奖励（step-wise temporal process reward），为时间线索提供细粒度信用分配；3) 设计联合过程-结果优化目标（joint process-outcome optimization），平衡推理保真度与任务正确性；4) 构建TimeThink-RFT-20K数据集，包含自动推导的时间证据片段。

Result: 在视频推理、时间定位和通用视频理解基准上，TimeThink一致性地提升了时间定位和推理性能，在开源视频RL模型中取得了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: 通过显式引导模型在推理过程中发现时间证据，TimeThink有效弥补了结果奖励监督的不足，证明了步骤级过程奖励在视频推理强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Video reasoning requires models to identify and verify temporally localized evidence within long video sequences. Recent Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have shown promising reasoning abilities when aligned with reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches typically rely on outcome-based rewards that supervise only the final prediction. Such supervision provides limited guidance on how models should discover the relevant temporal evidence during intermediate reasoning. In this work, we propose TimeThink, a reinforcement learning framework that explicitly guides temporal evidence discovery in Video-LLMs. Our key idea is to treat temporal clue steps as the fundamental optimization primitive of video reasoning, where each reasoning step references a candidate time interval in the video. We introduce a step-wise temporal process reward that provides localized credit assignment for these clues and a joint process--outcome optimization objective that balances reasoning fidelity with task correctness. To enable scalable training, we construct TimeThink-RFT-20K, a dataset with automatically derived temporal evidence segments. Extensive experiments across video reasoning, temporal grounding, and general video understanding benchmarks show that TimeThink consistently improves both temporal localization and reasoning performance, achieving state-of-the-art results among open-source video RL models.

</details>


### [287] [Be Indiscrete: The Benefits of Learning Continuous Spine Degeneration Severity Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05090)
*Maria Monzon,Andrew Zisserman,Robin Y. Park,Catherine R. Jutzeler,Amir Jamaludin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpineRankNet框架，将腰椎间盘退变分级从传统多类分类问题转化为连续严重程度排序问题，利用排序损失学习连续评分，在Genodisc数据集上多个退变指标上表现优于分类和序数回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腰椎MRI退变分级采用序数分级（如正常、轻、中、重）作为多类分类问题处理，忽略了序数等级间的误分类严重程度差异，且在连续疾病过程上施加了硬决策边界，无法反映疾病的连续特性。

Method: 提出SpineRankNet框架，使用排序损失（ranking loss）从腰椎MRI中学习连续的严重程度评分（scalar severity score），并与多类分类和序数回归方法进行对比。

Result: 在Genodisc数据集的多个退变指标上，排序损失训练的连续评分模型实现了细粒度的MRI扫描排序；从连续评分中恢复的序数分级准确率与直接分类训练模型相当；连续评分进一步提升了远距离类别间的判别能力。

Conclusion: 将腰椎退变建模为连续严重程度排序问题优于传统分类方法，既能保留序数分级的可解释性，又能提供更细粒度的连续评估能力。

Abstract: Lumbar spine degeneration is a major contributor to chronic low back pain and is routinely assessed on MRI using ordinal grading systems, e.g. normal, mild, moderate, severe. Consequently, most approaches to train models to grade these MRIs formulate grading as a multi-class classification problem, treating ordinal grades as categorical, ignoring differences in misclassification severity, and imposing hard decision boundaries on a continuous disease process. This work explores modeling spinal degeneration as a continuous severity ranking problem. We introduce SpineRankNet, a framework that learns scalar severity scores from lumbar spinal MRI, and compare it against multi-class classification and ordinal regression. Using multiple degeneration measures from the Genodisc dataset, we show that a model trained using a ranking loss to produce a continuous score enables fine-grained ordering of MRI scans. Furthermore, the ordinal grading classes can be recovered from the score with comparable accuracy to those from a model trained directly for classification. The score learned by ranking even improves discrimination between more distant classes. Source code is available at https://github.com/spinetools/spineranknet.

</details>


### [288] [Semantic Video Communication via Multi-Scale Convolution and Dynamic Routing for Next-Generation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05093)
*Gengtian Shi,Jinze Yu,Chenhao Wu,Shaofei Wang,Eiji Fukuzawa,Junjie Tang,Hiroshi Onoda,Jiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成式AI驱动的语义视频通信框架，通过多尺度时间卷积编码器和胶囊动态路由机制，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现高效的层次化时间建模与语义对齐，在ActivityNet Captions数据集上达到42.9% Recall@0.5和41.1% mean IoU，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频流量指数级增长要求从传输原始比特转向传输语义信息，现有方法在带宽受限传输的层次化时间建模和边缘设备上视频内容与自然语言查询的鲁棒语义对齐方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出多尺度时间卷积编码器（O(T)复杂度，适合IoT部署）捕获不同时间粒度的运动模式；提出基于胶囊的动态路由机制，迭代细化片段-查询关联，建模非单调语义对齐；通过多任务学习目标统一优化时间边界回归、跨模态对齐和胶囊多样性。

Result: 在ActivityNet Captions数据集上达到42.9% Recall@0.5和41.1% mean IoU，同时保持适用于边缘部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的生成式AI驱动框架有效解决了带宽受限传输中语义视频通信的效率与对齐问题，为资源受限的边缘设备上的目标导向通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The exponential growth of video traffic demands novel semantic communication paradigms that transmit meaning rather than raw bits. We present a generative AI-enabled framework for semantic video communication addressing two critical challenges: efficient hierarchical temporal modeling for bandwidth-constrained transmission and robust semantic alignment between video content and natural language queries at network edge devices. Our approach introduces a multi-scale temporal convolutional encoder that captures motion patterns across different temporal granularities with O(T) complexity suitable for resource-constrained IoT deployments. We further propose a capsule-based dynamic routing mechanism that iteratively refines segment-query associations, enabling flexible modeling of non-monotonic semantic alignments essential for goal-oriented communication. These components are unified through a multi-task learning objective optimizing temporal boundary regression, cross-modal alignment, and capsule diversity. Experiments on ActivityNet Captions demonstrate significant improvements, achieving 42.9% Recall@0.5 and 41.1% mean IoU while maintaining computational efficiency critical for edge deployment.

</details>


### [289] [Green for Go, Red for No: Visual Grounding via Semantic Segmentation for VLA Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05122)
*Adrian Szvoren,Dimitrios Kanoulas,Nilufer Tuptuk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次实证评估了视觉基础对VLA导航策略的影响，提出基于SegFormer的实时分割方法，可将最远航点误差降低27-44%，但主要作为轨迹长度正则化器，无法补偿分布外指令的缺失训练信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人导航中易受感知干扰和场景歧义影响，现有研究缺乏对视觉基础能力的系统性评估。

Method: 提出实时分割基础方法，使用SegFormer将可通行区域标记为绿色、不可通行区域标记为红色，在OmniVLA和Grand Tour数据集上评估观察-only分割和联合观察-目标增强两种变体。

Result: 视觉基础在最远航点将平均航点误差降低27-44%（取决于指令长度）；长指令受益更大，图像目标改进有限；将预测路径长度缩短30%，但未提升单位距离推理能力。

Conclusion: 视觉基础提供了一种简单、计算成本低的方法来改进VLA导航，无需模型重新训练，但不能补偿分布外指令中的缺失训练信号。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable robot navigation from natural language and visual goals, but remain susceptible to perceptual distractions and ambiguous scene interpretations. This paper presents the first empirical evaluation of visual grounding for VLA navigation policies. We propose a real-time segmentation-based grounding method that highlights traversable areas in green and non-traversable areas in red using SegFormer. Two variants are evaluated: observation-only segmentation and joint observation-goal augmentation. Using OmniVLA on the Grand Tour dataset, we show that visual grounding reduces the mean waypoint error by 27-44% at the farthest waypoint, depending on the instruction length. The benefits are greater for long instructions than for short instructions, and grounding provides little improvement for image goals. Normalized error analysis indicates that grounding primarily acts as a trajectory length regularizer, reducing the predicted path length by 30% without improving per-unit-distance reasoning. Our results indicate that visual grounding offers a simple, computationally inexpensive method to improve VLA navigation without model retraining, although it cannot compensate for missing training signals in out-of-distribution instructions.

</details>


### [290] [UNIVERSE: Unified Video Action Models for Autonomous Driving with Flexible Mask-Modulated Modality Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05133)
*Mengmeng Liu,Diankun Zhang,Jiuming Liu,Jianfeng Cui,Hongwei Xie,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Francesco Nex,Hao Cheng,Michael Ying Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UNIVERSE提出了一种统一视频-动作模型，基于单掩码调制扩散Transformer，通过联合训练未来视频潜变量和自车轨迹令牌，实现密集视频监督直接塑造轨迹去噪，并引入模态解耦可见性掩码保证因果有效性，在NAVSIM上达到91.0 PDMS，支持零样本迁移且推理速度提升4.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联或双DiT架构将视频想象与动作预测分离，导致视频学习的世界动力学知识难以有效传递到轨迹分支，动作模型仍可能过拟合数据集特定驾驶先验。

Method: 提出UNIVERSE统一视频-动作模型，核心创新包括：(1) 单掩码调制扩散Transformer，共享生成参数联合训练未来视频潜变量和自车轨迹令牌；(2) 模态解耦可见性掩码，历史上下文跨模态共享但阻止未来视频与轨迹令牌间的相互注意力，防止未来目标泄漏并支持测试时仅轨迹推理。

Result: NAVSIM上达到91.0 PDMS（优于双DiT变体的89.6），零样本迁移到nuScenes和Bench2Drive无需微调，推理速度提升4.3倍，消融实验验证了单DiT统一、视频联合训练和掩码解耦的重要性。

Conclusion: 单DiT统一架构结合模态解耦掩码能有效将视频建模优势转移到轨迹生成，同时保证因果正确性和推理效率，为自动驾驶世界动作模型提供了更优设计范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have shown strong potential for improving action generalization in autonomous driving by using future video prediction as dense supervision for scene dynamics and temporal causality. However, it remains unclear which architecture better transfers video-modeling benefits to trajectory generation. Existing cascaded or dual-DiT designs separate video imagination from action prediction, weakening the transfer of video-learned world dynamics to the trajectory branch: the action model may still overfit dataset-specific driving priors, while the video model only indirectly regularizes planning. We propose UNIVERSE, a unified video-action model built upon a single mask-modulated Diffusion Transformer. By co-training future video latents and ego-trajectory tokens within shared generative parameters, UNIVERSE allows dense video supervision to directly shape trajectory denoising, leading to stronger cross-domain action generalization. To ensure causal validity and efficient deployment, we introduce a Modality-Decoupling Visibility Mask, which shares historical context across modalities while blocking mutual attention between future video and trajectory tokens. This prevents future-target leakage and enables trajectory-only inference by removing future-video denoising at test time, achieving a $4.3\times$ speedup over joint video-action rollout while maintaining comparable planning accuracy. The same model also supports video-only and joint video-action rollouts. Experiments show that UNIVERSE achieves 91.0 PDMS on NAVSIM (vs. 89.6 for the Two-DiT variant), and demonstrates strong zero-shot transfer to nuScenes and Bench2Drive without fine-tuning, while ablations confirm the importance of single-DiT unification, video co-training, and mask-based modality decoupling.

</details>


### [291] [Fully Rotation-Equivariant Spectral-Spatial Learning for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05148)
*Peng Zhang,Tingfa Xu,Shuaihao Han,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FressDet是一个全旋转等变的谱-空间多光谱目标检测框架，通过谱隐式扭曲、旋转等变一致性加权和朝向感知头三个互补组件，在三个公开基准上以减少93%参数取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多光谱检测器受限于离散谱处理、金字塔层级间谱线索与空间线索可靠性随尺度变化而偏移、以及缺乏针对任意朝向目标的显式旋转等变几何先验。

Method: 提出FressDet框架，包含三个组件：1) 谱隐式扭曲(SpeIW)通过坐标条件隐式场实现查询式谱重采样，生成单调保序扭曲；2) 旋转等变一致性加权(ReCoW)基于分支可靠性自适应融合谱分支和空间分支，在金字塔各层级增强信息抑制噪声；3) 朝向感知头利用群索引特征无需参数复制即可稳定预测朝向目标。

Result: 在三个公开基准上取得最优性能，参数量减少93%。

Conclusion: FressDet能够学习更具判别性和鲁棒性的谱-空间表示，即使存在旋转扰动也能保持性能，证明了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Existing multispectral detectors are limited by discrete spectral processing, a scale-dependent shift in the relative reliability of spectral and spatial cues across pyramid levels, and the lack of explicit rotation-equivariant geometric priors for arbitrarily oriented objects. To tackle these limitations, we propose FressDet, a fully rotation-equivariant spectral-spatial learning framework for multispectral object detection, capable of capturing the continuous, ordered nature of spectral structure and enabling reliable spectral-spatial fusion across pyramid levels under arbitrary in-plane rotations. FressDet integrates three complementary components. Spectral Implicit Warp (SpeIW) enables query-based spectral resampling via a coordinate-conditioned implicit field, yielding a monotone, order-preserving warp. Rotation-Equivariant Consistency Weighting (ReCoW) adaptively fuses spectral and spatial branches based on branch reliability, reinforcing informative cues while suppressing noise across pyramid levels. The oriented-aware head exploits group-indexed features to stably predict oriented objects without parameter replication. Taken together, FressDet learns more discriminative and robust spectral-spatial representations even under rotational perturbations. By achieving state-of-the-art performance with 93% fewer parameters on three public benchmarks, FressDet demonstrates its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [292] [Claim-Level Rubric Rewards for Video Caption Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05150)
*Mingqi Gao,Hongyuan Dong,Yifei Chen,Zhisheng Zhong,Zheng Ruan,Wenjin Hou,Yu Chen,Han Hu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV
